## DicSo92/SandboxScience

https://github.com/DicSo92/SandboxScience

Pinned revision: `ba43fde7ed3fc2224302c2682a3ecd3030717ba5`.

License: AGPL-3.0-or-later.

========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: crates/nova-automata/Cargo.toml
========================================================================================
[package]
name = "nova-automata"
version.workspace = true
edition.workspace = true
rust-version.workspace = true
license = "AGPL-3.0-or-later"
[dependencies]
nova-core = { path = "../nova-core" }
anyhow.workspace = true
rayon.workspace = true
serde.workspace = true
serde_json.workspace = true
sha2.workspace = true


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: crates/nova-automata/src/analysis.rs
========================================================================================
//! Independent rule-space exploration. No CatConway source is copied.
//! Behavior classes are numerical heuristics, not formal complexity classifications.
use crate::{Boundary, Grid, Rules};
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use serde::Serialize;
use std::collections::VecDeque;
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]
pub struct Metrics {
    pub mean_density: f64,
    pub final_density: f64,
    pub density_range: f64,
    pub coefficient_of_variation: f64,
    pub activity: f64,
    pub shannon_entropy: f64,
    pub spatial_block_entropy: f64,
    pub trend: f64,
    pub lag1_autocorrelation: f64,
    pub monotonic_fraction: f64,
    pub roughness: f64,
    pub population_period: Option<usize>,
    pub exact_state_period: Option<usize>,
    pub langton_lambda: f64,
    pub behavior: &'static str,
}
pub struct Observer {
    densities: Vec<f64>,
    activities: Vec<f64>,
    history: VecDeque<Vec<u8>>,
    limit: usize,
    period: Option<usize>,
    blocks: f64,
}
impl Observer {
    pub fn new(cells: usize) -> Result<Self> {
        ensure!(cells > 0 && cells <= 16_777_216, "invalid observation size");
        Ok(Self {
            densities: vec![],
            activities: vec![],
            history: VecDeque::new(),
            limit: (32 * 1024 * 1024 / cells).clamp(1, 128),
            period: None,
            blocks: 0.,
        })
    }
    pub fn record(&mut self, g: &Grid, changed: u64) -> Result<()> {
        ensure!(
            self.densities.len() < 1_000_000,
            "observation budget exceeded"
        );
        self.densities
            .push(g.population() as f64 / g.cells.len() as f64);
        self.activities.push(changed as f64 / g.cells.len() as f64);
        self.period = self
            .history
            .iter()
            .rev()
            .position(|old| old == &g.cells)
            .map(|p| p + 1);
        self.history.push_back(g.cells.clone());
        while self.history.len() > self.limit {
            self.history.pop_front();
        }
        let mut counts = [0u64; 256];
        let zspan = if g.rules.dimensions == 3 { 2 } else { 1 };
        let zlast = if g.rules.dimensions == 3 {
            g.shape[2] - 1
        } else {
            1
        };
        for z in 0..zlast {
            for y in 0..g.shape[1] - 1 {
                for x in 0..g.shape[0] - 1 {
                    let mut pattern = 0;
                    for dz in 0..zspan {
                        for dy in 0..2 {
                            for dx in 0..2 {
                                let bit = dx + 2 * dy + 4 * dz;
                                pattern |= (g.get([
                                    (x + dx) as isize,
                                    (y + dy) as isize,
                                    (z + dz) as isize,
                                ]) as usize)
                                    << bit;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    counts[pattern] += 1;
                }
            }
        }
        let n = counts.iter().sum::<u64>() as f64;
        self.blocks = counts
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|&c| c > 0)
            .map(|c| {
                let p = c as f64 / n;
                -p * p.log2()
            })
            .sum();
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn finish(&self, rules: Rules) -> Result<Metrics> {
        rules.validate()?;
        ensure!(!self.densities.is_empty(), "no observations");
        let d = &self.densities;
        let n = d.len() as f64;
        let mean = d.iter().sum::<f64>() / n;
        let variance = d.iter().map(|x| (x - mean).powi(2)).sum::<f64>() / n;
        let cv = variance.sqrt() / mean.max(1e-12);
        let final_density = *d.last().unwrap();
        let range = d.iter().copied().fold(f64::NEG_INFINITY, f64::max)
            - d.iter().copied().fold(f64::INFINITY, f64::min);
        let activity = self.activities.iter().sum::<f64>() / n;
        let entropy = if mean > 0. && mean < 1. {
            -mean * mean.log2() - (1. - mean) * (1. - mean).log2()
        } else {
            0.
        };
        let trend = (final_density - d[0]) / n.max(2.);
        let autocorrelation = if variance > 1e-15 && d.len() > 1 {
            d.windows(2)
                .map(|x| (x[0] - mean) * (x[1] - mean))
                .sum::<f64>()
                / ((n - 1.) * variance)
        } else {
            0.
        };
        let monotonic = if d.len() > 1 {
            d.windows(2).filter(|x| x[1] >= x[0]).count() as f64 / (n - 1.)
        } else {
            0.
        };
        let roughness = if d.len() > 2 {
            d.windows(3)
                .map(|x| (x[2] - 2. * x[1] + x[0]).abs())
                .sum::<f64>()
                / (n - 2.)
        } else {
            0.
        };
        let period = (1..=128.min(d.len() / 2)).find(|&lag| {
            d.iter()
                .skip(lag)
                .zip(d)
                .all(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs() < 1e-12)
        });
        let behavior = if final_density == 0. {
            "dead"
        } else if activity == 0. {
            "static"
        } else if self.period.is_some() {
            "periodic"
        } else if final_density > 0.95 {
            "saturated"
        } else if trend > 0.001 {
            "growing"
        } else if trend < -0.001 {
            "declining"
        } else if activity > 0.35 {
            "chaotic-candidate"
        } else {
            "complex-candidate"
        };
        let neighbors = rules.max_neighbors();
        let mut transitions = 0f64;
        for k in 0..=neighbors {
            let mut choose = 1f64;
            for j in 0..k {
                choose *= ((neighbors - j) as f64) / ((j + 1) as f64);
            }
            transitions += choose * ((rules.birth >> k & 1) + (rules.survival >> k & 1)) as f64;
        }
        Ok(Metrics {
            mean_density: mean,
            final_density,
            density_range: range,
            coefficient_of_variation: cv,
            activity,
            shannon_entropy: entropy,
            spatial_block_entropy: self.blocks,
            trend,
            lag1_autocorrelation: autocorrelation,
            monotonic_fraction: monotonic,
            roughness,
            population_period: period,
            exact_state_period: self.period,
            langton_lambda: transitions / 2f64.powi((neighbors + 1) as i32),
            behavior,
        })
    }
}
#[derive(Serialize)]
pub struct Discovery {
    pub index: u64,
    pub rules: Rules,
    pub metrics: Metrics,
}
pub fn search(
    shape: [usize; 3],
    radius: u32,
    start: u64,
    limit: usize,
    ticks: usize,
    seed: u64,
) -> Result<Vec<Discovery>> {
    ensure!(
        limit > 0 && limit <= 4096 && ticks > 0 && ticks <= 4096,
        "bounded search requires 1..4096 rules/ticks"
    );
    let base = Rules {
        birth: 0,
        survival: 0,
        radius,
        dimensions: 2,
    };
    base.validate()?;
    let bits = base.max_neighbors() + 1;
    let maximum = 1u64 << (2 * bits);
    ensure!(
        start < maximum
            && start
                .checked_add(limit as u64)
                .is_some_and(|x| x <= maximum),
        "search range exceeds rule space"
    );
    let cells = shape
        .iter()
        .try_fold(1usize, |a, &b| a.checked_mul(b))
        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("shape overflow"))?;
    ensure!(
        cells <= 262144 && (cells as u128) * (ticks as u128) * (limit as u128) <= 2_000_000_000,
        "search work budget exceeded"
    );
    let mask = (1u64 << bits) - 1;
    let mut output = Vec::with_capacity(limit);
    for index in start..start + limit as u64 {
        let rules = Rules {
            birth: (index & mask) as u32,
            survival: ((index >> bits) & mask) as u32,
            ..base
        };
        let mut grid = Grid::new(shape, rules, Boundary::Periodic)?;
        grid.randomize(seed, 0.2)?;
        let mut observer = Observer::new(cells)?;
        for _ in 0..ticks {
            let stats = grid.step()?;
            observer.record(&grid, stats.changed)?;
        }
        output.push(Discovery {
            index,
            rules,
            metrics: observer.finish(rules)?,
        });
    }
    Ok(output)
}

ARQUIVO: crates/nova-automata/src/lib.rs

//! Native integer cellular automata: 2D Moore radius 1/2, 3D Moore-26.
//! Evolution/topology ports based on licensed references; no browser dependencies.
pub mod analysis;
pub mod patterns;
pub mod rle;
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use nova_core::SplitMix64;
use rayon::prelude::*;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::{
    io::{Read, Write},
    path::Path,
};
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
pub enum Boundary {
    Periodic,
    Dead,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Rules {
    pub birth: u32,
    pub survival: u32,
    pub radius: u32,
    pub dimensions: u32,
}
impl Rules {
    pub fn max_neighbors(self) -> u32 {
        if self.dimensions == 3 {
            26
        } else {
            (2 * self.radius + 1).pow(2) - 1
        }
    }
    pub fn validate(self) -> Result<()> {
        ensure!(
            matches!((self.dimensions, self.radius), (2, 1) | (2, 2) | (3, 1)),
            "supported neighborhoods: 2D radius 1/2, 3D radius 1"
        );
        let bits = (1u32 << (self.max_neighbors() + 1)) - 1;
        ensure!(
            self.birth & !bits == 0 && self.survival & !bits == 0,
            "rule count outside neighborhood"
        );
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn parse(text: &str, dimensions: u32, radius: u32) -> Result<Self> {
        let parts: Vec<_> = text.split('/').collect();
        ensure!(
            parts.len() == 2 || parts.len() == 3,
            "expected B.../S...[/R1|R2]"
        );
        let radius = if parts.len() == 3 {
            parts[2]
                .strip_prefix('R')
                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("invalid radius suffix"))?
                .parse()?
        } else {
            radius
        };
        let mut rule = Self {
            birth: 0,
            survival: 0,
            dimensions,
            radius,
        };
        rule.validate()?;
        let max = rule.max_neighbors();
        fn counts(text: &str, max: u32) -> Result<u32> {
            if text == "*" {
                return Ok((1u32 << (max + 1)) - 1);
            }
            let explicit = text.starts_with('{') && text.ends_with('}');
            let text = if explicit {
                &text[1..text.len() - 1]
            } else {
                text
            };
            let mut values = Vec::new();
            if text.contains(',') || text.contains('-') || explicit {
                for piece in text.split(',').filter(|x| !x.is_empty()) {
                    if let Some((a, b)) = piece.split_once('-') {
                        let a: u32 = a.parse()?;
                        let b: u32 = b.parse()?;
                        ensure!(a <= b && b <= max, "invalid neighbor range");
                        values.extend(a..=b);
                    } else {
                        values.push(piece.parse()?);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                for ch in text.chars() {
                    values.push(
                        ch.to_digit(10)
                            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("invalid neighbor count"))?,
                    );
                }
            }
            ensure!(
                values.iter().all(|&x| x <= max),
                "neighbor count exceeds {max}"
            );
            Ok(values.into_iter().fold(0, |a, n| a | (1 << n)))
        }
        rule.birth = counts(
            parts[0]
                .strip_prefix('B')
                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("missing B prefix"))?,
            max,
        )?;
        rule.survival = counts(
            parts[1]
                .strip_prefix('S')
                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("missing S prefix"))?,
            max,
        )?;
        rule.validate()?;
        Ok(rule)
    }
    pub fn preset(name: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        let (s, r) = match name {
            "conway" => ("B3/S23", 1),
            "highlife" => ("B36/S23", 1),
            "day-night" => ("B3678/S34678", 1),
            "seeds" => ("B2/S", 1),
            "life-without-death" => ("B3/S*", 1),
            "bugs" => ("B3-5/S4-8", 2),
            "globe" => ("B7-11/S7-11", 2),
            "majority" => ("B5-8/S4-10", 2),
            _ => anyhow::bail!("unknown rule preset"),
        };
        Self::parse(s, 2, r)
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Grid {
    pub shape: [usize; 3],
    pub rules: Rules,
    pub boundary: Boundary,
    pub tick: u64,
    pub cells: Vec<u8>,
}
impl Grid {
    pub fn new(shape: [usize; 3], rules: Rules, boundary: Boundary) -> Result<Self> {
        rules.validate()?;
        let minimum = (2 * rules.radius + 1) as usize;
        ensure!(
            shape[0] >= minimum
                && shape[1] >= minimum
                && if rules.dimensions == 2 {
                    shape[2] == 1
                } else {
                    shape[2] >= minimum
                },
            "shape incompatible with neighborhood"
        );
        let size = shape
            .iter()
            .try_fold(1usize, |a, &b| a.checked_mul(b))
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("grid size overflow"))?;
        ensure!(size <= 16_777_216, "native CA cell budget is 2^24");
        Ok(Self {
            shape,
            rules,
            boundary,
            tick: 0,
            cells: vec![0; size],
        })
    }
    pub fn randomize(&mut self, seed: u64, density: f32) -> Result<()> {
        ensure!(
            density.is_finite() && (0.0..=1.).contains(&density),
            "invalid density"
        );
        let mut rng = SplitMix64(seed);
        for cell in &mut self.cells {
            *cell = (rng.unit() < density) as u8;
        }
        self.tick = 0;
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {
        let expected = Self::new(self.shape, self.rules, self.boundary)?;
        ensure!(
            expected.cells.len() == self.cells.len() && self.cells.iter().all(|&x| x <= 1),
            "invalid binary grid"
        );
        Ok(())
    }
    fn offset(&self, mut p: [isize; 3]) -> Option<usize> {
        for (k, x) in p.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            if self.boundary == Boundary::Periodic {
                *x = x.rem_euclid(self.shape[k] as isize);
            } else if *x < 0 || *x >= self.shape[k] as isize {
                return None;
            }
        }
        Some((p[2] as usize * self.shape[1] + p[1] as usize) * self.shape[0] + p[0] as usize)
    }
    pub fn get(&self, p: [isize; 3]) -> bool {
        self.offset(p).is_some_and(|i| self.cells[i] != 0)
    }
    pub fn set(&mut self, p: [isize; 3], alive: bool) {
        if let Some(i) = self.offset(p) {
            self.cells[i] = alive as u8;
        }
    }
    pub fn step(&mut self) -> Result<StepStats> {
        self.rules.validate()?;
        ensure!(
            self.shape[0] >= (2 * self.rules.radius + 1) as usize
                && self.shape[1] >= (2 * self.rules.radius + 1) as usize
                && if self.rules.dimensions == 2 {
                    self.shape[2] == 1
                } else {
                    self.shape[2] >= 3
                },
            "invalid automaton shape"
        );
        ensure!(
            self.shape.iter().try_fold(1usize, |a, &b| a.checked_mul(b)) == Some(self.cells.len())
                && self.cells.iter().all(|&x| x <= 1),
            "invalid grid state"
        );
        let old: &Grid = self;
        let radius = old.rules.radius as isize;
        let zrange = if old.rules.dimensions == 3 { radius } else { 0 };
        let mut next = vec![0; old.cells.len()];
        next.par_iter_mut().enumerate().for_each(|(i, out)| {
            let x = (i % old.shape[0]) as isize;
            let y = (i / old.shape[0] % old.shape[1]) as isize;
            let z = (i / (old.shape[0] * old.shape[1])) as isize;
            let mut count = 0u32;
            for dz in -zrange..=zrange {
                for dy in -radius..=radius {
                    for dx in -radius..=radius {
                        if dx != 0 || dy != 0 || dz != 0 {
                            count += old.get([x + dx, y + dy, z + dz]) as u32;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            let mask = if old.cells[i] == 0 {
                old.rules.birth
            } else {
                old.rules.survival
            };
            *out = ((mask >> count) & 1) as u8;
        });
        let stats = StepStats {
            population: next.iter().map(|&x| x as u64).sum(),
            changed: next.iter().zip(&self.cells).filter(|(a, b)| a != b).count() as u64,
        };
        self.cells = next;
        self.tick += 1;
        Ok(stats)
    }
    pub fn population(&self) -> u64 {
        self.cells.iter().map(|&x| x as u64).sum()
    }
    pub fn brush(&mut self, center: [isize; 3], radius: usize, alive: bool) -> Result<()> {
        ensure!(
            radius <= 64 && center.iter().all(|x| x.unsigned_abs() <= 1_000_000),
            "brush radius/position budget exceeded"
        );
        let r = radius as isize;
        for z in -r..=r {
            if self.rules.dimensions == 2 && z != 0 {
                continue;
            }
            for y in -r..=r {
                for x in -r..=r {
                    if x * x + y * y + z * z <= r * r {
                        self.set([center[0] + x, center[1] + y, center[2] + z], alive);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn line(&mut self, a: [isize; 2], b: [isize; 2], alive: bool) -> Result<()> {
        ensure!(
            a.iter().chain(&b).all(|x| x.unsigned_abs() <= 1_000_000),
            "line extent exceeded"
        );
        ensure!(self.rules.dimensions == 2, "line drawing is 2D");
        let (mut x, mut y) = (a[0], a[1]);
        let dx = (b[0] - x).abs();
        let dy = -(b[1] - y).abs();
        let sx = if x < b[0] { 1 } else { -1 };
        let sy = if y < b[1] { 1 } else { -1 };
        let mut error = dx + dy;
        loop {
            self.set([x, y, 0], alive);
            if x == b[0] && y == b[1] {
                break;
            }
            let e = 2 * error;
            if e >= dy {
                error += dy;
                x += sx;
            }
            if e <= dx {
                error += dx;
                y += sy;
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn pattern(&mut self, name: &str) -> Result<()> {
        ensure!(self.rules.dimensions == 2, "pattern library is 2D");
        use patterns::Pattern;
        let pattern = match name {
            "glider" => Pattern::glider(),
            "blinker" => Pattern::blinker(),
            "beacon" => Pattern::beacon(),
            "toad" => Pattern::toad(),
            "lwss" => Pattern::lightweight_spaceship(),
            "pulsar" => Pattern::pulsar(),
            "gosper" => Pattern::gospel_glider_gun(),
            "r-pentomino" => Pattern {
                cells: vec![(1, 0), (2, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1), (1, 2)],
                name: "R-pentomino",
            },
            "acorn" => Pattern {
                cells: vec![(1, 0), (3, 1), (0, 2), (1, 2), (4, 2), (5, 2), (6, 2)],
                name: "Acorn",
            },
            _ => anyhow::bail!("unknown pattern"),
        };
        let cx = self.shape[0] as isize / 2;
        let cy = self.shape[1] as isize / 2;
        for (x, y) in pattern.cells {
            self.set([cx + x as isize, cy + y as isize, 0], true);
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Native binary state: <=4096-byte JSON header, newline, exact u8 cell payload.
    pub fn save(&self, path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> Result<()> {
        self.validate()?;
        use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
        let header = serde_json::json!({"schema":"NOVA-CA-v1","shape":self.shape,"rules":self.rules,"boundary":self.boundary,"tick":self.tick,"sha256":format!("{:x}",Sha256::digest(&self.cells))});
        let mut file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
            .write(true)
            .create_new(true)
            .open(path)?;
        writeln!(file, "{header}")?;
        file.write_all(&self.cells)?;
        Ok(())
    }
    pub fn load(path: impl AsRef<Path>) -> Result<Self> {
        use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
        let mut file = std::io::BufReader::new(std::fs::File::open(path)?);
        let mut header = Vec::new();
        loop {
            ensure!(header.len() < 4096, "CA header too large");
            let mut byte = [0];
            file.read_exact(&mut byte)?;
            if byte[0] == b'\n' {
                break;
            }
            header.push(byte[0]);
        }
        let h: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&header)?;
        ensure!(h["schema"] == "NOVA-CA-v1", "unknown state format");
        let shape = serde_json::from_value(h["shape"].clone())?;
        let rules = serde_json::from_value(h["rules"].clone())?;
        let boundary = serde_json::from_value(h["boundary"].clone())?;
        let mut grid = Self::new(shape, rules, boundary)?;
        file.read_exact(&mut grid.cells)?;
        let mut tail = [0];
        ensure!(
            file.read(&mut tail)? == 0,
            "unexpected bytes after CA state"
        );
        ensure!(
            h["sha256"] == format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(&grid.cells)),
            "state checksum mismatch"
        );
        grid.tick = h["tick"]
            .as_u64()
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("invalid tick"))?;
        grid.validate()?;
        Ok(grid)
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct StepStats {
    pub population: u64,
    pub changed: u64,
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
// Copyright (c) 2025 Game of Life Contributors.
// Adapted from Bridiro/game-of-life, pinned in third_party/upstream.lock.json.
#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct Pattern {
    pub cells: Vec<(i32, i32)>,
    pub name: &'static str,
}

impl Pattern {
    pub fn glider() -> Self {
        Pattern {
            cells: vec![(1, 0), (2, 1), (0, 2), (1, 2), (2, 2)],
            name: "Glider",
        }
    }

    pub fn blinker() -> Self {
        Pattern {
            cells: vec![(-1, 0), (0, 0), (1, 0)],
            name: "Blinker",
        }
    }

    pub fn beacon() -> Self {
        Pattern {
            cells: vec![(0, 0), (1, 0), (0, 1), (3, 2), (2, 3), (3, 3)],
            name: "Beacon",
        }
    }

    pub fn toad() -> Self {
        Pattern {
            cells: vec![(1, 0), (2, 0), (3, 0), (0, 1), (1, 1), (2, 1)],
            name: "Toad",
        }
    }

    pub fn lightweight_spaceship() -> Self {
        Pattern {
            cells: vec![
                (0, 1),
                (3, 1),
                (4, 2),
                (0, 3),
                (4, 3),
                (1, 4),
                (2, 4),
                (3, 4),
                (4, 4),
            ],
            name: "Lightweight Spaceship",
        }
    }

    pub fn pulsar() -> Self {
        let mut cells = Vec::new();

        for x in 2..=4 {
            cells.push((x, 0));
        }
        for x in 8..=10 {
            cells.push((x, 0));
        }

        cells.push((1, 2));
        cells.push((5, 2));
        cells.push((7, 2));
        cells.push((11, 2));
        cells.push((1, 3));
        cells.push((5, 3));
        cells.push((7, 3));
        cells.push((11, 3));
        cells.push((1, 4));
        cells.push((5, 4));
        cells.push((7, 4));
        cells.push((11, 4));

        for x in 2..=4 {
            cells.push((x, 5));
        }
        for x in 8..=10 {
            cells.push((x, 5));
        }
        for x in 2..=4 {
            cells.push((x, 7));
        }
        for x in 8..=10 {
            cells.push((x, 7));
        }

        cells.push((1, 8));
        cells.push((5, 8));
        cells.push((7, 8));
        cells.push((11, 8));
        cells.push((1, 9));
        cells.push((5, 9));
        cells.push((7, 9));
        cells.push((11, 9));
        cells.push((1, 10));
        cells.push((5, 10));
        cells.push((7, 10));
        cells.push((11, 10));

        for x in 2..=4 {
            cells.push((x, 12));
        }
        for x in 8..=10 {
            cells.push((x, 12));
        }

        Pattern {
            cells,
            name: "Pulsar",
        }
    }

    pub fn gospel_glider_gun() -> Self {
        let cells = vec![
            (0, 4),
            (0, 5),
            (1, 4),
            (1, 5),
            (10, 4),
            (10, 5),
            (10, 6),
            (11, 3),
            (11, 7),
            (12, 2),
            (12, 8),
            (13, 2),
            (13, 8),
            (14, 5),
            (15, 3),
            (15, 7),
            (16, 4),
            (16, 5),
            (16, 6),
            (17, 5),
            (20, 2),
            (20, 3),
            (20, 4),
            (21, 2),
            (21, 3),
            (21, 4),
            (22, 1),
            (22, 5),
            (24, 0),
            (24, 1),
            (24, 5),
            (24, 6),
            (34, 2),
            (34, 3),
            (35, 2),
            (35, 3),
        ];

        Pattern {
            cells,
            name: "Gosper Glider Gun",
        }
    }

    pub fn scaled_for_grid(&self, grid_width: u32, grid_height: u32) -> Vec<(u32, u32)> {
        let center_x = grid_width / 2;
        let center_y = grid_height / 2;

        let min_dimension = grid_width.min(grid_height);

        if min_dimension >= 200 {
            match self.name {
                "Glider" => {
                    let mut positions = Vec::new();
                    let offsets = [(-30, -30), (30, -30), (-30, 30), (30, 30)];

                    for (offset_x, offset_y) in offsets.iter() {
                        for (dx, dy) in &self.cells {
                            let x = center_x as i32 + dx + offset_x;
                            let y = center_y as i32 + dy + offset_y;

                            if x >= 0
                                && y >= 0
                                && (x as u32) < grid_width
                                && (y as u32) < grid_height
                            {
                                positions.push((x as u32, y as u32));
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    return positions;
                }
                "Gosper Glider Gun" => {
                    return self
                        .cells
                        .iter()
                        .filter_map(|(dx, dy)| {
                            let x = center_x as i32 + dx;
                            let y = center_y as i32 + dy;

                            if x >= 0
                                && y >= 0
                                && (x as u32) < grid_width
                                && (y as u32) < grid_height
                            {
                                Some((x as u32, y as u32))
                            } else {
                                None
                            }
                        })
                        .collect();
                }
                _ => {}
            }
        }

        self.cells
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|(dx, dy)| {
                let x = center_x as i32 + dx;
                let y = center_y as i32 + dy;

                if x >= 0 && y >= 0 && (x as u32) < grid_width && (y as u32) < grid_height {
                    Some((x as u32, y as u32))
                } else {
                    None
                }
            })
            .collect()
    }
}

//! Bounded standard 2D Life RLE import/export for native editing and interchange.
use crate::Grid;
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
pub fn import(grid: &mut Grid, text: &str, origin: [isize; 2]) -> Result<()> {
    ensure!(
        grid.rules.dimensions == 2 && text.len() <= 4 * 1024 * 1024,
        "bounded 2D RLE required"
    );
    let data = text
        .lines()
        .map(str::trim)
        .filter(|l| !l.starts_with('#') && !l.starts_with('x') && !l.is_empty())
        .collect::<String>();
    let (mut x, mut y, mut count) = (0isize, 0isize, 0usize);
    let mut ended = false;
    for ch in data.chars() {
        if ch.is_ascii_whitespace() {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(d) = ch.to_digit(10) {
            count = count
                .checked_mul(10)
                .and_then(|n| n.checked_add(d as usize))
                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("RLE run overflow"))?;
            ensure!(count <= grid.cells.len(), "RLE run exceeds cell budget");
            continue;
        }
        let run = count.max(1);
        count = 0;
        match ch {
            'b' | 'o' => {
                ensure!(
                    (x as usize)
                        .checked_add(run)
                        .is_some_and(|n| n <= grid.shape[0]),
                    "RLE row exceeds target width"
                );
                if ch == 'o' {
                    for i in 0..run {
                        grid.set([origin[0] + x + i as isize, origin[1] + y, 0], true);
                    }
                }
                x += run as isize;
            }
            '$' => {
                y += run as isize;
                ensure!(y < (grid.shape[1] as isize), "RLE exceeds target height");
                x = 0;
            }
            '!' => {
                ended = true;
                break;
            }
            _ => anyhow::bail!("invalid RLE token"),
        }
    }
    ensure!(ended, "RLE missing terminator");
    Ok(())
}
pub fn export(grid: &Grid) -> Result<String> {
    ensure!(grid.rules.dimensions == 2, "RLE export is 2D");
    grid.validate()?;
    let mut out = format!(
        "#C NOVA native generation {}\nx = {}, y = {}\n",
        grid.tick, grid.shape[0], grid.shape[1]
    );
    for y in 0..grid.shape[1] {
        let mut start = 0;
        while start < grid.shape[0] {
            let alive = grid.cells[y * grid.shape[0] + start];
            let mut end = start + 1;
            while end < grid.shape[0] && grid.cells[y * grid.shape[0] + end] == alive {
                end += 1;
            }
            let n = end - start;
            if n > 1 {
                out.push_str(&n.to_string());
            }
            out.push(if alive == 0 { 'b' } else { 'o' });
            start = end;
        }
        if y + 1 < grid.shape[1] {
            out.push('$');
        }
    }
    out.push('!');
    out.push('\n');
    Ok(out)
}

//! Shared memory contracts. AoS is exactly 64 bytes; hot columns are aligned SoA.
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use bytemuck::{Pod, Zeroable};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

#[repr(C, align(64))]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Pod, Zeroable, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Particle {
    pub pos_rad: [f32; 4],
    pub vel_mass: [f32; 4],
    pub phenotype: [u32; 4],
    pub genome_tape: [u8; 16],
}
const _: () = assert!(std::mem::size_of::<Particle>() == 64);
#[repr(C, align(16))]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Pod, Zeroable)]
pub struct Float4(pub [f32; 4]);
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Lineage {
    pub hash: String,
    pub parents: Vec<String>,
    pub generation: u32,
    pub born_tick: u64,
}
impl Lineage {
    pub fn new(tape: &[u8; 16], parents: Vec<String>, generation: u32, tick: u64) -> Self {
        let mut h = Sha256::new();
        h.update(b"NOVA-LINEAGE-v1");
        h.update(tape);
        for p in &parents {
            h.update(p.as_bytes());
        }
        h.update(generation.to_le_bytes());
        h.update(tick.to_le_bytes());
        Self {
            hash: format!("{:x}", h.finalize()),
            parents,
            generation,
            born_tick: tick,
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Default)]
pub struct Particles {
    pub positions: Vec<Float4>,
    pub velocities: Vec<Float4>,
    pub phenotypes: Vec<[u32; 4]>,
    pub genomes: Vec<[u8; 16]>,
    pub ids: Vec<u64>,
    pub lineages: Vec<Lineage>,
    next_id: u64,
}
impl Particles {
    pub fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.positions.len()
    }
    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.positions.is_empty()
    }
    pub fn push(&mut self, p: Particle, lineage: Lineage) -> Result<u64> {
        ensure!(
            p.pos_rad
                .iter()
                .chain(p.vel_mass.iter())
                .all(|v| v.is_finite()),
            "nonfinite particle"
        );
        ensure!(
            p.pos_rad[3] > 0. && p.vel_mass[3] > 0. && p.phenotype[0] < 8,
            "invalid radius, mass or species"
        );
        let id = self.next_id;
        self.next_id = id
            .checked_add(1)
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("particle ID exhausted"))?;
        self.positions.push(Float4(p.pos_rad));
        self.velocities.push(Float4(p.vel_mass));
        self.phenotypes.push(p.phenotype);
        self.genomes.push(p.genome_tape);
        self.ids.push(id);
        self.lineages.push(lineage);
        Ok(id)
    }
    pub fn get(&self, i: usize) -> Particle {
        Particle {
            pos_rad: self.positions[i].0,
            vel_mass: self.velocities[i].0,
            phenotype: self.phenotypes[i],
            genome_tape: self.genomes[i],
        }
    }
    pub fn reorder(&mut self, order: &[usize]) {
        fn permute<T: Clone>(v: &mut Vec<T>, order: &[usize]) {
            *v = order.iter().map(|&i| v[i].clone()).collect();
        }
        permute(&mut self.positions, order);
        permute(&mut self.velocities, order);
        permute(&mut self.phenotypes, order);
        permute(&mut self.genomes, order);
        permute(&mut self.ids, order);
        permute(&mut self.lineages, order);
    }
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<()> {
        let n = self.len();
        ensure!(
            [
                self.velocities.len(),
                self.phenotypes.len(),
                self.genomes.len(),
                self.ids.len(),
                self.lineages.len()
            ]
            .iter()
            .all(|&x| x == n),
            "SoA column length mismatch"
        );
        ensure!(
            self.positions
                .iter()
                .chain(&self.velocities)
                .all(|x| x.0.iter().all(|x| x.is_finite())),
            "nonfinite simulation state"
        );
        ensure!(
            self.positions.iter().all(|x| x.0[3] > 0.)
                && self.velocities.iter().all(|x| x.0[3] > 0.)
                && self.phenotypes.iter().all(|x| x[0] < 8),
            "invalid particle"
        );
        Ok(())
    }
}
/// Fixed algorithm, independent of platform RNG implementations.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct SplitMix64(pub u64);
impl SplitMix64 {
    pub fn next_u64(&mut self) -> u64 {
        self.0 = self.0.wrapping_add(0x9e3779b97f4a7c15);
        let mut z = self.0;
        z = (z ^ (z >> 30)).wrapping_mul(0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9);
        z = (z ^ (z >> 27)).wrapping_mul(0x94d049bb133111eb);
        z ^ (z >> 31)
    }
    pub fn unit(&mut self) -> f32 {
        ((self.next_u64() >> 40) as f32) / 16777216.
    }
    pub fn signed(&mut self) -> f32 {
        2. * self.unit() - 1.
    }
}
pub fn norm(v: [f32; 3]) -> f32 {
    (v[0] * v[0] + v[1] * v[1] + v[2] * v[2]).sqrt()
}
pub fn delta(a: [f32; 4], b: [f32; 4]) -> [f32; 3] {
    [b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1], b[2] - a[2]]
}

fn main() {
    println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=src/lib.rs");
    println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed=../nova-core/src/lib.rs");
    let dir = std::env::var("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR").unwrap();
    let config = cbindgen::Config {
        language: cbindgen::Language::C,
        include_guard: Some("NOVA_H".into()),
        cpp_compat: true,
        usize_is_size_t: true,
        parse: cbindgen::ParseConfig {
            parse_deps: true,
            include: Some(vec!["nova-core".into()]),
            ..Default::default()
        },
        ..Default::default()
    };
    cbindgen::Builder::new()
        .with_crate(dir)
        .with_config(config)
        .generate()
        .expect("generate C header")
        .write_to_file(std::path::PathBuf::from(std::env::var("OUT_DIR").unwrap()).join("nova.h"));
}

//! C ABI. Pointer validity, ownership and synchronization are caller obligations.
use nova_core::{Lineage, Particle};
use nova_physics::{Config, Universe};
use std::{
    cell::RefCell,
    ffi::c_char,
    panic::{catch_unwind, AssertUnwindSafe},
    ptr,
};
pub struct NovaHandle {
    universe: Universe,
}
/// Portable 64-byte C transfer record; native SoA storage has separate alignment.
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub struct NovaParticle {
    pub pos_rad: [f32; 4],
    pub vel_mass: [f32; 4],
    pub phenotype: [u32; 4],
    pub genome_tape: [u8; 16],
}
const _: () = assert!(std::mem::size_of::<NovaParticle>() == 64);
thread_local! {static LAST_ERROR:RefCell<String>=const{RefCell::new(String::new())};}
fn boundary(f: impl FnOnce() -> Result<(), String>) -> i32 {
    match catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(f)) {
        Ok(Ok(())) => {
            LAST_ERROR.with(|e| e.borrow_mut().clear());
            0
        }
        Ok(Err(e)) => {
            LAST_ERROR.with(|s| *s.borrow_mut() = e);
            -1
        }
        Err(_) => {
            LAST_ERROR.with(|s| *s.borrow_mut() = "Rust panic at ABI boundary".into());
            -2
        }
    }
}
/// Creates an owned handle. preset: 0=canonical, 1=cosmic ink, 2=PVM, 3=liquid MPM.
/// # Safety
/// out must point to a writable handle slot. Free a successful result exactly once.
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn nova_create(
    count: usize,
    seed: u64,
    preset: u32,
    crossover: bool,
    out: *mut *mut NovaHandle,
) -> i32 {
    boundary(|| {
        if out.is_null() {
            return Err("null output pointer".into());
        }
        unsafe {
            *out = ptr::null_mut();
        }
        let mut config = Config {
            seed,
            ..Default::default()
        };
        match preset {
            0 => (),
            1 => config.curl = 0.4,
            2 => {
                config.pvm = true;
                config.crossover = crossover;
            }
            3 => {
                config.mpm = true;
                config.dt = 0.001;
            }
            _ => return Err("unknown preset".into()),
        }
        if crossover && !config.pvm {
            return Err("crossover requires PVM".into());
        }
        let universe = Universe::new(count, config).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        unsafe {
            *out = Box::into_raw(Box::new(NovaHandle { universe }));
        }
        Ok(())
    })
}
/// # Safety
/// handle must be live and exclusively owned for this call; no concurrent array reads.
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn nova_step(handle: *mut NovaHandle, ticks: usize) -> i32 {
    boundary(|| {
        let h = unsafe { handle.as_mut() }.ok_or("null handle")?;
        h.universe.step(ticks).map_err(|e| e.to_string())
    })
}
/// # Safety
/// handle must be live, exclusively owned and not previously freed. Null is allowed.
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn nova_destroy(handle: *mut NovaHandle) {
    if !handle.is_null() {
        let _ = catch_unwind(AssertUnwindSafe(|| unsafe { drop(Box::from_raw(handle)) }));
    }
}
/// Borrow float4 positions/radius. Pointer remains valid only until next mutation or destroy.
/// # Safety
/// handle must be live; data and count writable. Do not mutate or free returned memory.
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn nova_positions(
    handle: *const NovaHandle,
    data: *mut *const f32,
    count: *mut usize,
) -> i32 {
    boundary(|| {
        let h = unsafe { handle.as_ref() }.ok_or("null handle")?;
        if data.is_null() || count.is_null() {
            return Err("null output".into());
        }
        unsafe {
            *data = h.universe.particles.positions.as_ptr().cast();
            *count = h.universe.particles.len();
        }
        Ok(())
    })
}
/// # Safety
/// Same lifetime and synchronization contract as nova_positions.
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn nova_velocities(
    handle: *const NovaHandle,
    data: *mut *const f32,
    count: *mut usize,
) -> i32 {
    boundary(|| {
        let h = unsafe { handle.as_ref() }.ok_or("null handle")?;
        if data.is_null() || count.is_null() {
            return Err("null output".into());
        }
        unsafe {
            *data = h.universe.particles.velocities.as_ptr().cast();
            *count = h.universe.particles.len();
        }
        Ok(())
    })
}
/// Inject a custom 64-byte particle record. The C struct need not be 64-byte aligned.
/// # Safety
/// handle exclusively live; particle points to 64 readable bytes in native C layout.
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn nova_inject(
    handle: *mut NovaHandle,
    particle: *const NovaParticle,
) -> i32 {
    boundary(|| {
        let h = unsafe { handle.as_mut() }.ok_or("null handle")?;
        if particle.is_null() {
            return Err("null particle".into());
        }
        let raw = unsafe { ptr::read_unaligned(particle) };
        let p = Particle {
            pos_rad: raw.pos_rad,
            vel_mass: raw.vel_mass,
            phenotype: raw.phenotype,
            genome_tape: raw.genome_tape,
        };
        if h.universe.config.mpm || h.universe.fusion.is_some() {
            return Err("MPM/fusion membership is fixed for a run".into());
        }
        if h.universe.particles.len() >= h.universe.config.max_particles {
            return Err("particle capacity exceeded".into());
        }
        h.universe
            .particles
            .push(p, Lineage::new(&p.genome_tape, vec![], 0, h.universe.tick))
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        Ok(())
    })
}
/// Returns required bytes including NUL. Copies at most capacity bytes of last thread-local error.
/// # Safety
/// If nonnull, destination must be writable for capacity bytes.
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn nova_last_error(destination: *mut c_char, capacity: usize) -> usize {
    LAST_ERROR.with(|e| {
        let e = e.borrow();
        let bytes = e.as_bytes();
        if !destination.is_null() && capacity > 0 {
            let n = bytes.len().min(capacity - 1);
            unsafe {
                ptr::copy_nonoverlapping(bytes.as_ptr(), destination.cast(), n);
                *destination.add(n) = 0;
            }
        }
        bytes.len() + 1
    })
}

/// Native upstream family: 0=hunar, 1=realethersim, 2=primordial-dish,
/// 3=par-particle-life, 4=fusion, 5=cellflow (independent implementation).
/// # Safety
/// out must be a writable handle slot; destroy a successful result exactly once.
#[no_mangle]
pub unsafe extern "C" fn nova_create_fusion(
    count: usize,
    seed: u64,
    family: u32,
    out: *mut *mut NovaHandle,
) -> i32 {
    boundary(|| {
        if out.is_null() {
            return Err("null output pointer".into());
        }
        unsafe {
            *out = ptr::null_mut();
        }
        let names = [
            "hunar",
            "realethersim",
            "primordial-dish",
            "par-particle-life",
            "fusion",
            "cellflow",
        ];
        let name = names.get(family as usize).ok_or("unknown fusion family")?;
        let f = nova_physics::fusion_family(name).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let universe = Universe::new(
            count,
            Config {
                seed,
                fusion: Some(f),
                ..Default::default()
            },
        )
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        unsafe {
            *out = Box::into_raw(Box::new(NovaHandle { universe }));
        }
        Ok(())
    })
}

//! Native RK4 ports of the four initial realethersim strange-attractor systems (MIT).
use anyhow::{Result, ensure};
use rayon::prelude::*;
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub enum Attractor {
    Lorenz,
    Rossler,
    Aizawa,
    Thomas,
}
impl Attractor {
    pub fn derivative(self, [x, y, z]: [f64; 3]) -> [f64; 3] {
        match self {
            Self::Lorenz => [10. * (y - x), x * (28. - z) - y, x * y - 8. / 3. * z],
            Self::Rossler => [-y - z, x + 0.2 * y, 0.2 + z * (x - 5.7)],
            Self::Aizawa => [
                (z - 0.7) * x - 3.5 * y,
                3.5 * x + (z - 0.7) * y,
                0.6 + 0.95 * z - z * z * z / 3. - (x * x + y * y) * (1. + 0.25 * z)
                    + 0.1 * z * x * x * x,
            ],
            Self::Thomas => [y.sin() - 0.19 * x, z.sin() - 0.19 * y, x.sin() - 0.19 * z],
        }
    }
    pub fn step(self, states: &mut [[f64; 3]], dt: f64) -> Result<()> {
        ensure!(
            dt.is_finite()
                && dt > 0.
                && dt <= 0.05
                && states.iter().flatten().all(|x| x.is_finite()),
            "invalid RK4 input"
        );
        states.par_iter_mut().for_each(|x| {
            let k1 = self.derivative(*x);
            let k2 = self.derivative(std::array::from_fn(|i| x[i] + 0.5 * dt * k1[i]));
            let k3 = self.derivative(std::array::from_fn(|i| x[i] + 0.5 * dt * k2[i]));
            let k4 = self.derivative(std::array::from_fn(|i| x[i] + dt * k3[i]));
            for i in 0..3 {
                x[i] += dt * (k1[i] + 2. * k2[i] + 2. * k3[i] + k4[i]) / 6.;
            }
        });
        ensure!(
            states.iter().flatten().all(|x| x.is_finite()),
            "attractor became nonfinite"
        );
        Ok(())
    }
}

//! Port of primordial-dish's six-child matrix breeding (MIT, Matthias Puchstein).
use crate::Matrix;
use anyhow::{Result, ensure};
use nova_core::SplitMix64;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DishPreset {
    pub name: String,
    pub num_species: usize,
    pub rmax: f32,
    pub beta: f32,
    pub damping: f32,
    pub force_factor: f32,
    pub matrix: Vec<f32>,
}
impl DishPreset {
    pub fn matrix8(&self) -> Result<Matrix> {
        ensure!(
            (1..=8).contains(&self.num_species)
                && self.matrix.len() == self.num_species * self.num_species
                && self.matrix.iter().all(|x| x.is_finite() && x.abs() <= 1.),
            "invalid dish matrix"
        );
        Ok(std::array::from_fn(|i| {
            std::array::from_fn(|j| {
                if i < self.num_species && j < self.num_species {
                    self.matrix[i * self.num_species + j]
                } else {
                    0.
                }
            })
        }))
    }
}
pub fn preset(name: &str) -> Result<DishPreset> {
    let json = match name {
        "genesis" => include_str!(
            "../../../third_party/upstream/primordial-dish/godot/presets/01_genesis.json"
        ),
        "orbs" => {
            include_str!("../../../third_party/upstream/primordial-dish/godot/presets/02_orbs.json")
        }
        "garden" => include_str!(
            "../../../third_party/upstream/primordial-dish/godot/presets/03_garden.json"
        ),
        "symbiotes" => include_str!(
            "../../../third_party/upstream/primordial-dish/godot/presets/04_symbiotes.json"
        ),
        "polymers" => include_str!(
            "../../../third_party/upstream/primordial-dish/godot/presets/05_polymers.json"
        ),
        _ => anyhow::bail!("unknown primordial preset: {name}"),
    };
    let p: DishPreset = serde_json::from_str(json)?;
    p.matrix8()?;
    Ok(p)
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RuleLineage {
    pub hash: String,
    pub parents: Vec<String>,
    pub generation: u32,
    pub operator: String,
}
impl RuleLineage {
    pub fn new(m: &Matrix, parents: Vec<String>, generation: u32, operator: &str) -> Self {
        let mut h = Sha256::new();
        for x in m.iter().flatten() {
            h.update(x.to_le_bytes());
        }
        for p in &parents {
            h.update(p.as_bytes());
        }
        h.update(generation.to_le_bytes());
        h.update(operator.as_bytes());
        Self {
            hash: format!("{:x}", h.finalize()),
            parents,
            generation,
            operator: operator.into(),
        }
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Candidate {
    pub matrix: Matrix,
    pub lineage: RuleLineage,
}
fn gaussian(rng: &mut SplitMix64) -> f32 {
    (-2. * rng.unit().max(1e-7).ln()).sqrt() * (std::f32::consts::TAU * rng.unit()).cos()
}
pub fn breed(
    parent: &Matrix,
    mate: &Matrix,
    k: usize,
    seed: u64,
    generation: u32,
) -> Result<Vec<Candidate>> {
    ensure!(
        (1..=8).contains(&k)
            && parent
                .iter()
                .flatten()
                .chain(mate.iter().flatten())
                .all(|x| x.is_finite() && x.abs() <= 1.),
        "invalid breeding parents"
    );
    let mut rng = SplitMix64(seed);
    let a = RuleLineage::new(parent, vec![], 0, "parent").hash;
    let b = RuleLineage::new(mate, vec![], 0, "mate").hash;
    let mut candidates = Vec::new();
    for child in 0..6 {
        let mut m = *parent;
        let op = match child {
            0 => "clone",
            1 => "mutate-0.08",
            2 => "mutate-0.18",
            3 => "mutate-0.35",
            4 => "row-crossover",
            _ => "wild",
        };
        for i in 0..k {
            let use_mate = child == 4 && rng.unit() < 0.5;
            for j in 0..k {
                m[i][j] = match child {
                    1..=3 => (m[i][j] + [0.08, 0.18, 0.35][child - 1] * gaussian(&mut rng))
                        .clamp(-1., 1.),
                    4 => {
                        if use_mate {
                            mate[i][j]
                        } else {
                            m[i][j]
                        }
                    }
                    5 => rng.signed(),
                    _ => m[i][j],
                };
            }
        }
        let parents = if child == 4 {
            vec![a.clone(), b.clone()]
        } else {
            vec![a.clone()]
        };
        let lineage = RuleLineage::new(&m, parents, generation, op);
        candidates.push(Candidate { matrix: m, lineage });
    }
    Ok(candidates)
}

//! Compact count/prefix/scatter grid; toroidal neighbors are deduplicated for 1/2 cells.
use crate::{Boundary, Settings};
use anyhow::{Result, ensure};
use nova_core::Particles;
pub struct Grid {
    pub offsets: Vec<usize>,
    pub order: Vec<usize>,
    dims: [usize; 3],
    half: [f32; 3],
    width: [f32; 3],
    periodic: bool,
    axes: usize,
}
impl Grid {
    pub fn build(p: &Particles, s: &Settings) -> Result<Self> {
        let cutoff = s.cutoff();
        let mut dims = [1; 3];
        let mut width = [1.; 3];
        for k in 0..s.dimensions {
            dims[k] = ((2. * s.half_extent[k] / cutoff).floor() as usize).max(1);
            width[k] = 2. * s.half_extent[k] / dims[k] as f32;
        }
        let cells = dims
            .iter()
            .try_fold(1usize, |a, &b| a.checked_mul(b))
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("grid size overflow"))?;
        ensure!(cells <= 8_000_000, "fusion grid cell budget exceeded");
        let mut grid = Self {
            offsets: vec![0; cells + 1],
            order: vec![0; p.len()],
            dims,
            half: s.half_extent,
            width,
            periodic: s.boundary == Boundary::Wrap,
            axes: s.dimensions,
        };
        // Nonperiodic open clouds must fit the declared index extent; never silently omit neighbors.
        ensure!(
            p.positions.iter().all(|p| (0..s.dimensions)
                .all(|k| p.0[k] >= -s.half_extent[k] && p.0[k] <= s.half_extent[k])),
            "cloud left fusion index extent; enlarge half_extent or use wrap/bounce"
        );
        for pos in &p.positions {
            let key = grid.key(grid.coord(pos.0));
            grid.offsets[key + 1] += 1;
        }
        for c in 1..=cells {
            grid.offsets[c] += grid.offsets[c - 1];
        }
        let mut cursor = grid.offsets[..cells].to_vec();
        for (i, pos) in p.positions.iter().enumerate() {
            let key = grid.key(grid.coord(pos.0));
            grid.order[cursor[key]] = i;
            cursor[key] += 1;
        }
        Ok(grid)
    }
    fn coord(&self, p: [f32; 4]) -> [usize; 3] {
        std::array::from_fn(|k| {
            if k < self.axes {
                (((p[k] + self.half[k]) / self.width[k]).floor() as usize).min(self.dims[k] - 1)
            } else {
                0
            }
        })
    }
    fn key(&self, c: [usize; 3]) -> usize {
        (c[2] * self.dims[1] + c[1]) * self.dims[0] + c[0]
    }
    pub fn candidates(&self, p: [f32; 4]) -> impl Iterator<Item = usize> + '_ {
        let c = self.coord(p);
        let mut keys = [usize::MAX; 27];
        let mut count = 0;
        for z in -1..=1 {
            if self.axes == 2 && z != 0 {
                continue;
            }
            for y in -1..=1 {
                for x in -1..=1 {
                    let mut q = [0; 3];
                    let offsets = [x, y, z];
                    let mut valid = true;
                    for k in 0..self.axes {
                        let v = c[k] as i64 + offsets[k];
                        if self.periodic {
                            q[k] = v.rem_euclid(self.dims[k] as i64) as usize;
                        } else if v < 0 || v >= self.dims[k] as i64 {
                            valid = false;
                            break;
                        } else {
                            q[k] = v as usize;
                        }
                    }
                    if valid {
                        let key = self.key(q);
                        if !keys[..count].contains(&key) {
                            keys[count] = key;
                            count += 1;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        keys.into_iter()
            .take(count)
            .flat_map(|key| self.offsets[key]..self.offsets[key + 1])
            .map(|idx| self.order[idx])
    }
}
