// SPDX-License-Identifier: AGPL-3.0-or-later
// Adapted from paulrobello/par-particle-life @ 6f495dae5b2bbb65fdafd2b7b6dab59142dfdf02.
// Copyright Paul Robello. Original and license retained in third_party/upstream.
// Particle data structures for the simulation.

use bytemuck::{Pod, Zeroable};
use rand::{Rng, RngExt};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

/// A single particle in the simulation.
///
/// CPU-side interchange type only — never uploaded to the GPU as an AoS blob.
/// The GPU storage hot path uses the SoA types `ParticlePosType`, `ParticleVel`,
/// and `ParticleVelHalf` (see below), each of which carries its own
/// `#[repr(C, align(...))]` layout. `Particle` is `repr(C, align(16))` with
/// explicit padding to 32 bytes so that `Vec<Particle>` is 16-byte aligned for
/// SIMD-friendly access; the size is locked by the static assertion at the end
/// of this file. Position and velocity are stored as 2D vectors, with the
/// particle type indicating which species/color the particle belongs to.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Pod, Zeroable)]
#[repr(C, align(16))]
pub struct Particle {
    /// X position in world coordinates.
    pub x: f32,
    /// Y position in world coordinates.
    pub y: f32,
    /// X velocity component.
    pub vx: f32,
    /// Y velocity component.
    pub vy: f32,
    /// Particle type/species index (0 to num_types-1).
    pub particle_type: u32,
    /// Explicit tail padding rounding the struct to the 16-byte alignment
    /// required by `repr(align(16))`. Carries no semantic meaning; the SoA
    /// GPU types are the source of truth for storage-buffer layout.
    pub _padding: [u32; 3],
}

// Lock the layout: 5 used fields (20 bytes) + 12 bytes tail padding = 32 bytes.
// Drop this assertion only if you are intentionally changing `Particle`'s
// CPU-side stride and have audited every allocator / cast site.
const _: () = assert!(std::mem::size_of::<Particle>() == 32);
const _: () = assert!(std::mem::align_of::<Particle>() == 16);
const _: () = assert!(std::mem::offset_of!(Particle, particle_type) == 16);

impl Default for Particle {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            x: 0.0,
            y: 0.0,
            vx: 0.0,
            vy: 0.0,
            particle_type: 0,
            _padding: [0; 3],
        }
    }
}

impl Particle {
    /// Create a new particle at the given position with zero velocity.
    pub fn new(x: f32, y: f32, particle_type: u32) -> Self {
        Self {
            x,
            y,
            particle_type,
            ..Default::default()
        }
    }

    /// Create a new particle with position and velocity.
    pub fn with_velocity(x: f32, y: f32, vx: f32, vy: f32, particle_type: u32) -> Self {
        Self {
            x,
            y,
            vx,
            vy,
            particle_type,
            ..Default::default()
        }
    }

    /// Create a zero-velocity particle at a random position inside the world.
    pub fn random_in_world<R: Rng + ?Sized>(
        rng: &mut R,
        world_width: f32,
        world_height: f32,
        num_types: u32,
    ) -> Self {
        let x = rng.random::<f32>() * world_width;
        let y = rng.random::<f32>() * world_height;
        let particle_type = rng.random_range(0..num_types.max(1));
        Self::new(x, y, particle_type)
    }

    /// Get position as a glam Vec2.
    #[inline]
    pub fn position(&self) -> glam::Vec2 {
        glam::Vec2::new(self.x, self.y)
    }

    /// Set position from a glam Vec2.
    #[inline]
    pub fn set_position(&mut self, pos: glam::Vec2) {
        self.x = pos.x;
        self.y = pos.y;
    }

    /// Get velocity as a glam Vec2.
    #[inline]
    pub fn velocity(&self) -> glam::Vec2 {
        glam::Vec2::new(self.vx, self.vy)
    }

    /// Set velocity from a glam Vec2.
    #[inline]
    pub fn set_velocity(&mut self, vel: glam::Vec2) {
        self.vx = vel.x;
        self.vy = vel.y;
    }

    /// Get the speed (magnitude of velocity).
    #[inline]
    pub fn speed(&self) -> f32 {
        self.velocity().length()
    }
}

/// Interaction matrix defining attraction/repulsion between particle types.
///
/// The matrix is stored as a flattened 1D array where `data[i * size + j]`
/// gives the interaction strength from particle type `i` towards type `j`.
///
/// - Positive values indicate attraction
/// - Negative values indicate repulsion
/// - Values typically range from -1.0 to 1.0
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct InteractionMatrix {
    /// Flattened NxN matrix data.
    pub data: Vec<f32>,
    /// Number of particle types (matrix is size x size).
    pub size: usize,
}

impl InteractionMatrix {
    /// Create a new zero-initialized interaction matrix.
    pub fn new(size: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            data: vec![0.0; size * size],
            size,
        }
    }

    /// Create a matrix filled with a constant value.
    pub fn filled(size: usize, value: f32) -> Self {
        Self {
            data: vec![value; size * size],
            size,
        }
    }

    /// Create an identity-like matrix where same-type particles attract.
    pub fn identity(size: usize) -> Self {
        let mut matrix = Self::new(size);
        for i in 0..size {
            matrix.set(i, i, 1.0);
        }
        matrix
    }

    /// Get the interaction strength between two particle types.
    #[inline]
    pub fn get(&self, from_type: usize, to_type: usize) -> f32 {
        debug_assert!(from_type < self.size && to_type < self.size);
        self.data[from_type * self.size + to_type]
    }

    /// Set the interaction strength between two particle types.
    #[inline]
    pub fn set(&mut self, from_type: usize, to_type: usize, value: f32) {
        debug_assert!(from_type < self.size && to_type < self.size);
        self.data[from_type * self.size + to_type] = value;
    }

    /// Make the matrix symmetric (average of m[i][j] and m[j][i]).
    pub fn symmetrize(&mut self) {
        for i in 0..self.size {
            for j in i + 1..self.size {
                let avg = (self.get(i, j) + self.get(j, i)) / 2.0;
                self.set(i, j, avg);
                self.set(j, i, avg);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Make the matrix anti-symmetric (m[i][j] = -m[j][i]).
    pub fn anti_symmetrize(&mut self) {
        for i in 0..self.size {
            for j in i + 1..self.size {
                let val = (self.get(i, j) - self.get(j, i)) / 2.0;
                self.set(i, j, val);
                self.set(j, i, -val);
            }
            // Diagonal should be zero for anti-symmetric
            self.set(i, i, 0.0);
        }
    }

    /// Clamp all values to the given range.
    pub fn clamp(&mut self, min: f32, max: f32) {
        for val in &mut self.data {
            *val = val.clamp(min, max);
        }
    }

    /// Validate that all values are within expected bounds.
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        for (i, &val) in self.data.iter().enumerate() {
            if val.is_nan() {
                return Err(format!("NaN value at index {i}"));
            }
            if val.is_infinite() {
                return Err(format!("Infinite value at index {i}"));
            }
            if !(-2.0..=2.0).contains(&val) {
                return Err(format!(
                    "Value {val} at index {i} is outside expected range [-2, 2]"
                ));
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Radius matrices defining the minimum and maximum interaction distances.
///
/// For each pair of particle types (i, j), the interaction occurs when
/// the distance is between `min_radius[i*size + j]` and `max_radius[i*size + j]`.
///
/// - Below min_radius: Repulsion force is applied
/// - Between min and max: Attraction/repulsion from InteractionMatrix
/// - Above max_radius: No interaction
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct RadiusMatrix {
    /// Minimum interaction distances (flattened NxN).
    pub min_radius: Vec<f32>,
    /// Maximum interaction distances (flattened NxN).
    pub max_radius: Vec<f32>,
    /// Number of particle types.
    pub size: usize,
}

impl RadiusMatrix {
    /// Create new radius matrices with uniform values.
    pub fn new(size: usize, min_radius: f32, max_radius: f32) -> Self {
        Self {
            min_radius: vec![min_radius; size * size],
            max_radius: vec![max_radius; size * size],
            size,
        }
    }

    /// Create radius matrices with default values (30-80 pixels).
    pub fn default_for_size(size: usize) -> Self {
        Self::new(size, 30.0, 80.0)
    }

    /// Get the minimum radius for interaction between two types.
    #[inline]
    pub fn get_min(&self, from_type: usize, to_type: usize) -> f32 {
        self.min_radius[from_type * self.size + to_type]
    }

    /// Get the maximum radius for interaction between two types.
    #[inline]
    pub fn get_max(&self, from_type: usize, to_type: usize) -> f32 {
        self.max_radius[from_type * self.size + to_type]
    }

    /// Set the radius range for interaction between two types.
    pub fn set(&mut self, from_type: usize, to_type: usize, min: f32, max: f32) {
        let idx = from_type * self.size + to_type;
        self.min_radius[idx] = min;
        self.max_radius[idx] = max;
    }

    /// Set the same radius range for all type pairs.
    pub fn set_uniform(&mut self, min: f32, max: f32) {
        for val in &mut self.min_radius {
            *val = min;
        }
        for val in &mut self.max_radius {
            *val = max;
        }
    }

    /// Get the maximum radius value in the matrix (for spatial hash sizing).
    pub fn max_interaction_radius(&self) -> f32 {
        self.max_radius.iter().copied().fold(0.0, f32::max)
    }

    /// Validate that all radius values are sensible.
    pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        if self.min_radius.len() != self.size * self.size {
            return Err("min_radius size mismatch".to_string());
        }
        if self.max_radius.len() != self.size * self.size {
            return Err("max_radius size mismatch".to_string());
        }

        for i in 0..self.size * self.size {
            let min = self.min_radius[i];
            let max = self.max_radius[i];

            if min < 0.0 {
                return Err(format!("Negative min_radius at index {i}"));
            }
            if max < min {
                return Err(format!(
                    "max_radius ({max}) < min_radius ({min}) at index {i}"
                ));
            }
            if max.is_nan() || min.is_nan() {
                return Err(format!("NaN radius at index {i}"));
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

/// Structure for Position and Type (SoA layout).
/// aligned to 16 bytes to match WGSL vec2<f32> + u32 + pad.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Pod, Zeroable)]
#[repr(C, align(16))]
pub struct ParticlePosType {
    pub x: f32,
    pub y: f32,
    pub particle_type: u32,
    pub _padding: u32,
}

impl From<&Particle> for ParticlePosType {
    fn from(p: &Particle) -> Self {
        Self {
            x: p.x,
            y: p.y,
            particle_type: p.particle_type,
            _padding: 0,
        }
    }
}

/// Structure for Velocity (SoA layout).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Pod, Zeroable)]
#[repr(C)] // size 8, align 4. Matches vec2<f32> array stride 8.
pub struct ParticleVel {
    pub vx: f32,
    pub vy: f32,
}

impl From<&Particle> for ParticleVel {
    fn from(p: &Particle) -> Self {
        Self { vx: p.vx, vy: p.vy }
    }
}

/// Structure for Position and Type (SoA layout, FP16).
/// Size: 2 (x) + 2 (y) + 4 (type) = 8 bytes.
/// Alignment: 4 bytes (max align of u32 and vec2<f16>).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Pod, Zeroable)]
#[repr(C, align(4))]
pub struct ParticlePosTypeHalf {
    pub x: half::f16,
    pub y: half::f16,
    pub particle_type: u32,
}

impl From<&Particle> for ParticlePosTypeHalf {
    fn from(p: &Particle) -> Self {
        Self {
            x: half::f16::from_f32(p.x),
            y: half::f16::from_f32(p.y),
            particle_type: p.particle_type,
        }
    }
}

/// Structure for Velocity (SoA layout, FP16).
/// Size: 2 (vx) + 2 (vy) = 4 bytes.
/// Alignment: 4 bytes (matches vec2<f16> array stride).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Pod, Zeroable)]
#[repr(C, align(4))]
pub struct ParticleVelHalf {
    pub vx: half::f16,
    pub vy: half::f16,
}

impl From<&Particle> for ParticleVelHalf {
    fn from(p: &Particle) -> Self {
        Self {
            vx: half::f16::from_f32(p.vx),
            vy: half::f16::from_f32(p.vy),
        }
    }
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: AGPL-3.0-or-later
// Adapted from paulrobello/par-particle-life @ 6f495dae5b2bbb65fdafd2b7b6dab59142dfdf02.
// Copyright Paul Robello. Original and license retained in third_party/upstream.
// Spatial hashing for optimized neighbor queries.
//
// This module provides a spatial hash grid that allows O(1) average-case
// neighbor lookups instead of O(n) brute force scanning.

use super::Particle;
use glam::Vec2;

/// Hard cap on total grid cells to prevent OOM allocation when `world_size` is
/// huge or `cell_size` is tiny (SEC-005). At ~24 bytes per empty `Vec<usize>`
/// header, 4M cells is roughly 100 MB — far beyond any realistic config, but
/// bounded enough that a corrupt `world_size = 1e30` cannot OOM the machine.
const MAX_SPATIAL_HASH_CELLS: usize = 4_096_000;

/// A spatial hash grid for efficient neighbor queries.
///
/// The world is divided into cells of uniform size. Each cell stores
/// indices of particles that fall within it. Neighbor queries check
/// only the relevant cells instead of all particles.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SpatialHash {
    /// Cell size in world units.
    cell_size: f32,
    /// Number of cells in X direction.
    grid_width: usize,
    /// Number of cells in Y direction.
    grid_height: usize,
    /// Particle indices stored per cell.
    cells: Vec<Vec<usize>>,
    /// World bounds.
    world_size: Vec2,
}

impl SpatialHash {
    /// Build a spatial hash from a slice of particles.
    ///
    /// Returns `Err` if:
    /// * `world_size` is non-finite/non-positive,
    /// * the implied grid would overflow `usize` or exceed `MAX_SPATIAL_HASH_CELLS`,
    /// * `cell_size < max_interaction_radius` (the load-bearing neighbour-correctness
    ///   invariant — previously enforced ad hoc at each call site, now at the
    ///   type boundary per ARC-037).
    ///
    /// Callers should fall back to brute-force neighbour search on error.
    ///
    /// # Arguments
    /// * `particles` - Particles to index
    /// * `cell_size` - Size of each cell; must be `>= max_interaction_radius`
    ///   so a single cell-radius ring covers every interaction partner.
    /// * `max_interaction_radius` - Largest interaction radius any particle
    ///   pair can have. Enforced as a lower bound on `cell_size`.
    /// * `world_size` - Size of the world bounds
    pub fn build(
        particles: &[Particle],
        cell_size: f32,
        max_interaction_radius: f32,
        world_size: Vec2,
    ) -> Result<Self, String> {
        if !world_size.x.is_finite()
            || !world_size.y.is_finite()
            || world_size.x <= 0.0
            || world_size.y <= 0.0
        {
            return Err(format!(
                "SpatialHash world_size must be positive and finite, got ({}, {})",
                world_size.x, world_size.y
            ));
        }
        // ARC-037: cell-size invariant belongs here, not at every call site.
        // `query_radius` only scans a single cell-width ring around the
        // query cell — if any interaction radius exceeds the cell size, a
        // neighbour could be silently missed. Reject that at construction.
        let max_radius = max_interaction_radius.max(0.0);
        if !max_radius.is_finite() || cell_size < max_radius {
            return Err(format!(
                "SpatialHash cell_size ({cell_size}) must be >= max_interaction_radius \
                 ({max_radius}); increase cell_size or reduce interaction radii \
                 (world_size=({wx}, {wy}))",
                wx = world_size.x,
                wy = world_size.y,
            ));
        }
        let cell_size = cell_size.max(1.0); // Prevent division by zero
        let grid_width = ((world_size.x / cell_size).ceil() as usize).max(1);
        let grid_height = ((world_size.y / cell_size).ceil() as usize).max(1);

        let total_cells = grid_width.checked_mul(grid_height).ok_or_else(|| {
            format!(
                "SpatialHash grid {grid_width}x{grid_height} overflows usize \
                 (world_size=({wx}, {wy}), cell_size={cs})",
                wx = world_size.x,
                wy = world_size.y,
                cs = cell_size
            )
        })?;
        if total_cells > MAX_SPATIAL_HASH_CELLS {
            return Err(format!(
                "SpatialHash grid {grid_width}x{grid_height} ({total_cells} cells) \
                 exceeds limit of {MAX_SPATIAL_HASH_CELLS}; \
                 increase cell_size or reduce world_size \
                 (world_size=({wx}, {wy}), cell_size={cs})",
                wx = world_size.x,
                wy = world_size.y,
                cs = cell_size
            ));
        }

        let mut cells = vec![Vec::new(); total_cells];

        for (i, p) in particles.iter().enumerate() {
            let cell_x = ((p.x / cell_size) as usize).min(grid_width.saturating_sub(1));
            let cell_y = ((p.y / cell_size) as usize).min(grid_height.saturating_sub(1));
            let cell_idx = cell_y * grid_width + cell_x;

            if cell_idx < cells.len() {
                cells[cell_idx].push(i);
            }
        }

        Ok(Self {
            cell_size,
            grid_width,
            grid_height,
            cells,
            world_size,
        })
    }

    /// Query all particle indices within a radius of a position.
    ///
    /// Returns an iterator over particle indices that might be within range.
    /// Note: This returns particles in nearby cells, so some may be slightly
    /// outside the radius. Caller should do precise distance checks.
    ///
    /// # Arguments
    /// * `position` - Center point to query from
    /// * `radius` - Maximum distance to search
    /// * `world_size` - World bounds (for wrapping)
    /// * `wrap` - Whether to consider wrapped positions
    pub fn query_radius(
        &self,
        position: Vec2,
        radius: f32,
        _world_size: Vec2,
        wrap: bool,
    ) -> Vec<usize> {
        let mut result = Vec::new();

        // Calculate cell range to check
        let cells_to_check = (radius / self.cell_size).ceil() as i32 + 1;

        let center_x = (position.x / self.cell_size) as i32;
        let center_y = (position.y / self.cell_size) as i32;

        for dy in -cells_to_check..=cells_to_check {
            for dx in -cells_to_check..=cells_to_check {
                let mut cell_x = center_x + dx;
                let mut cell_y = center_y + dy;

                // Handle wrapping
                if wrap {
                    cell_x = cell_x.rem_euclid(self.grid_width as i32);
                    cell_y = cell_y.rem_euclid(self.grid_height as i32);
                } else {
                    // Skip out-of-bounds cells
                    if cell_x < 0 || cell_x >= self.grid_width as i32 {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if cell_y < 0 || cell_y >= self.grid_height as i32 {
                        continue;
                    }
                }

                let cell_idx = (cell_y as usize) * self.grid_width + (cell_x as usize);
                if cell_idx < self.cells.len() {
                    result.extend(self.cells[cell_idx].iter().copied());
                }
            }
        }

        result
    }

    /// Get the cell index for a position.
    #[inline]
    pub fn get_cell_index(&self, x: f32, y: f32) -> Option<usize> {
        if x < 0.0 || y < 0.0 || x >= self.world_size.x || y >= self.world_size.y {
            return None;
        }

        let cell_x = (x / self.cell_size) as usize;
        let cell_y = (y / self.cell_size) as usize;

        if cell_x < self.grid_width && cell_y < self.grid_height {
            Some(cell_y * self.grid_width + cell_x)
        } else {
            None
        }
    }

    /// Get particles in a specific cell.
    pub fn get_cell(&self, cell_idx: usize) -> &[usize] {
        self.cells
            .get(cell_idx)
            .map(|c| c.as_slice())
            .unwrap_or(&[])
    }

    /// Get the number of cells in the grid.
    pub fn num_cells(&self) -> usize {
        self.cells.len()
    }

    /// Get grid dimensions.
    pub fn dimensions(&self) -> (usize, usize) {
        (self.grid_width, self.grid_height)
    }

    /// Get the cell size.
    pub fn cell_size(&self) -> f32 {
        self.cell_size
    }

    /// Clear all cells (for reuse).
    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        for cell in &mut self.cells {
            cell.clear();
        }
    }

    /// Get statistics about the spatial hash distribution.
    pub fn stats(&self) -> SpatialHashStats {
        let non_empty = self.cells.iter().filter(|c| !c.is_empty()).count();
        let total_particles: usize = self.cells.iter().map(|c| c.len()).sum();
        let max_per_cell = self.cells.iter().map(|c| c.len()).max().unwrap_or(0);
        let avg_per_cell = if non_empty > 0 {
            total_particles as f32 / non_empty as f32
        } else {
            0.0
        };

        SpatialHashStats {
            total_cells: self.cells.len(),
            non_empty_cells: non_empty,
            total_particles,
            max_per_cell,
            avg_per_cell,
        }
    }
}

/// Statistics about spatial hash distribution.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SpatialHashStats {
    /// Total number of cells in the grid.
    pub total_cells: usize,
    /// Number of cells containing at least one particle.
    pub non_empty_cells: usize,
    /// Total particles indexed.
    pub total_particles: usize,
    /// Maximum particles in any single cell.
    pub max_per_cell: usize,
    /// Average particles per non-empty cell.
    pub avg_per_cell: f32,
}

fn main() {
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir("shaders").expect("shaders directory") {
        let p = entry.unwrap().path();
        if p.extension().and_then(|x| x.to_str()) != Some("wgsl") {
            continue;
        }
        println!("cargo:rerun-if-changed={}", p.display());
        let source = std::fs::read_to_string(&p).unwrap();
        let module = naga::front::wgsl::parse_str(&source)
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {}", p.display(), e.emit_to_string(&source)));
        naga::valid::Validator::new(
            naga::valid::ValidationFlags::all(),
            naga::valid::Capabilities::empty(),
        )
        .validate(&module)
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| panic!("{}: {:?}", p.display(), e));
    }
}

// Integer Moore automata. Native WGSL adaptation of the g3dl/WebGL ping-pong model.
// Licensed source references and modifications are documented in THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md.
struct Config {shape:vec4<u32>,rule:vec4<u32>,control:vec4<u32>}
@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage,read> src:array<u32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage,read_write> dst:array<u32>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> cfg:Config;
@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage,read_write> counters:array<atomic<u32>>;
@group(0) @binding(4) var<storage,read_write> living:array<u32>;
fn value(p:vec3<i32>)->u32 {
 let shape=vec3<i32>(cfg.shape.xyz);var q=p;
 if(cfg.rule.w==1u){q=((q%shape)+shape)%shape;}
 else if(any(q<vec3(0))||any(q>=shape)){return 0u;}
 return src[(u32(q.z)*cfg.shape.y+u32(q.y))*cfg.shape.x+u32(q.x)];
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn evolve(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
 let i=id.x+id.y*cfg.control.y;if(i>=cfg.shape.w){return;}
 let x=i%cfg.shape.x;let y=(i/cfg.shape.x)%cfg.shape.y;let z=i/(cfg.shape.x*cfg.shape.y);let p=vec3<i32>(i32(x),i32(y),i32(z));
 let radius=i32(cfg.rule.z);let zr=select(0,radius,cfg.control.x==3u);var total=0u;
 for(var dz=-zr;dz<=zr;dz++){for(var dy=-radius;dy<=radius;dy++){for(var dx=-radius;dx<=radius;dx++){
  if(dx!=0||dy!=0||dz!=0){total+=value(p+vec3(dx,dy,dz));}
 }}}
 let mask=select(cfg.rule.x,cfg.rule.y,src[i]!=0u);let next=(mask>>total)&1u;dst[i]=next;
 atomicAdd(&counters[0],next);atomicAdd(&counters[1],select(0u,1u,next!=src[i]));
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn compact(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
 let i=id.x+id.y*cfg.control.y;if(i<cfg.shape.w&&src[i]!=0u){let slot=atomicAdd(&counters[0],1u);living[slot]=i;}
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: AGPL-3.0-or-later
// Native NOVA fusion kernels; PAR force/alignment adaptation, MIT force-law ports.
// Cell density-feedback formula is an independent implementation, not copied CellFlow source.
struct Particle {p:vec4<f32>,v:vec4<f32>,phenotype:vec4<u32>,genome:vec4<u32>}
struct Params {
 counts:vec4<u32>,domain:vec4<f32>,dynamics:vec4<f32>,limits:vec4<f32>,
 step:vec4<u32>,cell:vec4<f32>,feedback:vec4<f32>,brush:vec4<f32>,brush_radius:vec4<f32>,
 matrix:array<vec4<f32>,16>,inner:array<vec4<f32>,16>,outer:array<vec4<f32>,16>,masses:array<vec4<f32>,2>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage,read> src:array<Particle>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage,read_write> dst:array<Particle>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage,read> ranges:array<vec2<u32>>;
@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage,read> neighbors:array<u32>;
@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> cfg:Params;
fn difference(a:vec3<f32>,b:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
 var d=b-a;if(cfg.counts.w==1u){let size=2.*cfg.domain.xyz;d=d-size*round(d/size);}
 if(cfg.counts.z==2u){d.z=0.;}return d;
}
fn hash(value:u32)->u32 {let x=value*747796405u+2891336453u;let y=((x>>((x>>28u)+4u))^x)*277803737u;return (y>>22u)^y;}
fn thermal(id:u32,k:u32)->f32{return f32(hash(id+cfg.step.x*74839u+k*7919u))/4294967295.-.5;}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn advance(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid:vec3<u32>){
 let i=gid.x;if(i>=cfg.counts.x){return;}
 let a=src[i];let range=ranges[i];let family=cfg.counts.y;let dimensions=cfg.counts.z;
 var force=vec3(0.);var alignment=vec3(0.);var weight=0.;var count=0u;
 let epsilon=select(.0001,1e-10,family==1u);
 for(var cursor=range.x;cursor<range.y;cursor++){
  let j=neighbors[cursor];if(j==i){continue;}
  let other=src[j];let d=difference(a.p.xyz,other.p.xyz);let d2=dot(d,d);
  let key=a.phenotype.x*8u+other.phenotype.x;
  let lo=cfg.inner[key/4u][key%4u];let hi=cfg.outer[key/4u][key%4u];
  if(d2<epsilon||d2>=hi*hi){continue;}
  count+=1u;let distance=sqrt(d2);let affinity=cfg.matrix[key/4u][key%4u];var f=0.;
  if(family==0u){f=-affinity;}
  else if(family==5u){let q=distance/hi;f=affinity*(cfg.cell.x/(1.+pow(cfg.cell.z*q,2.))-cfg.cell.y*q*q);}
  else if(distance<lo){f=(distance/lo-1.)*cfg.dynamics.z;}
  else{f=affinity*(1.-abs(2.*distance-lo-hi)/(hi-lo));}
  force+=d/distance*f;
  if(cfg.dynamics.w>0.&&distance>=lo){let w=1.-(distance-lo)/(hi-lo);alignment+=(other.v.xyz-a.v.xyz)*w;weight+=w;}
 }
 if(weight>0.){force+=cfg.dynamics.w*alignment/weight;}
 let checked=f32(range.y-range.x-1u);var scale=1.;if(cfg.limits.z>0.&&checked>cfg.limits.z){scale=cfg.limits.z/checked;}
 var adaptive=1.;if(family==5u){let occupancy=clamp(f32(a.phenotype.z)/cfg.feedback.x,0.,1.);adaptive=1.+3.*cfg.cell.w-3.99*cfg.cell.w*occupancy;}
 let mass=cfg.masses[a.phenotype.x/4u][a.phenotype.x%4u];
 let dt=cfg.domain.w;let retention=cfg.dynamics.y;
 let kick=select(cfg.dynamics.x*dt,cfg.dynamics.x,family==0u||family==3u);
 let acceleration=force*(kick*scale*adaptive/mass);
 var velocity=(a.v.xyz+acceleration)*retention;
 if(family==2u||family==5u){velocity=a.v.xyz*retention+acceleration;}
 if(cfg.limits.y>0.){for(var k=0u;k<dimensions;k++){velocity[k]+=thermal(a.genome.x,k)*cfg.limits.y;}}
 if(cfg.brush_radius.x>0.){let d=difference(a.p.xyz,cfg.brush.xyz);let distance=length(d);if(distance>1e-6&&distance<cfg.brush_radius.x){velocity+=d/distance*cfg.brush.w*(1.-distance/cfg.brush_radius.x)*dt;}}
 let speed=length(velocity);var flags=select(0u,2u,scale<1.);
 if(cfg.limits.x>0.&&speed>cfg.limits.x){velocity*=cfg.limits.x/speed;flags|=1u;}
 var position=a.p.xyz+dt*velocity;
 for(var k=0u;k<dimensions;k++){
  let half=cfg.domain[k];let width=2.*half;
  if(cfg.counts.w==1u){position[k]=(position[k]+half)-width*floor((position[k]+half)/width)-half;}
  if(cfg.counts.w==2u){let t=position[k]+half;let q=t-2.*width*floor(t/(2.*width));if(q>width){position[k]=2.*width-q-half;velocity[k]=-velocity[k];}else{position[k]=q-half;}}
 }
 var result=a;result.p=vec4(position,a.p.w);result.v=vec4(velocity,a.v.w);result.phenotype.y=flags;result.phenotype.z=count;dst[i]=result;
}


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: crates/nova-gpu/shaders/particle_life.wgsl
========================================================================================
struct Particle {p:vec4<f32>,v:vec4<f32>,phenotype:vec4<u32>,genome:vec4<u32>}
struct Params {counts:vec4<u32>,law:vec4<f32>,extra:vec4<f32>,matrix:array<vec4<f32>,16>}
@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage,read> src:array<Particle>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage,read_write> dst:array<Particle>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage,read> ranges:array<vec2<u32>>;
@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage,read> neighbors:array<u32>;
@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> cfg:Params;
fn vortex(p:vec3<f32>,t:f32)->vec3<f32>{
 return vec3(sin(p.x+.7*t)*cos(p.y)-sin(p.x)*cos(p.z+.3*t),sin(p.y+.9*t)*cos(p.z)-sin(p.y)*cos(p.x+.7*t),sin(p.z+.3*t)*cos(p.x)-sin(p.z)*cos(p.y+.9*t));
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn advance(@builtin(global_invocation_id) gid:vec3<u32>){
 let i=gid.x;if(i>=cfg.counts.x){return;}
 let a=src[i];var force=vec3(0.);let range=ranges[i];
 for(var k=range.x;k<range.y;k++){
  let j=neighbors[k];if(i==j){continue;}
  let b=src[j];let d=b.p.xyz-a.p.xyz;let distance=length(d);
  if(distance>=cfg.law.y){continue;}
  let q=distance/cfg.law.y;let index=a.phenotype.x*8u+b.phenotype.x;
  let affinity=cfg.matrix[index/4u][index%4u];
  var f=affinity*(1.-abs(2.*q-1.-cfg.law.z)/(1.-cfg.law.z));
  if(q<cfg.law.z){f=q/cfg.law.z-1.;}
  var direction=d/max(distance,1e-9);
  if(distance<1e-9){direction=vec3(select(-1.,1.,i<j),0.,0.);}
  force+=direction*f*cfg.law.w;
 }
 var out=a;
 out.v=vec4((a.v.xyz+cfg.law.x*(force+cfg.extra.y*vortex(a.p.xyz,cfg.extra.z))/a.v.w)*exp(-cfg.extra.x*cfg.law.x),a.v.w);
 out.p=vec4(a.p.xyz+cfg.law.x*out.v.xyz,a.p.w);dst[i]=out;
}

//! GPU-resident CA state, bounded dispatch batching and complete living-cell extraction.
use super::Gpu;
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use nova_automata::{Boundary, Grid};
use wgpu::util::DeviceExt;
pub struct GpuAutomaton {
    gpu: Gpu,
    states: [wgpu::Buffer; 2],
    groups: [wgpu::BindGroup; 2],
    pipeline: wgpu::ComputePipeline,
    compact: wgpu::ComputePipeline,
    counters: wgpu::Buffer,
    living: wgpu::Buffer,
    shape: [usize; 3],
    rules: nova_automata::Rules,
    boundary: Boundary,
    current: usize,
    pub tick: u64,
    dispatch: [u32; 2],
    cells: usize,
}
impl GpuAutomaton {
    pub fn new(grid: &Grid) -> Result<Self> {
        grid.validate()?;
        let gpu = Gpu::new()?;
        let n = grid.cells.len();
        let limit = gpu.device.limits();
        ensure!(
            n * 4 <= limit.max_storage_buffer_binding_size as usize
                && (n as u64) * 16 <= 512 * 1024 * 1024,
            "CA GPU memory budget exceeded"
        );
        let max = limit.max_compute_workgroups_per_dimension;
        let gx = (n.div_ceil(64) as u32).min(max);
        let gy = (n.div_ceil(64) as u32).div_ceil(gx);
        ensure!(gy <= max, "CA dispatch exceeds adapter limits");
        let data: Vec<u32> = grid.cells.iter().map(|&x| x as u32).collect();
        let state = |data: &[u32]| {
            gpu.device
                .create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
                    label: Some("native CA state"),
                    contents: bytemuck::cast_slice(data),
                    usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_SRC,
                })
        };
        let states = [state(&data), state(&vec![0; n])];
        let config: [[u32; 4]; 3] = [
            [
                grid.shape[0] as u32,
                grid.shape[1] as u32,
                grid.shape[2] as u32,
                n as u32,
            ],
            [
                grid.rules.birth,
                grid.rules.survival,
                grid.rules.radius,
                (grid.boundary == Boundary::Periodic) as u32,
            ],
            [grid.rules.dimensions, gx * 64, 0, 0],
        ];
        let params = gpu
            .device
            .create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
                label: None,
                contents: bytemuck::cast_slice(&config),
                usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::UNIFORM,
            });
        let counters = gpu.device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
            label: None,
            size: 8,
            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE
                | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST
                | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_SRC,
            mapped_at_creation: false,
        });
        let living = gpu.device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
            label: Some("complete live list, no truncation"),
            size: (n * 4) as u64,
            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_SRC,
            mapped_at_creation: false,
        });
        let entries: Vec<_> = (0..5)
            .map(|i| wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
                binding: i,
                visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages::COMPUTE,
                ty: wgpu::BindingType::Buffer {
                    ty: if i == 2 {
                        wgpu::BufferBindingType::Uniform
                    } else {
                        wgpu::BufferBindingType::Storage { read_only: i == 0 }
                    },
                    has_dynamic_offset: false,
                    min_binding_size: None,
                },
                count: None,
            })
            .collect();
        let layout = gpu
            .device
            .create_bind_group_layout(&wgpu::BindGroupLayoutDescriptor {
                label: None,
                entries: &entries,
            });
        let pl = gpu
            .device
            .create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor {
                label: None,
                bind_group_layouts: &[&layout],
                push_constant_ranges: &[],
            });
        let shader = gpu
            .device
            .create_shader_module(wgpu::ShaderModuleDescriptor {
                label: Some("Conway native integer"),
                source: wgpu::ShaderSource::Wgsl(include_str!("../shaders/automata.wgsl").into()),
            });
        let create = |entry_point| {
            gpu.device
                .create_compute_pipeline(&wgpu::ComputePipelineDescriptor {
                    label: None,
                    layout: Some(&pl),
                    module: &shader,
                    entry_point,
                })
        };
        let pipeline = create("evolve");
        let compact = create("compact");
        let groups = std::array::from_fn(|i| {
            let buffers = [&states[i], &states[1 - i], &params, &counters, &living];
            let entries: Vec<_> = buffers
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .map(|(binding, b)| wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                    binding: binding as u32,
                    resource: b.as_entire_binding(),
                })
                .collect();
            gpu.device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor {
                label: None,
                layout: &layout,
                entries: &entries,
            })
        });
        Ok(Self {
            gpu,
            states,
            groups,
            pipeline,
            compact,
            counters,
            living,
            shape: grid.shape,
            rules: grid.rules,
            boundary: grid.boundary,
            current: 0,
            tick: grid.tick,
            dispatch: [gx, gy],
            cells: n,
        })
    }
    pub fn adapter(&self) -> &str {
        &self.gpu.adapter
    }
    pub fn step(&mut self, ticks: usize) -> Result<()> {
        ensure!(ticks <= 1_000_000, "GPU step budget exceeded");
        let mut remaining = ticks;
        while remaining > 0 {
            let batch = remaining.min(128);
            let mut encoder = self.gpu.device.create_command_encoder(&Default::default());
            for _ in 0..batch {
                encoder.clear_buffer(&self.counters, 0, None);
                {
                    let mut pass = encoder.begin_compute_pass(&wgpu::ComputePassDescriptor {
                        label: None,
                        timestamp_writes: None,
                    });
                    pass.set_pipeline(&self.pipeline);
                    pass.set_bind_group(0, &self.groups[self.current], &[]);
                    pass.dispatch_workgroups(self.dispatch[0], self.dispatch[1], 1);
                }
                self.current = 1 - self.current;
                self.tick += 1;
            }
            self.gpu.queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
            self.gpu.device.poll(wgpu::Maintain::Wait);
            remaining -= batch;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    fn read_words(&self, source: &wgpu::Buffer, words: usize) -> Result<Vec<u32>> {
        if words == 0 {
            return Ok(vec![]);
        }
        let staging = self.gpu.device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
            label: None,
            size: (words * 4) as u64,
            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST | wgpu::BufferUsages::MAP_READ,
            mapped_at_creation: false,
        });
        let mut encoder = self.gpu.device.create_command_encoder(&Default::default());
        encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer(source, 0, &staging, 0, (words * 4) as u64);
        self.gpu.queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
        let slice = staging.slice(..);
        let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
        slice.map_async(wgpu::MapMode::Read, move |x| {
            let _ = tx.send(x);
        });
        self.gpu.device.poll(wgpu::Maintain::Wait);
        rx.recv()??;
        let data = slice
            .get_mapped_range()
            .chunks_exact(4)
            .map(|x| u32::from_le_bytes(x.try_into().unwrap()))
            .collect();
        staging.unmap();
        Ok(data)
    }
    pub fn read(&self) -> Result<Grid> {
        let mut grid = Grid::new(self.shape, self.rules, self.boundary)?;
        grid.cells = self
            .read_words(&self.states[self.current], self.cells)?
            .into_iter()
            .map(|x| x as u8)
            .collect();
        grid.tick = self.tick;
        grid.validate()?;
        Ok(grid)
    }
    pub fn living_cells(&self) -> Result<Vec<[u32; 3]>> {
        let mut encoder = self.gpu.device.create_command_encoder(&Default::default());
        encoder.clear_buffer(&self.counters, 0, None);
        {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_compute_pass(&wgpu::ComputePassDescriptor {
                label: None,
                timestamp_writes: None,
            });
            pass.set_pipeline(&self.compact);
            pass.set_bind_group(0, &self.groups[self.current], &[]);
            pass.dispatch_workgroups(self.dispatch[0], self.dispatch[1], 1);
        }
        self.gpu.queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
        let count = self.read_words(&self.counters, 2)?[0] as usize;
        ensure!(count <= self.cells, "invalid live-cell count");
        Ok(self
            .read_words(&self.living, count)?
            .into_iter()
            .map(|i| {
                [
                    i % self.shape[0] as u32,
                    (i / self.shape[0] as u32) % self.shape[1] as u32,
                    i / (self.shape[0] * self.shape[1]) as u32,
                ]
            })
            .collect())
    }
}

//! Reusable GPU buffers for native fusion. CPU spatial indexing, no neighbor truncation.
use super::Gpu;
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use nova_core::Particle;
use nova_physics::Universe;
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
struct Params {
    counts: [u32; 4],
    domain: [f32; 4],
    dynamics: [f32; 4],
    limits: [f32; 4],
    step: [u32; 4],
    cell: [f32; 4],
    feedback: [f32; 4],
    brush: [f32; 4],
    brush_radius: [f32; 4],
    matrix: [[f32; 4]; 16],
    inner: [[f32; 4]; 16],
    outer: [[f32; 4]; 16],
    masses: [[f32; 4]; 2],
}
pub(crate) struct Buffers {
    capacity: usize,
    neighbors_capacity: usize,
    src: wgpu::Buffer,
    dst: wgpu::Buffer,
    ranges: wgpu::Buffer,
    neighbors: wgpu::Buffer,
    params: wgpu::Buffer,
    readback: wgpu::Buffer,
    bind: wgpu::BindGroup,
}
impl Gpu {
    pub(crate) fn step_fusion(&self, u: &mut Universe) -> Result<()> {
        let model = u
            .fusion
            .as_mut()
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("missing fusion model"))?;
        model.settings.dt = u.config.dt;
        model.settings.validate()?;
        u.particles.validate()?;
        ensure!(
            u.matrix
                .iter()
                .flatten()
                .all(|x| x.is_finite() && x.abs() <= 1.),
            "invalid fusion matrix"
        );
        let n = u.particles.len();
        ensure!(
            n > 0 && model.previous_neighbors.len() == n,
            "invalid fusion membership"
        );
        let brush = model.take_brush();
        let s = &model.settings;
        let grid = nova_fusion::grid::Grid::build(&u.particles, s)?;
        let max = self.device.limits().max_storage_buffer_binding_size as usize;
        ensure!(
            n * 64 <= max
                && n.div_ceil(64)
                    <= self.device.limits().max_compute_workgroups_per_dimension as usize,
            "GPU particle limit exceeded"
        );
        let mut ranges = Vec::with_capacity(n);
        let mut neighbors = Vec::new();
        for p in &u.particles.positions {
            let start = neighbors.len();
            for j in grid.candidates(p.0) {
                ensure!(
                    (neighbors.len() + 1) * 4 <= max,
                    "GPU candidate buffer budget exceeded (no truncation)"
                );
                neighbors.push(j as u32);
            }
            ranges.push([start as u32, neighbors.len() as u32]);
        }
        let mut input: Vec<_> = (0..n).map(|i| u.particles.get(i)).collect();
        ensure!(
            input.iter().all(|p| (p.phenotype[0] as usize) < s.species),
            "particle type exceeds selected species count"
        );
        for (i, p) in input.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            p.vel_mass[3] = s.masses[p.phenotype[0] as usize];
            p.phenotype[2] = model.previous_neighbors[i];
            p.genome_tape[..4].copy_from_slice(&(u.particles.ids[i] as u32).to_le_bytes());
        }
        fn packed(m: &[[f32; 8]; 8]) -> [[f32; 4]; 16] {
            std::array::from_fn(|i| std::array::from_fn(|j| m[(i * 4 + j) / 8][(i * 4 + j) % 8]))
        }
        let p = Params {
            counts: [
                n as u32,
                s.family as u32,
                s.dimensions as u32,
                s.boundary as u32,
            ],
            domain: [s.half_extent[0], s.half_extent[1], s.half_extent[2], s.dt],
            dynamics: [s.force, s.retention, s.repel_strength, s.velocity_coupling],
            limits: [s.max_speed, s.temperature, s.density_limit, 0.],
            step: [u.tick as u32, 0, 0, 0],
            cell: [
                s.cell.repulsion,
                s.cell.attraction,
                s.cell.falloff,
                s.cell.balance,
            ],
            feedback: [s.cell.expected_neighbors, 0., 0., 0.],
            brush: brush
                .map(|(p, _, f)| [p[0], p[1], p[2], f])
                .unwrap_or([0.; 4]),
            brush_radius: brush.map(|(_, r, _)| [r, 0., 0., 0.]).unwrap_or([0.; 4]),
            matrix: packed(&model.varied_matrix(&u.matrix, u.tick)),
            inner: packed(&s.min_radius),
            outer: packed(&s.max_radius),
            masses: std::array::from_fn(|i| std::array::from_fn(|j| s.masses[i * 4 + j])),
        };
        let mut cache = self
            .fusion_buffers
            .lock()
            .map_err(|_| anyhow::anyhow!("GPU buffer cache poisoned"))?;
        let rebuild = cache
            .as_ref()
            .is_none_or(|b| b.capacity < n || b.neighbors_capacity < neighbors.len());
        if rebuild {
            use wgpu::BufferUsages as U;
            let capacity = n.next_power_of_two().min(max / 64).max(n);
            let neighbor_capacity = neighbors
                .len()
                .next_power_of_two()
                .min(max / 4)
                .max(neighbors.len());
            let make = |size: usize, usage| {
                self.device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
                    label: Some("NOVA fusion reusable"),
                    size: size.max(4) as u64,
                    usage,
                    mapped_at_creation: false,
                })
            };
            let src = make(capacity * 64, U::STORAGE | U::COPY_DST);
            let dst = make(capacity * 64, U::STORAGE | U::COPY_SRC);
            let ranges = make(capacity * 8, U::STORAGE | U::COPY_DST);
            let neighbors = make(neighbor_capacity * 4, U::STORAGE | U::COPY_DST);
            let params = make(std::mem::size_of::<Params>(), U::UNIFORM | U::COPY_DST);
            let readback = make(capacity * 64, U::MAP_READ | U::COPY_DST);
            let buffers = [&src, &dst, &ranges, &neighbors, &params];
            let entries: Vec<_> = buffers
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .map(|(i, b)| wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                    binding: i as u32,
                    resource: b.as_entire_binding(),
                })
                .collect();
            let bind = self.device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor {
                label: None,
                layout: &self.fusion_pipeline.get_bind_group_layout(0),
                entries: &entries,
            });
            *cache = Some(Buffers {
                capacity,
                neighbors_capacity: neighbor_capacity,
                src,
                dst,
                ranges,
                neighbors,
                params,
                readback,
                bind,
            });
        }
        let b = cache.as_ref().unwrap();
        self.queue
            .write_buffer(&b.src, 0, bytemuck::cast_slice(&input));
        self.queue
            .write_buffer(&b.ranges, 0, bytemuck::cast_slice(&ranges));
        self.queue
            .write_buffer(&b.neighbors, 0, bytemuck::cast_slice(&neighbors));
        self.queue
            .write_buffer(&b.params, 0, bytemuck::bytes_of(&p));
        let mut encoder = self.device.create_command_encoder(&Default::default());
        {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_compute_pass(&wgpu::ComputePassDescriptor {
                label: Some("native fusion"),
                timestamp_writes: None,
            });
            pass.set_pipeline(&self.fusion_pipeline);
            pass.set_bind_group(0, &b.bind, &[]);
            pass.dispatch_workgroups(n.div_ceil(64) as u32, 1, 1);
        }
        encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer(&b.dst, 0, &b.readback, 0, (n * 64) as u64);
        self.queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
        let slice = b.readback.slice(..(n * 64) as u64);
        let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
        slice.map_async(wgpu::MapMode::Read, move |result| {
            let _ = tx.send(result);
        });
        self.device.poll(wgpu::Maintain::Wait);
        rx.recv()??;
        {
            let mapped = slice.get_mapped_range();
            for (i, bytes) in mapped.chunks_exact(64).enumerate() {
                let p: Particle = bytemuck::pod_read_unaligned(bytes);
                u.particles.positions[i].0 = p.pos_rad;
                u.particles.velocities[i].0 = p.vel_mass;
                model.previous_neighbors[i] = p.phenotype[2];
                model.stats.candidates += (ranges[i][1] - ranges[i][0] - 1) as u64;
                model.stats.interacting_pairs += p.phenotype[2] as u64;
                model.stats.speed_clamps += (p.phenotype[1] & 1) as u64;
                model.stats.density_scaled += ((p.phenotype[1] >> 1) & 1) as u64;
            }
        }
        b.readback.unmap();
        u.particles.validate()?;
        u.tick += 1;
        Ok(())
    }
}

ARQUIVO: crates/nova-gpu/src/lib.rs

pub mod automata;
mod fusion;
// Exact CPU cell candidates + GPU force/integration. Explicit readback per tick.
// No hidden all-pairs fallback and no silently truncated neighbor lists.
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use nova_core::Particle;
use nova_physics::Universe;
use nova_spatial::CellList;
use wgpu::util::DeviceExt;
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
struct Params {
    counts: [u32; 4],
    law: [f32; 4],
    extra: [f32; 4],
    matrix: [[f32; 4]; 16],
}
pub struct Gpu {
    device: wgpu::Device,
    queue: wgpu::Queue,
    pipeline: wgpu::ComputePipeline,
    fusion_pipeline: wgpu::ComputePipeline,
    fusion_buffers: std::sync::Mutex<Option<fusion::Buffers>>,
    pub adapter: String,
}
impl Gpu {
    pub fn new() -> Result<Self> {
        pollster::block_on(Self::create())
    }
    async fn create() -> Result<Self> {
        let instance = wgpu::Instance::default();
        let adapter = instance
            .request_adapter(&wgpu::RequestAdapterOptions {
                power_preference: wgpu::PowerPreference::HighPerformance,
                force_fallback_adapter: false,
                compatible_surface: None,
            })
            .await
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("no native compute adapter"))?;
        let info = adapter.get_info();
        let limits = adapter.limits();
        let (device, queue) = adapter
            .request_device(
                &wgpu::DeviceDescriptor {
                    label: Some("NOVA compute"),
                    required_features: wgpu::Features::empty(),
                    required_limits: limits,
                },
                None,
            )
            .await?;
        let shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::ShaderModuleDescriptor {
            label: Some("Particle Life"),
            source: wgpu::ShaderSource::Wgsl(include_str!("../shaders/particle_life.wgsl").into()),
        });
        let pipeline = device.create_compute_pipeline(&wgpu::ComputePipelineDescriptor {
            label: Some("Particle Life"),
            layout: None,
            module: &shader,
            entry_point: "advance",
        });
        let fusion_shader = device.create_shader_module(wgpu::ShaderModuleDescriptor {
            label: Some("NOVA upstream fusion"),
            source: wgpu::ShaderSource::Wgsl(include_str!("../shaders/fusion.wgsl").into()),
        });
        let fusion_pipeline = device.create_compute_pipeline(&wgpu::ComputePipelineDescriptor {
            label: None,
            layout: None,
            module: &fusion_shader,
            entry_point: "advance",
        });
        Ok(Self {
            fusion_pipeline,
            fusion_buffers: std::sync::Mutex::new(None),
            device,
            queue,
            pipeline,
            adapter: info.name,
        })
    }
    pub fn step(&self, u: &mut Universe) -> Result<()> {
        if u.fusion.is_some() {
            return self.step_fusion(u);
        }
        u.config.validate()?;
        ensure!(
            !u.config.pvm && !u.config.crossover && !u.config.mpm,
            "GPU backend supports canonical Particle Life; use CPU for PVM/crossover/MPM"
        );
        ensure!(
            u.matrix
                .iter()
                .flatten()
                .all(|x| x.is_finite() && x.abs() <= 1.),
            "invalid affinity matrix"
        );
        let cells = CellList::rebuild(&mut u.particles, u.config.cutoff)?;
        let n = u.particles.len();
        ensure!(
            n > 0 && n <= u32::MAX as usize,
            "invalid GPU particle count"
        );
        let max = self.device.limits().max_storage_buffer_binding_size as usize;
        ensure!(
            n * 64 <= max
                && n * 8 <= max
                && n.div_ceil(64)
                    <= self.device.limits().max_compute_workgroups_per_dimension as usize,
            "GPU particle budget exceeds adapter limits"
        );
        let mut ranges = Vec::with_capacity(n);
        let mut neighbors = Vec::<u32>::new();
        for p in &u.particles.positions {
            let begin = neighbors.len();
            for j in cells.candidates(p.0) {
                ensure!(
                    (neighbors.len() + 1) * 4 <= max,
                    "GPU neighbor buffer exhausted; reduce density/particles (no truncation)"
                );
                neighbors.push(j as u32);
            }
            ranges.push([begin as u32, neighbors.len() as u32]);
        }
        let input: Vec<_> = (0..n).map(|i| u.particles.get(i)).collect();
        let make = |label, data: &[u8], usage| {
            self.device
                .create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
                    label: Some(label),
                    contents: data,
                    usage,
                })
        };
        let src = make(
            "particles",
            bytemuck::cast_slice(&input),
            wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE,
        );
        let dst = self.device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
            label: Some("output"),
            size: (n * 64) as u64,
            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_SRC,
            mapped_at_creation: false,
        });
        let ranges = make(
            "ranges",
            bytemuck::cast_slice(&ranges),
            wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE,
        );
        let neighbors = make(
            "neighbors",
            bytemuck::cast_slice(&neighbors),
            wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE,
        );
        let c = &u.config;
        let mut matrix = [[0.; 4]; 16];
        for i in 0..64 {
            matrix[i / 4][i % 4] = u.matrix[i / 8][i % 8];
        }
        let params = Params {
            counts: [n as u32, 0, 0, 0],
            law: [c.dt, c.cutoff, c.beta, c.strength],
            extra: [c.damping, c.curl, u.tick as f32 * c.dt, 0.],
            matrix,
        };
        let uniform = make(
            "config",
            bytemuck::bytes_of(&params),
            wgpu::BufferUsages::UNIFORM,
        );
        let buffers = [&src, &dst, &ranges, &neighbors, &uniform];
        let entries: Vec<_> = buffers
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, b)| wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                binding: i as u32,
                resource: b.as_entire_binding(),
            })
            .collect();
        let group = self.device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor {
            label: None,
            layout: &self.pipeline.get_bind_group_layout(0),
            entries: &entries,
        });
        let readback = self.device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
            label: None,
            size: (n * 64) as u64,
            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::MAP_READ | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST,
            mapped_at_creation: false,
        });
        let mut encoder = self.device.create_command_encoder(&Default::default());
        {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_compute_pass(&wgpu::ComputePassDescriptor {
                label: None,
                timestamp_writes: None,
            });
            pass.set_pipeline(&self.pipeline);
            pass.set_bind_group(0, &group, &[]);
            pass.dispatch_workgroups(n.div_ceil(64) as u32, 1, 1);
        }
        encoder.copy_buffer_to_buffer(&dst, 0, &readback, 0, (n * 64) as u64);
        self.queue.submit(Some(encoder.finish()));
        let slice = readback.slice(..);
        let (tx, rx) = std::sync::mpsc::channel();
        slice.map_async(wgpu::MapMode::Read, move |r| {
            let _ = tx.send(r);
        });
        self.device.poll(wgpu::Maintain::Wait);
        rx.recv()??;
        {
            let mapped = slice.get_mapped_range();
            for (i, bytes) in mapped.chunks_exact(64).enumerate() {
                let p: Particle = bytemuck::pod_read_unaligned(bytes);
                u.particles.positions[i].0 = p.pos_rad;
                u.particles.velocities[i].0 = p.vel_mass;
            }
        }
        readback.unmap();
        u.particles.validate()?;
        u.tick += 1;
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[path = "conway_simd.rs"]
mod simd;
// Periodic, exact B/S automata. 2D uses 64 cells per word and bit-sliced counts.
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use nova_core::SplitMix64;
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Rule {
    pub birth: u32,
    pub survive: u32,
}
impl Rule {
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        let parts: Vec<_> = s.split('/').collect();
        ensure!(
            parts.len() == 2 && parts[0].starts_with('B') && parts[1].starts_with('S'),
            "expected B3/S23 or B6/S5,6,7"
        );
        fn mask(s: &str) -> Result<u32> {
            let values: Vec<u32> = if s.contains(',') {
                s.split(',')
                    .map(str::parse)
                    .collect::<std::result::Result<_, _>>()?
            } else {
                s.chars()
                    .map(|c| {
                        c.to_digit(10)
                            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("invalid rule"))
                    })
                    .collect::<Result<_>>()?
            };
            ensure!(values.iter().all(|&x| x <= 26), "neighbor count exceeds 26");
            Ok(values.into_iter().fold(0, |a, x| a | (1 << x)))
        }
        Ok(Self {
            birth: mask(&parts[0][1..])?,
            survive: mask(&parts[1][1..])?,
        })
    }
}
pub struct Conway {
    pub width: usize,
    pub height: usize,
    pub words: Vec<u64>,
    pub rule: Rule,
    pub tick: u64,
}
impl Conway {
    pub fn new(width: usize, height: usize, rule: Rule, seed: u64) -> Result<Self> {
        ensure!(
            width >= 64
                && width % 64 == 0
                && height >= 3
                && width.checked_mul(height).is_some_and(|n| n <= 1 << 30),
            "grid needs width multiple of 64, height >=3, <=2^30 cells"
        );
        ensure!(
            rule.birth < 512 && rule.survive < 512,
            "2D counts must be <=8"
        );
        let mut rng = SplitMix64(seed);
        let words = (0..width / 64 * height)
            .map(|_| rng.next_u64() & rng.next_u64())
            .collect();
        Ok(Self {
            width,
            height,
            words,
            rule,
            tick: 0,
        })
    }
    pub fn step(&mut self) {
        use rayon::prelude::*;
        let stride = self.width / 64;
        let rows = self.height;
        let old = &self.words;
        let rule = self.rule;
        let mut next = vec![0; old.len()];
        next.par_chunks_mut(stride)
            .enumerate()
            .for_each(|(y, row)| {
                for (block, chunk) in row.chunks_mut(4).enumerate() {
                    let mut neighbors = [[0u64; 4]; 8];
                    let mut current = [0u64; 4];
                    for lane in 0..chunk.len() {
                        let x = block * 4 + lane;
                        current[lane] = old[y * stride + x];
                        let mut n = 0;
                        for yy in [(y + rows - 1) % rows, y, (y + 1) % rows] {
                            let center = old[yy * stride + x];
                            neighbors[n][lane] = (center << 1)
                                | (old[yy * stride + (x + stride - 1) % stride] >> 63);
                            n += 1;
                            if yy != y {
                                neighbors[n][lane] = center;
                                n += 1;
                            }
                            neighbors[n][lane] =
                                (center >> 1) | (old[yy * stride + (x + 1) % stride] << 63);
                            n += 1;
                        }
                    }
                    let result = simd::transition(neighbors, current, rule.birth, rule.survive);
                    chunk.copy_from_slice(&result[..chunk.len()]);
                }
            });
        self.words = next;
        self.tick += 1;
    }
    pub fn population(&self) -> u64 {
        self.words.iter().map(|x| x.count_ones() as u64).sum()
    }
}
/// Separate integer Moore-26 implementation; this is not the 2D Conway rule.
pub fn step_3d(old: &[u8], shape: [usize; 3], rule: Rule) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
    ensure!(
        shape.iter().all(|&n| n >= 3)
            && shape.iter().try_fold(1usize, |a, &b| a.checked_mul(b)) == Some(old.len())
            && old.iter().all(|&x| x <= 1),
        "invalid 3D boolean grid"
    );
    let [nx, ny, nz] = shape;
    let mut new = vec![0; old.len()];
    for x in 0..nx {
        for y in 0..ny {
            for z in 0..nz {
                let mut count = 0;
                for dx in [-1isize, 0, 1] {
                    for dy in [-1isize, 0, 1] {
                        for dz in [-1isize, 0, 1] {
                            if dx == 0 && dy == 0 && dz == 0 {
                                continue;
                            }
                            let ix = (x as isize + dx).rem_euclid(nx as isize) as usize;
                            let iy = (y as isize + dy).rem_euclid(ny as isize) as usize;
                            let iz = (z as isize + dz).rem_euclid(nz as isize) as usize;
                            count += old[(ix * ny + iy) * nz + iz] as u32;
                        }
                    }
                }
                let i = (x * ny + y) * nz + z;
                new[i] = (((if old[i] == 1 {
                    rule.survive
                } else {
                    rule.birth
                }) >> count)
                    & 1) as u8;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(new)
}

ARQUIVO: crates/nova-physics/src/conway_simd.rs

//! Runtime-dispatched AVX2 / baseline AArch64 NEON, scalar fallback everywhere else.
pub fn transition(
    neighbors: [[u64; 4]; 8],
    current: [u64; 4],
    birth: u32,
    survive: u32,
) -> [u64; 4] {
    #[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
    if std::arch::is_x86_feature_detected!("avx2") {
        // SAFETY: feature detection precedes the target-feature call; arrays have 32 readable bytes.
        return unsafe { avx2(neighbors, current, birth, survive) };
    }
    #[cfg(target_arch = "aarch64")]
    {
        return unsafe { neon(neighbors, current, birth, survive) };
    }
    #[allow(unreachable_code)]
    scalar(neighbors, current, birth, survive)
}
fn scalar(neighbors: [[u64; 4]; 8], current: [u64; 4], birth: u32, survive: u32) -> [u64; 4] {
    let mut out = [0; 4];
    for lane in 0..4 {
        let mut bits = [0; 4];
        for neighbor in neighbors {
            let mut carry = neighbor[lane];
            for bit in &mut bits {
                let c = *bit & carry;
                *bit ^= carry;
                carry = c;
            }
        }
        let mut born = 0;
        let mut live = 0;
        for n in 0..=8 {
            let mut eq = !0;
            for (k, &bit) in bits.iter().enumerate() {
                eq &= if n & (1 << k) != 0 { bit } else { !bit };
            }
            if birth & (1 << n) != 0 {
                born |= eq;
            }
            if survive & (1 << n) != 0 {
                live |= eq;
            }
        }
        out[lane] = (born & !current[lane]) | (live & current[lane]);
    }
    out
}
#[cfg(target_arch = "x86_64")]
#[target_feature(enable = "avx2")]
unsafe fn avx2(neighbors: [[u64; 4]; 8], current: [u64; 4], birth: u32, survive: u32) -> [u64; 4] {
    use std::arch::x86_64::*;
    let zero = _mm256_setzero_si256();
    let ones = _mm256_set1_epi64x(-1);
    let mut bits = [zero; 4];
    for row in &neighbors {
        let mut carry = unsafe { _mm256_loadu_si256(row.as_ptr().cast()) };
        for bit in &mut bits {
            let c = _mm256_and_si256(*bit, carry);
            *bit = _mm256_xor_si256(*bit, carry);
            carry = c;
        }
    }
    let mut born = zero;
    let mut live = zero;
    for n in 0..=8 {
        let mut eq = ones;
        for (k, &bit) in bits.iter().enumerate() {
            eq = _mm256_and_si256(
                eq,
                if n & (1 << k) != 0 {
                    bit
                } else {
                    _mm256_xor_si256(bit, ones)
                },
            );
        }
        if birth & (1 << n) != 0 {
            born = _mm256_or_si256(born, eq);
        }
        if survive & (1 << n) != 0 {
            live = _mm256_or_si256(live, eq);
        }
    }
    let c = unsafe { _mm256_loadu_si256(current.as_ptr().cast()) };
    let result = _mm256_or_si256(_mm256_andnot_si256(c, born), _mm256_and_si256(c, live));
    let mut out = [0u64; 4];
    unsafe {
        _mm256_storeu_si256(out.as_mut_ptr().cast(), result);
    }
    out
}
#[cfg(target_arch = "aarch64")]
#[target_feature(enable = "neon")]
unsafe fn neon(neighbors: [[u64; 4]; 8], current: [u64; 4], birth: u32, survive: u32) -> [u64; 4] {
    use std::arch::aarch64::*;
    let mut out = [0; 4];
    for offset in [0, 2] {
        let zero = vdupq_n_u64(0);
        let ones = vdupq_n_u64(u64::MAX);
        let mut bits = [zero; 4];
        for row in &neighbors {
            let mut carry = unsafe { vld1q_u64(row.as_ptr().add(offset)) };
            for bit in &mut bits {
                let c = vandq_u64(*bit, carry);
                *bit = veorq_u64(*bit, carry);
                carry = c;
            }
        }
        let mut born = zero;
        let mut live = zero;
        for n in 0..=8 {
            let mut eq = ones;
            for (k, &bit) in bits.iter().enumerate() {
                eq = vandq_u64(
                    eq,
                    if n & (1 << k) != 0 {
                        bit
                    } else {
                        veorq_u64(bit, ones)
                    },
                );
            }
            if birth & (1 << n) != 0 {
                born = vorrq_u64(born, eq);
            }
            if survive & (1 << n) != 0 {
                live = vorrq_u64(live, eq);
            }
        }
        let c = unsafe { vld1q_u64(current.as_ptr().add(offset)) };
        let result = vorrq_u64(vbicq_u64(born, c), vandq_u64(live, c));
        unsafe {
            vst1q_u64(out.as_mut_ptr().add(offset), result);
        }
    }
    out
}

ARQUIVO: crates/nova-physics/src/lib.rs
