ARQUIVO: crates/nova-render/src/lib.rs
========================================================================================
mod controls;
// Native winit/wgpu fluid surface renderer; all shader assets embedded.
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use nova_physics::Universe;
use nova_storage::Lake;
use std::{
    cell::RefCell,
    path::PathBuf,
    rc::Rc,
    sync::Arc,
    time::{Duration, Instant},
};
use wgpu::util::DeviceExt;
use winit::{
    event::{ElementState, Event, MouseScrollDelta, WindowEvent},
    event_loop::{ControlFlow, EventLoop},
    keyboard::{KeyCode, PhysicalKey},
    window::{Window, WindowBuilder},
};
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
struct RenderParticle {
    p: [f32; 4],
    v: [f32; 4],
    previous: [f32; 4],
}
#[repr(C)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, bytemuck::Pod, bytemuck::Zeroable)]
struct Filter {
    axis: [i32; 4],
    values: [f32; 4],
}
struct Targets {
    depth: Vec<wgpu::TextureView>,
    pigment: Vec<wgpu::TextureView>,
    hardware: wgpu::TextureView,
    hdr: wgpu::TextureView,
    filters: Vec<wgpu::BindGroup>,
    lighting: wgpu::BindGroup,
    post: wgpu::BindGroup,
}
struct Renderer {
    ui: controls::Panel,
    window: Arc<Window>,
    surface: wgpu::Surface<'static>,
    device: wgpu::Device,
    queue: wgpu::Queue,
    config: wgpu::SurfaceConfiguration,
    particles: wgpu::Buffer,
    camera: wgpu::Buffer,
    post_config: wgpu::Buffer,
    _palette: wgpu::Buffer,
    sampler: wgpu::Sampler,
    filter_configs: [wgpu::Buffer; 2],
    depth_pipeline: wgpu::RenderPipeline,
    pigment_pipeline: wgpu::RenderPipeline,
    filter_pipeline: wgpu::ComputePipeline,
    light_pipeline: wgpu::RenderPipeline,
    post_pipeline: wgpu::RenderPipeline,
    splats: wgpu::BindGroup,
    targets: Option<Targets>,
    yaw: f32,
    pitch: f32,
    distance: f32,
    scene_scale: f32,
    capacity: usize,
}
fn buffer(device: &wgpu::Device, data: &[u8], usage: wgpu::BufferUsages) -> wgpu::Buffer {
    device.create_buffer_init(&wgpu::util::BufferInitDescriptor {
        label: None,
        contents: data,
        usage,
    })
}
fn entry(
    binding: u32,
    ty: wgpu::BindingType,
    visibility: wgpu::ShaderStages,
) -> wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
    wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry {
        binding,
        visibility,
        ty,
        count: None,
    }
}
fn uniform() -> wgpu::BindingType {
    wgpu::BindingType::Buffer {
        ty: wgpu::BufferBindingType::Uniform,
        has_dynamic_offset: false,
        min_binding_size: None,
    }
}
fn texture(filterable: bool) -> wgpu::BindingType {
    wgpu::BindingType::Texture {
        sample_type: wgpu::TextureSampleType::Float { filterable },
        view_dimension: wgpu::TextureViewDimension::D2,
        multisampled: false,
    }
}
fn layout(device: &wgpu::Device, entries: &[wgpu::BindGroupLayoutEntry]) -> wgpu::BindGroupLayout {
    device.create_bind_group_layout(&wgpu::BindGroupLayoutDescriptor {
        label: None,
        entries,
    })
}
fn group(
    device: &wgpu::Device,
    layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout,
    resources: &[wgpu::BindingResource<'_>],
) -> wgpu::BindGroup {
    device.create_bind_group(&wgpu::BindGroupDescriptor {
        label: None,
        layout,
        entries: &resources
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, r)| wgpu::BindGroupEntry {
                binding: i as u32,
                resource: r.clone(),
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
    })
}
fn render_pipeline(
    device: &wgpu::Device,
    shader: &wgpu::ShaderModule,
    layout: &wgpu::BindGroupLayout,
    vertex: &str,
    fragment: &str,
    format: wgpu::TextureFormat,
    blend: Option<wgpu::BlendState>,
    depth: bool,
) -> wgpu::RenderPipeline {
    let pipeline_layout = device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor {
        label: None,
        bind_group_layouts: &[layout],
        push_constant_ranges: &[],
    });
    device.create_render_pipeline(&wgpu::RenderPipelineDescriptor {
        label: None,
        layout: Some(&pipeline_layout),
        vertex: wgpu::VertexState {
            module: shader,
            entry_point: vertex,
            buffers: &[],
        },
        fragment: Some(wgpu::FragmentState {
            module: shader,
            entry_point: fragment,
            targets: &[Some(wgpu::ColorTargetState {
                format,
                blend,
                write_mask: wgpu::ColorWrites::ALL,
            })],
        }),
        primitive: wgpu::PrimitiveState::default(),
        depth_stencil: depth.then_some(wgpu::DepthStencilState {
            format: wgpu::TextureFormat::Depth32Float,
            depth_write_enabled: true,
            depth_compare: wgpu::CompareFunction::Less,
            stencil: Default::default(),
            bias: Default::default(),
        }),
        multisample: Default::default(),
        multiview: None,
    })
}
impl Renderer {
    async fn new(window: Arc<Window>, capacity: usize) -> Result<Self> {
        let instance = wgpu::Instance::default();
        let surface = instance.create_surface(window.clone())?;
        let adapter = instance
            .request_adapter(&wgpu::RequestAdapterOptions {
                power_preference: wgpu::PowerPreference::HighPerformance,
                compatible_surface: Some(&surface),
                force_fallback_adapter: false,
            })
            .await
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("no graphics adapter for native window"))?;
        ensure!(
            capacity * std::mem::size_of::<RenderParticle>()
                <= adapter.limits().max_storage_buffer_binding_size as usize,
            "particle render buffer exceeds adapter capacity"
        );
        let (device, queue) = adapter
            .request_device(
                &wgpu::DeviceDescriptor {
                    label: Some("NOVA liquid"),
                    required_features: wgpu::Features::empty(),
                    required_limits: adapter.limits(),
                },
                None,
            )
            .await?;
        let size = window.inner_size();
        let caps = surface.get_capabilities(&adapter);
        let format = caps
            .formats
            .iter()
            .copied()
            .find(|f| !f.is_srgb())
            .unwrap_or(caps.formats[0]);
        let config = wgpu::SurfaceConfiguration {
            usage: wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT,
            format,
            width: size.width.max(1),
            height: size.height.max(1),
            present_mode: wgpu::PresentMode::Fifo,
            alpha_mode: caps.alpha_modes[0],
            view_formats: vec![],
            desired_maximum_frame_latency: 2,
        };
        surface.configure(&device, &config);
        let camera = buffer(
            &device,
            &[0u8; 128],
            wgpu::BufferUsages::UNIFORM | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST,
        );
        let post_config = buffer(
            &device,
            &[0u8; 48],
            wgpu::BufferUsages::UNIFORM | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST,
        );
        let colors: [[f32; 4]; 8] = [
            [0.03, 0.5, 0.9, 1.],
            [0.8, 0.05, 0.3, 1.],
            [0.4, 0.05, 0.9, 1.],
            [0.02, 0.8, 0.65, 1.],
            [0.9, 0.45, 0.03, 1.],
            [0.1, 0.2, 0.9, 1.],
            [0.9, 0.1, 0.6, 1.],
            [0.5, 0.9, 0.1, 1.],
        ];
        let palette = buffer(
            &device,
            bytemuck::cast_slice(&colors),
            wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST,
        );
        let particles = device.create_buffer(&wgpu::BufferDescriptor {
            label: Some("presentation particles"),
            size: (capacity * 48) as u64,
            usage: wgpu::BufferUsages::STORAGE | wgpu::BufferUsages::COPY_DST,
            mapped_at_creation: false,
        });
        let shader = |name, source: &'static str| {
            device.create_shader_module(wgpu::ShaderModuleDescriptor {
                label: Some(name),
                source: wgpu::ShaderSource::Wgsl(source.into()),
            })
        };
        let splat_shader = shader(
            "sphere depth",
            include_str!("../shaders/fluid_surface.wgsl"),
        );
        let filter_shader = shader("bilateral", include_str!("../shaders/fluid_filter.wgsl"));
        let light_shader = shader(
            "liquid optics",
            include_str!("../shaders/fluid_lighting.wgsl"),
        );
        let post_shader = shader("post", include_str!("../shaders/fluid_post.wgsl"));
        use wgpu::ShaderStages as S;
        let storage = wgpu::BindingType::Buffer {
            ty: wgpu::BufferBindingType::Storage { read_only: true },
            has_dynamic_offset: false,
            min_binding_size: None,
        };
        let splat_layout = layout(
            &device,
            &[
                entry(0, storage, S::VERTEX),
                entry(1, uniform(), S::VERTEX_FRAGMENT),
                entry(2, storage, S::VERTEX),
            ],
        );
        let depth_pipeline = render_pipeline(
            &device,
            &splat_shader,
            &splat_layout,
            "splat",
            "sphere_depth",
            wgpu::TextureFormat::R32Float,
            None,
            true,
        );
        let additive = wgpu::BlendComponent {
            src_factor: wgpu::BlendFactor::One,
            dst_factor: wgpu::BlendFactor::One,
            operation: wgpu::BlendOperation::Add,
        };
        let pigment_pipeline = render_pipeline(
            &device,
            &splat_shader,
            &splat_layout,
            "splat",
            "optical_thickness",
            wgpu::TextureFormat::Rgba16Float,
            Some(wgpu::BlendState {
                color: additive,
                alpha: additive,
            }),
            false,
        );
        let splats = group(
            &device,
            &splat_layout,
            &[
                particles.as_entire_binding(),
                camera.as_entire_binding(),
                palette.as_entire_binding(),
            ],
        );
        let output = |format| wgpu::BindingType::StorageTexture {
            access: wgpu::StorageTextureAccess::WriteOnly,
            format,
            view_dimension: wgpu::TextureViewDimension::D2,
        };
        let filter_layout = layout(
            &device,
            &[
                entry(0, texture(false), S::COMPUTE),
                entry(1, texture(false), S::COMPUTE),
                entry(2, output(wgpu::TextureFormat::R32Float), S::COMPUTE),
                entry(3, output(wgpu::TextureFormat::Rgba16Float), S::COMPUTE),
                entry(4, uniform(), S::COMPUTE),
            ],
        );
        let pl = device.create_pipeline_layout(&wgpu::PipelineLayoutDescriptor {
            label: None,
            bind_group_layouts: &[&filter_layout],
            push_constant_ranges: &[],
        });
        let filter_pipeline = device.create_compute_pipeline(&wgpu::ComputePipelineDescriptor {
            label: None,
            layout: Some(&pl),
            module: &filter_shader,
            entry_point: "bilateral_main",
        });
        let light_layout = layout(
            &device,
            &[
                entry(0, texture(false), S::FRAGMENT),
                entry(1, texture(false), S::FRAGMENT),
                entry(2, uniform(), S::FRAGMENT),
            ],
        );
        let light_pipeline = render_pipeline(
            &device,
            &light_shader,
            &light_layout,
            "fullscreen",
            "shade",
            wgpu::TextureFormat::Rgba16Float,
            None,
            false,
        );
        let post_layout = layout(
            &device,
            &[
                entry(0, texture(true), S::FRAGMENT),
                entry(1, texture(false), S::FRAGMENT),
                entry(
                    2,
                    wgpu::BindingType::Sampler(wgpu::SamplerBindingType::Filtering),
                    S::FRAGMENT,
                ),
                entry(3, uniform(), S::FRAGMENT),
            ],
        );
        let post_pipeline = render_pipeline(
            &device,
            &post_shader,
            &post_layout,
            "fullscreen",
            "post",
            format,
            None,
            false,
        );
        let sampler = device.create_sampler(&wgpu::SamplerDescriptor {
            mag_filter: wgpu::FilterMode::Linear,
            min_filter: wgpu::FilterMode::Linear,
            ..Default::default()
        });
        let filter_configs = [[1, 0, 5, 0], [0, 1, 5, 0]].map(|axis| {
            buffer(
                &device,
                bytemuck::bytes_of(&Filter {
                    axis,
                    values: [9., 0.01, 0., 0.],
                }),
                wgpu::BufferUsages::UNIFORM,
            )
        });
        let ui = controls::Panel::new(&window, &device, format);
        let mut s = Self {
            ui,
            window,
            surface,
            device,
            queue,
            config,
            particles,
            camera,
            post_config,
            _palette: palette,
            sampler,
            filter_configs,
            depth_pipeline,
            pigment_pipeline,
            filter_pipeline,
            light_pipeline,
            post_pipeline,
            splats,
            targets: None,
            yaw: 0.,
            pitch: 0.15,
            distance: 5.,
            scene_scale: 10.,
            capacity,
        };
        s.resize(size.width, size.height);
        Ok(s)
    }
    fn resize(&mut self, width: u32, height: u32) {
        if width == 0 || height == 0 {
            return;
        }
        self.config.width = width;
        self.config.height = height;
        self.surface.configure(&self.device, &self.config);
        let tex = |format, usage| {
            self.device
                .create_texture(&wgpu::TextureDescriptor {
                    label: None,
                    size: wgpu::Extent3d {
                        width,
                        height,
                        depth_or_array_layers: 1,
                    },
                    mip_level_count: 1,
                    sample_count: 1,
                    dimension: wgpu::TextureDimension::D2,
                    format,
                    usage,
                    view_formats: &[],
                })
                .create_view(&Default::default())
        };
        let usage = wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT
            | wgpu::TextureUsages::TEXTURE_BINDING
            | wgpu::TextureUsages::STORAGE_BINDING;
        let depth: Vec<_> = (0..3)
            .map(|_| tex(wgpu::TextureFormat::R32Float, usage))
            .collect();
        let pigment: Vec<_> = (0..3)
            .map(|_| tex(wgpu::TextureFormat::Rgba16Float, usage))
            .collect();
        let hardware = tex(
            wgpu::TextureFormat::Depth32Float,
            wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT,
        );
        let hdr = tex(
            wgpu::TextureFormat::Rgba16Float,
            wgpu::TextureUsages::RENDER_ATTACHMENT | wgpu::TextureUsages::TEXTURE_BINDING,
        );
        let filters = (0..2)
            .map(|i| {
                group(
                    &self.device,
                    &self.filter_pipeline.get_bind_group_layout(0),
                    &[
                        wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(&depth[i]),
                        wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(&pigment[i]),
                        wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(&depth[i + 1]),
                        wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(&pigment[i + 1]),
                        self.filter_configs[i].as_entire_binding(),
                    ],
                )
            })
            .collect();
        let lighting = group(
            &self.device,
            &self.light_pipeline.get_bind_group_layout(0),
            &[
                wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(&depth[2]),
                wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(&pigment[2]),
                self.camera.as_entire_binding(),
            ],
        );
        let post = group(
            &self.device,
            &self.post_pipeline.get_bind_group_layout(0),
            &[
                wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(&hdr),
                wgpu::BindingResource::TextureView(&depth[2]),
                wgpu::BindingResource::Sampler(&self.sampler),
                self.post_config.as_entire_binding(),
            ],
        );
        self.targets = Some(Targets {
            depth,
            pigment,
            hardware,
            hdr,
            filters,
            lighting,
            post,
        });
    }
    fn draw(&mut self, u: &mut Universe, paused: &mut bool) -> Result<()> {
        let output_ui = self.ui.frame(&self.window, u, paused);
        if let Some(model) = &u.fusion {
            self.scene_scale = model.settings.half_extent.into_iter().fold(0., f32::max);
            self.queue
                .write_buffer(&self._palette, 0, bytemuck::cast_slice(&model.palette));
        }
        ensure!(
            u.particles.len() <= self.capacity,
            "renderer particle capacity exceeded"
        );
        let rows: Vec<_> = (0..u.particles.len())
            .map(|i| {
                let p = u.particles.get(i);
                RenderParticle {
                    p: [
                        p.pos_rad[0] / self.scene_scale,
                        p.pos_rad[1] / self.scene_scale,
                        p.pos_rad[2] / self.scene_scale,
                        p.phenotype[0] as f32,
                    ],
                    v: p.vel_mass,
                    previous: [0.; 4],
                }
            })
            .collect();
        self.queue
            .write_buffer(&self.particles, 0, bytemuck::cast_slice(&rows));
        let f = [
            -self.yaw.sin() * self.pitch.cos(),
            -self.pitch.sin(),
            -self.yaw.cos() * self.pitch.cos(),
        ];
        let right = [self.yaw.cos(), 0., -self.yaw.sin()];
        let up = [
            -self.yaw.sin() * self.pitch.sin(),
            self.pitch.cos(),
            -self.yaw.cos() * self.pitch.sin(),
        ];
        let camera: [[f32; 4]; 8] = [
            [
                -f[0] * self.distance,
                -f[1] * self.distance,
                -f[2] * self.distance,
                0.,
            ],
            [right[0], right[1], right[2], 0.],
            [up[0], up[1], up[2], 0.],
            [f[0], f[1], f[2], 0.5],
            [
                self.config.width as f32,
                self.config.height as f32,
                self.ui.presentation.radius,
                0.,
            ],
            [self.ui.presentation.roughness, 4., 0.2, 450.],
            [0., 0., 0.25, 0.25],
            [0.; 4],
        ];
        self.queue
            .write_buffer(&self.camera, 0, bytemuck::cast_slice(&camera));
        let post: [[f32; 4]; 3] = [
            [
                self.config.width as f32,
                self.config.height as f32,
                u.tick as f32,
                0.,
            ],
            [self.distance, 0.8, 0.15, self.ui.presentation.exposure],
            [
                0.25,
                0.002,
                if self.config.format.is_srgb() { 1. } else { 0. },
                0.,
            ],
        ];
        self.queue
            .write_buffer(&self.post_config, 0, bytemuck::cast_slice(&post));
        let output = match self.surface.get_current_texture() {
            Ok(x) => x,
            Err(wgpu::SurfaceError::Lost | wgpu::SurfaceError::Outdated) => {
                self.surface.configure(&self.device, &self.config);
                return Ok(());
            }
            Err(wgpu::SurfaceError::Timeout) => return Ok(()),
            Err(e) => return Err(e.into()),
        };
        let view = output.texture.create_view(&Default::default());
        let t = self.targets.as_ref().unwrap();
        let mut encoder = self.device.create_command_encoder(&Default::default());
        fn render(
            encoder: &mut wgpu::CommandEncoder,
            target: &wgpu::TextureView,
            clear: wgpu::Color,
            hardware: Option<&wgpu::TextureView>,
            pipeline: &wgpu::RenderPipeline,
            bind: &wgpu::BindGroup,
            vertices: u32,
            instances: u32,
        ) {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor {
                label: None,
                color_attachments: &[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment {
                    view: target,
                    resolve_target: None,
                    ops: wgpu::Operations {
                        load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(clear),
                        store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                    },
                })],
                depth_stencil_attachment: hardware.map(|view| {
                    wgpu::RenderPassDepthStencilAttachment {
                        view,
                        depth_ops: Some(wgpu::Operations {
                            load: wgpu::LoadOp::Clear(1.),
                            store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                        }),
                        stencil_ops: None,
                    }
                }),
                timestamp_writes: None,
                occlusion_query_set: None,
            });
            pass.set_pipeline(pipeline);
            pass.set_bind_group(0, bind, &[]);
            pass.draw(0..vertices, 0..instances);
        }
        render(
            &mut encoder,
            &t.depth[0],
            wgpu::Color {
                r: 10.,
                g: 0.,
                b: 0.,
                a: 0.,
            },
            Some(&t.hardware),
            &self.depth_pipeline,
            &self.splats,
            6,
            rows.len() as u32,
        );
        render(
            &mut encoder,
            &t.pigment[0],
            wgpu::Color::TRANSPARENT,
            None,
            &self.pigment_pipeline,
            &self.splats,
            6,
            rows.len() as u32,
        );
        for bind in &t.filters {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_compute_pass(&wgpu::ComputePassDescriptor {
                label: Some("bilateral"),
                timestamp_writes: None,
            });
            pass.set_pipeline(&self.filter_pipeline);
            pass.set_bind_group(0, bind, &[]);
            pass.dispatch_workgroups(
                self.config.width.div_ceil(8),
                self.config.height.div_ceil(8),
                1,
            );
        }
        render(
            &mut encoder,
            &t.hdr,
            wgpu::Color::BLACK,
            None,
            &self.light_pipeline,
            &t.lighting,
            3,
            1,
        );
        render(
            &mut encoder,
            &view,
            wgpu::Color::BLACK,
            None,
            &self.post_pipeline,
            &t.post,
            3,
            1,
        );
        let prepared = self.ui.prepare(
            &self.device,
            &self.queue,
            &mut encoder,
            output_ui,
            [self.config.width, self.config.height],
        );
        {
            let mut pass = encoder.begin_render_pass(&wgpu::RenderPassDescriptor {
                label: Some("NOVA native inspector"),
                color_attachments: &[Some(wgpu::RenderPassColorAttachment {
                    view: &view,
                    resolve_target: None,
                    ops: wgpu::Operations {
                        load: wgpu::LoadOp::Load,
                        store: wgpu::StoreOp::Store,
                    },
                })],
                depth_stencil_attachment: None,
                timestamp_writes: None,
                occlusion_query_set: None,
            });
            self.ui.paint(&mut pass, &prepared);
        }
        self.ui.finish(&prepared);
        self.queue
            .submit(prepared.commands.into_iter().chain([encoder.finish()]));
        output.present();
        Ok(())
    }
}
/// Space pauses, arrows orbit, wheel zooms, E injects a jet, Escape exits.
pub fn run(mut universe: Universe, gpu: bool, output: Option<PathBuf>) -> Result<()> {
    let event_loop = EventLoop::new()?;
    let window = Arc::new(
        WindowBuilder::new()
            .with_title("NOVA native · space: pause · arrows: orbit · wheel: zoom · E: emit")
            .with_inner_size(winit::dpi::LogicalSize::new(1280, 800))
            .build(&event_loop)?,
    );
    let mut renderer = pollster::block_on(Renderer::new(window, universe.config.max_particles))?;
    if let Some(model) = &universe.fusion {
        renderer.scene_scale = model.settings.half_extent.into_iter().fold(0., f32::max);
        renderer.pitch = if model.settings.dimensions == 2 {
            0.
        } else {
            0.15
        };
        renderer
            .queue
            .write_buffer(&renderer._palette, 0, bytemuck::cast_slice(&model.palette));
    }
    let compute = if gpu {
        Some(nova_gpu::Gpu::new()?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let lake = Rc::new(RefCell::new(
        output
            .map(|p| Lake::new(p, &universe, 24, 32.))
            .transpose()?,
    ));
    let recorder = lake.clone();
    let error = Rc::new(RefCell::new(None::<String>));
    let failures = error.clone();
    let mut paused = false;
    let mut children = Vec::<nova_physics::FusionCandidate>::new();
    let mut last_title = Instant::now();
    let mut frames = 0;
    let mut pointer = [0f64; 2];
    let mut radial = 0f32;
    event_loop.run(move |event, elwt| {
        elwt.set_control_flow(ControlFlow::WaitUntil(
            Instant::now() + Duration::from_millis(16),
        ));
        let result: Result<()> = (|| {
            if let Event::WindowEvent { event, .. } = &event {
                if renderer.ui.on_event(&renderer.window, event) {
                    radial = 0.;
                    return Ok(());
                }
            }
            match event {
                Event::WindowEvent { event, .. } => match event {
                    WindowEvent::CloseRequested => elwt.exit(),
                    WindowEvent::Resized(size) => renderer.resize(size.width, size.height),
                    WindowEvent::KeyboardInput { event, .. }
                        if event.state == ElementState::Pressed =>
                    {
                        match event.physical_key {
                            PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::Escape) => elwt.exit(),
                            PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::Space) => paused = !paused,
                            PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::ArrowLeft) => renderer.yaw -= 0.1,
                            PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::ArrowRight) => renderer.yaw += 0.1,
                            PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::ArrowUp) => {
                                renderer.pitch = (renderer.pitch + 0.1).min(1.4)
                            }
                            PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::ArrowDown) => {
                                renderer.pitch = (renderer.pitch - 0.1).max(-1.4)
                            }
                            PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::KeyE)
                                if !universe.config.mpm && universe.fusion.is_none() =>
                            {
                                let room = universe.config.max_particles - universe.particles.len();
                                universe.emit(room.min(256))?;
                            }
                            PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::KeyB) if universe.fusion.is_some() => {
                                children = universe.breed_rules()?;
                            }
                            PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::KeyM) if universe.fusion.is_some() => {
                                let choices = universe.breed_rules()?;
                                universe.choose_rules(&choices[2])?;
                            }
                            PhysicalKey::Code(KeyCode::KeyR) if universe.fusion.is_some() => {
                                let choices = universe.breed_rules()?;
                                universe.choose_rules(&choices[5])?;
                            }
                            PhysicalKey::Code(code) if !children.is_empty() => {
                                let i = match code {
                                    KeyCode::Digit1 => Some(0),
                                    KeyCode::Digit2 => Some(1),
                                    KeyCode::Digit3 => Some(2),
                                    KeyCode::Digit4 => Some(3),
                                    KeyCode::Digit5 => Some(4),
                                    KeyCode::Digit6 => Some(5),
                                    _ => None,
                                };
                                if let Some(i) = i {
                                    universe.choose_rules(&children[i])?;
                                    children.clear();
                                }
                            }
                            _ => (),
                        }
                    }
                    WindowEvent::CursorMoved { position, .. } => {
                        pointer = [position.x, position.y];
                    }
                    WindowEvent::MouseInput { state, button, .. } => {
                        if matches!(
                            button,
                            winit::event::MouseButton::Left | winit::event::MouseButton::Right
                        ) {
                            radial = if state == ElementState::Released {
                                0.
                            } else if button == winit::event::MouseButton::Left {
                                1.
                            } else {
                                -1.
                            };
                        }
                    }
                    WindowEvent::MouseWheel { delta, .. } => {
                        let amount = match delta {
                            MouseScrollDelta::LineDelta(_, y) => y,
                            MouseScrollDelta::PixelDelta(p) => p.y as f32 * 0.02,
                        };
                        renderer.distance = (renderer.distance - amount * 0.2).clamp(1., 8.);
                    }
                    WindowEvent::RedrawRequested => {
                        if renderer.window.inner_size().width == 0
                            || renderer.window.inner_size().height == 0
                        {
                            return Ok(());
                        }
                        if !paused || renderer.ui.single_step {
                            renderer.ui.single_step = false;
                            if radial != 0. {
                                if let Some(model) = &mut universe.fusion {
                                    let aspect = renderer.config.width as f32
                                        / renderer.config.height as f32;
                                    let x = (pointer[0] as f32 / renderer.config.width as f32 * 2.
                                        - 1.)
                                        * 0.5
                                        * renderer.distance
                                        * aspect
                                        * renderer.scene_scale;
                                    let y = (1.
                                        - pointer[1] as f32 / renderer.config.height as f32 * 2.)
                                        * 0.5
                                        * renderer.distance
                                        * renderer.scene_scale;
                                    model.set_brush(
                                        [x, y, 0.],
                                        model.settings.cutoff() * 2.,
                                        radial * model.settings.force * 4.,
                                    )?;
                                }
                            }
                            if let Some(gpu) = &compute {
                                gpu.step(&mut universe)?;
                            } else {
                                universe.step(1)?;
                            }
                            if universe.tick % 10 == 0 {
                                if let Some(lake) = recorder.borrow_mut().as_mut() {
                                    lake.record(&universe, true)?;
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        renderer.draw(&mut universe, &mut paused)?;
                        frames += 1;
                        if last_title.elapsed() > Duration::from_secs(1) {
                            renderer.window.set_title(&format!(
                                "NOVA · {} particles · {} births · {:0.1} FPS · {}",
                                universe.particles.len(),
                                universe.births,
                                frames as f64 / last_title.elapsed().as_secs_f64(),
                                if paused { "paused" } else { "running" }
                            ));
                            frames = 0;
                            last_title = Instant::now();
                        }
                    }
                    _ => (),
                },
                Event::AboutToWait => renderer.window.request_redraw(),
                _ => (),
            }
            Ok(())
        })();
        if let Err(e) = result {
            *failures.borrow_mut() = Some(format!("{e:#}"));
            elwt.exit();
        }
    })?;
    if let Some(lake) = lake.borrow_mut().take() {
        lake.finish()?;
    }
    if let Some(e) = error.borrow_mut().take() {
        anyhow::bail!(e);
    }
    Ok(())
}

//! Finite observations and candidate equations, never automatic physical theorems.
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use nova_core::Particles;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Observables {
    pub mass: f64,
    pub kinetic: f64,
    pub momentum: [f64; 3],
    pub angular_momentum: [f64; 3],
    pub metabolic_energy: u64,
}
pub fn observe(p: &Particles) -> Observables {
    let mut o = Observables {
        mass: 0.,
        kinetic: 0.,
        momentum: [0.; 3],
        angular_momentum: [0.; 3],
        metabolic_energy: 0,
    };
    for i in 0..p.len() {
        let x = p.positions[i].0;
        let v = p.velocities[i].0;
        let m = v[3] as f64;
        o.mass += m;
        o.metabolic_energy += p.phenotypes[i][3] as u64;
        for k in 0..3 {
            o.kinetic += 0.5 * m * (v[k] as f64).powi(2);
            o.momentum[k] += m * v[k] as f64;
            o.angular_momentum[k] += m
                * (x[(k + 1) % 3] as f64 * v[(k + 2) % 3] as f64
                    - x[(k + 2) % 3] as f64 * v[(k + 1) % 3] as f64);
        }
    }
    o
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Relevance {
    pub normalized_entropy: f64,
    pub occupied_cells: usize,
    pub h0_lifetimes: Vec<f32>,
    pub persistent_components: usize,
    pub accepted: bool,
    pub topology_scope: &'static str,
}
/// Exact H0 Vietoris-Rips death times on a stable, at-most-64-particle subsample.
/// Kruskal MST, not a claim to H1/H2 persistent homology of the full universe.
pub fn relevance(p: &Particles) -> Relevance {
    let mut bins = HashMap::new();
    for x in &p.positions {
        *bins
            .entry([
                (x.0[0] / 0.5).floor() as i32,
                (x.0[1] / 0.5).floor() as i32,
                (x.0[2] / 0.5).floor() as i32,
            ])
            .or_insert(0usize) += 1;
    }
    let entropy = bins
        .values()
        .map(|&n| {
            let q = n as f64 / p.len().max(1) as f64;
            -q * q.ln()
        })
        .sum::<f64>()
        / (p.len().max(2) as f64).ln();
    let mut order: Vec<_> = (0..p.len()).collect();
    order.sort_unstable_by_key(|&i| p.ids[i]);
    order.truncate(64);
    let mut edges = Vec::new();
    for i in 0..order.len() {
        for j in i + 1..order.len() {
            edges.push((
                nova_core::norm(nova_core::delta(
                    p.positions[order[i]].0,
                    p.positions[order[j]].0,
                )),
                i,
                j,
            ));
        }
    }
    edges.sort_by(|a, b| a.0.total_cmp(&b.0));
    let mut roots: Vec<_> = (0..order.len()).collect();
    fn root(r: &[usize], mut i: usize) -> usize {
        while r[i] != i {
            i = r[i];
        }
        i
    }
    let mut lifetimes = Vec::new();
    for (d, i, j) in edges {
        let a = root(&roots, i);
        let b = root(&roots, j);
        if a != b {
            roots[a] = b;
            lifetimes.push(d);
        }
    }
    let persistent = lifetimes.iter().filter(|&&x| x > 0.5 && x < 8.).count();
    Relevance {
        normalized_entropy: entropy,
        occupied_cells: bins.len(),
        persistent_components: persistent,
        accepted: p.len() > 1 && entropy > 0.05 && entropy < 0.98 && persistent > 0,
        h0_lifetimes: lifetimes,
        topology_scope: "H0 on <=64 stable IDs; no H1/H2 or scientific relevance guarantee",
    }
}
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize)]
pub struct Candidate {
    pub equation: String,
    pub coefficients: Vec<f64>,
    pub residual_rmse: f64,
    pub samples: usize,
    pub status: &'static str,
}
/// L1 coordinate descent on [1,x,y,z,vx,vy,vz], standardized by column RMS.
pub fn sparse_fit(x: &[[f64; 7]], y: &[f64], lambda: f64) -> Result<Candidate> {
    ensure!(
        !x.is_empty()
            && x.len() == y.len()
            && x.iter().flatten().chain(y).all(|v| v.is_finite())
            && lambda >= 0.
            && lambda.is_finite(),
        "invalid regression data"
    );
    let n = x.len() as f64;
    let mut scale = [1.; 7];
    for j in 0..7 {
        scale[j] = (x.iter().map(|r| r[j] * r[j]).sum::<f64>() / n)
            .sqrt()
            .max(1e-10);
    }
    let mut coefficients = [0.; 7];
    let mut residual = y.to_vec();
    for _ in 0..200 {
        for j in 0..7 {
            let old = coefficients[j];
            let rho = x
                .iter()
                .zip(&residual)
                .map(|(r, &v)| {
                    let a = r[j] / scale[j];
                    a * (v + a * old)
                })
                .sum::<f64>()
                / n;
            let denom = x.iter().map(|r| (r[j] / scale[j]).powi(2)).sum::<f64>() / n;
            let value = if j == 0 {
                rho
            } else {
                rho.signum() * (rho.abs() - lambda).max(0.)
            } / denom.max(1e-12);
            for (r, v) in x.iter().zip(&mut residual) {
                *v += (old - value) * r[j] / scale[j];
            }
            coefficients[j] = value;
        }
    }
    let coefficients: Vec<_> = coefficients.iter().zip(scale).map(|(c, s)| c / s).collect();
    let labels = ["1", "x", "y", "z", "vx", "vy", "vz"];
    let equation = coefficients
        .iter()
        .zip(labels)
        .filter(|(c, _)| c.abs() > 1e-9)
        .map(|(c, l)| format!("({c:0.9})*{l}"))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(" + ");
    Ok(Candidate {
        equation: format!("ax = {}", if equation.is_empty() { "0" } else { &equation }),
        coefficients,
        residual_rmse: (residual.iter().map(|v| v * v).sum::<f64>() / n).sqrt(),
        samples: x.len(),
        status: "candidate_not_verified",
    })
}
/// Lie derivative of I along f estimated by a centered directional difference.
pub fn lie_derivative(
    state: &[f64],
    field: &[f64],
    epsilon: f64,
    invariant: impl Fn(&[f64]) -> f64,
) -> Result<f64> {
    ensure!(
        state.len() == field.len()
            && epsilon.is_finite()
            && epsilon > 0.
            && state.iter().chain(field).all(|x| x.is_finite()),
        "invalid Lie audit input"
    );
    let plus: Vec<_> = state
        .iter()
        .zip(field)
        .map(|(x, f)| x + epsilon * f)
        .collect();
    let minus: Vec<_> = state
        .iter()
        .zip(field)
        .map(|(x, f)| x - epsilon * f)
        .collect();
    Ok((invariant(&plus) - invariant(&minus)) / (2. * epsilon))
}
pub fn lean_template() -> &'static str {
    r#"import Mathlib
-- This is an algebraic pair-impulse identity, not a proof of a simulated law.
-- Receipt remains pending until `lake env lean PairMomentum.lean` succeeds.
theorem pair_impulse_momentum (ma mb va vb impulse : ℝ)
    (ha : ma ≠ 0) (hb : mb ≠ 0) :
    ma * (va + impulse / ma) + mb * (vb - impulse / mb) = ma * va + mb * vb := by
  field_simp
  <;> ring
"#
}

//! Median-split bounding-volume hierarchy for nonuniform broad-phase queries.
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub struct Bounds {
    pub min: [f32; 3],
    pub max: [f32; 3],
}
impl Bounds {
    fn intersects(&self, other: &Self) -> bool {
        (0..3).all(|k| self.min[k] <= other.max[k] && other.min[k] <= self.max[k])
    }
    fn union(&self, other: &Self) -> Self {
        Self {
            min: std::array::from_fn(|k| self.min[k].min(other.min[k])),
            max: std::array::from_fn(|k| self.max[k].max(other.max[k])),
        }
    }
}
enum Node {
    Leaf(Bounds, Vec<usize>),
    Branch(Bounds, Box<Node>, Box<Node>),
}
pub struct Bvh {
    root: Option<Node>,
}
impl Bvh {
    pub fn build(boxes: &[Bounds]) -> Result<Self> {
        ensure!(
            boxes.iter().all(|b| (0..3)
                .all(|k| b.min[k].is_finite() && b.max[k].is_finite() && b.min[k] <= b.max[k])),
            "invalid AABB"
        );
        fn node(boxes: &[Bounds], mut ids: Vec<usize>) -> Node {
            let bounds = ids
                .iter()
                .skip(1)
                .fold(boxes[ids[0]], |b, &i| b.union(&boxes[i]));
            if ids.len() <= 8 {
                return Node::Leaf(bounds, ids);
            }
            let axis = (0..3)
                .max_by(|&a, &b| {
                    (bounds.max[a] - bounds.min[a]).total_cmp(&(bounds.max[b] - bounds.min[b]))
                })
                .unwrap();
            ids.sort_unstable_by(|&a, &b| {
                (boxes[a].min[axis] + boxes[a].max[axis])
                    .total_cmp(&(boxes[b].min[axis] + boxes[b].max[axis]))
            });
            let right = ids.split_off(ids.len() / 2);
            Node::Branch(
                bounds,
                Box::new(node(boxes, ids)),
                Box::new(node(boxes, right)),
            )
        }
        Ok(Self {
            root: (!boxes.is_empty()).then(|| node(boxes, (0..boxes.len()).collect())),
        })
    }
    /// Broad-phase candidates; caller applies exact geometry intersection.
    pub fn query(&self, bounds: Bounds) -> Vec<usize> {
        fn visit(n: &Node, b: &Bounds, out: &mut Vec<usize>) {
            match n {
                Node::Leaf(a, ids) => {
                    if a.intersects(b) {
                        out.extend(ids);
                    }
                }
                Node::Branch(a, l, r) => {
                    if a.intersects(b) {
                        visit(l, b, out);
                        visit(r, b, out);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if let Some(root) = &self.root {
            visit(root, &bounds, &mut out);
        }
        out
    }
}

//! Exact neighbor candidate queries in signed 21-bit cells. No wrapped keys.
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use nova_core::Particles;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::ops::Range;
const BIAS: i32 = 1 << 20;
fn spread(v: u32) -> u64 {
    let mut x = (v & 0x1fffff) as u64;
    x = (x | (x << 32)) & 0x1f00000000ffff;
    x = (x | (x << 16)) & 0x1f0000ff0000ff;
    x = (x | (x << 8)) & 0x100f00f00f00f00f;
    x = (x | (x << 4)) & 0x10c30c30c30c30c3;
    (x | (x << 2)) & 0x1249249249249249
}
pub fn morton(cell: [i32; 3]) -> Option<u64> {
    if cell.iter().any(|&x| !(-BIAS..BIAS).contains(&x)) {
        return None;
    }
    Some(
        spread((cell[0] + BIAS) as u32)
            | (spread((cell[1] + BIAS) as u32) << 1)
            | (spread((cell[2] + BIAS) as u32) << 2),
    )
}
pub struct CellList {
    width: f32,
    ranges: HashMap<u64, Range<usize>>,
}
impl CellList {
    pub fn rebuild(p: &mut Particles, width: f32) -> Result<Self> {
        ensure!(
            width.is_finite() && width > 0.,
            "positive finite cell width required"
        );
        p.validate()?;
        let mut indexed = Vec::with_capacity(p.len());
        for (i, pos) in p.positions.iter().enumerate() {
            let cell = Self::coordinate(pos.0, width)?;
            indexed.push((morton(cell).unwrap(), p.ids[i], i));
        }
        indexed.sort_unstable();
        p.reorder(&indexed.iter().map(|x| x.2).collect::<Vec<_>>());
        let mut ranges = HashMap::new();
        for (i, (key, _, _)) in indexed.iter().enumerate() {
            ranges
                .entry(*key)
                .and_modify(|r: &mut Range<usize>| r.end = i + 1)
                .or_insert(i..i + 1);
        }
        Ok(Self { width, ranges })
    }
    fn coordinate(p: [f32; 4], width: f32) -> Result<[i32; 3]> {
        let q = [
            (p[0] / width).floor(),
            (p[1] / width).floor(),
            (p[2] / width).floor(),
        ];
        ensure!(
            q.iter()
                .all(|x| x.is_finite() && *x >= -(BIAS as f32) && *x < (BIAS as f32)),
            "position exceeds signed 21-bit spatial domain"
        );
        Ok(q.map(|x| x as i32))
    }
    /// Call with a query radius no greater than the cell width.
    pub fn candidates(&self, p: [f32; 4]) -> impl Iterator<Item = usize> + '_ {
        let c = Self::coordinate(p, self.width).expect("validated particle position");
        (-1..=1)
            .flat_map(move |x| {
                (-1..=1).flat_map(move |y| (-1..=1).map(move |z| [c[0] + x, c[1] + y, c[2] + z]))
            })
            .filter_map(|c| morton(c).and_then(|k| self.ranges.get(&k)))
            .flat_map(|r| r.clone())
    }
}

//! Sparse octree over a finite bounding cube, rebuilt when the open cloud expands.
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
struct Node {
    center: [f32; 3],
    half: f32,
    ids: Vec<usize>,
    children: Vec<Node>,
}
pub struct Octree {
    points: Vec<[f32; 3]>,
    root: Node,
}
impl Octree {
    pub fn build(points: &[[f32; 3]]) -> Result<Self> {
        ensure!(
            points.len() <= 10_000_000
                && points
                    .iter()
                    .flatten()
                    .all(|x| x.is_finite() && x.abs() < 1e15),
            "invalid octree point cloud"
        );
        let mut lo = [f32::INFINITY; 3];
        let mut hi = [f32::NEG_INFINITY; 3];
        for p in points {
            for k in 0..3 {
                lo[k] = lo[k].min(p[k]);
                hi[k] = hi[k].max(p[k]);
            }
        }
        let center = if points.is_empty() {
            [0.; 3]
        } else {
            std::array::from_fn(|k| (lo[k] + hi[k]) * 0.5)
        };
        let half = if points.is_empty() {
            1.
        } else {
            (0..3)
                .map(|k| (hi[k] - lo[k]) * 0.50001)
                .fold(1e-5, f32::max)
        };
        fn build_node(
            points: &[[f32; 3]],
            ids: Vec<usize>,
            center: [f32; 3],
            half: f32,
            depth: u32,
        ) -> Node {
            if ids.len() <= 16 || depth == 20 {
                return Node {
                    center,
                    half,
                    ids,
                    children: vec![],
                };
            }
            let mut buckets: [Vec<usize>; 8] = std::array::from_fn(|_| vec![]);
            for id in ids {
                let p = points[id];
                let key = (p[0] >= center[0]) as usize
                    | ((p[1] >= center[1]) as usize) << 1
                    | ((p[2] >= center[2]) as usize) << 2;
                buckets[key].push(id);
            }
            let mut children = vec![];
            for (key, ids) in buckets.into_iter().enumerate() {
                if !ids.is_empty() {
                    let c = std::array::from_fn(|k| {
                        center[k]
                            + if key & (1 << k) == 0 {
                                -half * 0.5
                            } else {
                                half * 0.5
                            }
                    });
                    children.push(build_node(points, ids, c, half * 0.5, depth + 1));
                }
            }
            Node {
                center,
                half,
                ids: vec![],
                children,
            }
        }
        Ok(Self {
            points: points.to_vec(),
            root: build_node(points, (0..points.len()).collect(), center, half, 0),
        })
    }
    pub fn within(&self, center: [f32; 3], radius: f32) -> Result<Vec<usize>> {
        ensure!(
            center.iter().all(|x| x.is_finite()) && radius.is_finite() && radius >= 0.,
            "invalid sphere"
        );
        fn visit(
            node: &Node,
            points: &[[f32; 3]],
            center: [f32; 3],
            r2: f32,
            out: &mut Vec<usize>,
        ) {
            let distance: f32 = (0..3)
                .map(|k| {
                    ((center[k] - node.center[k]).abs() - node.half)
                        .max(0.)
                        .powi(2)
                })
                .sum();
            if distance > r2 {
                return;
            }
            for &id in &node.ids {
                if (0..3)
                    .map(|k| (points[id][k] - center[k]).powi(2))
                    .sum::<f32>()
                    <= r2
                {
                    out.push(id);
                }
            }
            for child in &node.children {
                visit(child, points, center, r2, out);
            }
        }
        let mut out = vec![];
        visit(&self.root, &self.points, center, radius * radius, &mut out);
        Ok(out)
    }
}

//! Incremental Arrow IPC, Parquet and Zarr v3; bounded per-frame working memory.
use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use arrow::{
    array::{ArrayRef, Float32Array, Float64Array, StringArray, UInt32Array, UInt64Array},
    datatypes::{DataType, Field, Schema},
    ipc::writer::StreamWriter,
    record_batch::RecordBatch,
};
use nova_core::Particles;
use nova_physics::Universe;
use nova_science::{observe, relevance, sparse_fit, Observables};
use parquet::{
    arrow::ArrowWriter,
    basic::{Compression, ZstdLevel},
    file::properties::WriterProperties,
};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::{
    collections::{HashMap, HashSet},
    fs::{self, File, OpenOptions},
    io::{BufWriter, Write},
    path::{Path, PathBuf},
    sync::Arc,
};
fn schema(fields: &[(&str, DataType)]) -> Arc<Schema> {
    Arc::new(Schema::new(
        fields
            .iter()
            .map(|(n, t)| Field::new(*n, t.clone(), false))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
    ))
}
fn write_json(path: &Path, value: &Value) -> Result<()> {
    let tmp = path.with_extension("json.tmp");
    fs::write(&tmp, serde_json::to_vec_pretty(value)?)?;
    fs::rename(tmp, path)?;
    Ok(())
}
pub struct Lake {
    root: PathBuf,
    id: String,
    grid: usize,
    extent: f32,
    frames: usize,
    seen: HashSet<String>,
    trajectory: StreamWriter<BufWriter<File>>,
    genomes: StreamWriter<BufWriter<File>>,
    symbolic: ArrowWriter<File>,
    ts: Arc<Schema>,
    gs: Arc<Schema>,
    ss: Arc<Schema>,
    lineage: BufWriter<File>,
    index: BufWriter<File>,
    offset: u64,
    previous: HashMap<u64, ([f32; 4], [f32; 4])>,
    previous_observation: Option<Observables>,
    previous_tick: Option<u64>,
}
impl Lake {
    pub fn new(root: impl AsRef<Path>, u: &Universe, grid: usize, extent: f32) -> Result<Self> {
        ensure!(
            (4..=128).contains(&grid) && extent.is_finite() && extent > 0.,
            "invalid field grid or extent"
        );
        let root = root.as_ref().to_owned();
        if root.exists() {
            ensure!(
                fs::read_dir(&root)?.next().is_none(),
                "output directory must be empty; refusing to overwrite a lake"
            );
        } else {
            fs::create_dir_all(&root)?;
        }
        let id = format!(
            "nova-{:016x}-{}",
            u.config.seed,
            std::time::SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)?
                .as_nanos()
        );
        let ts = schema(&[
            ("tick", DataType::UInt64),
            ("particle_id", DataType::UInt64),
            ("x", DataType::Float32),
            ("y", DataType::Float32),
            ("z", DataType::Float32),
            ("vx", DataType::Float32),
            ("vy", DataType::Float32),
            ("vz", DataType::Float32),
            ("mass", DataType::Float32),
            ("species", DataType::UInt32),
        ]);
        let gs = schema(&[
            ("lineage_hash", DataType::Utf8),
            ("generation_depth", DataType::UInt32),
            ("bytecode_payload", DataType::Utf8),
            ("phenotype_metrics", DataType::Utf8),
            ("parents", DataType::Utf8),
            ("born_tick", DataType::UInt64),
        ]);
        let ss = schema(&[
            ("dataset_id", DataType::Utf8),
            ("tick", DataType::UInt64),
            ("trajectory_sample", DataType::Utf8),
            ("discovered_equation", DataType::Utf8),
            ("noether_invariant_drift", DataType::Float64),
            ("lean_verification_receipt", DataType::Utf8),
            ("observables", DataType::Utf8),
            ("relevance", DataType::Utf8),
        ]);
        let trajectory = StreamWriter::try_new(
            BufWriter::new(File::create(root.join("trajectories.arrow"))?),
            &ts,
        )?;
        let genomes = StreamWriter::try_new(
            BufWriter::new(File::create(root.join("genomes.arrow"))?),
            &gs,
        )?;
        let props = WriterProperties::builder()
            .set_compression(Compression::ZSTD(ZstdLevel::try_new(3)?))
            .set_max_row_group_size(128)
            .build();
        let symbolic = ArrowWriter::try_new(
            File::create(root.join("symbolic.parquet"))?,
            ss.clone(),
            Some(props),
        )?;
        fs::create_dir_all(root.join("world.zarr/fields"))?;
        write_json(
            &root.join("world.zarr/zarr.json"),
            &json!({"zarr_format":3,"node_type":"group","attributes":{"dataset_id":id}}),
        )?;
        fs::write(
            root.join("PairMomentum.lean"),
            nova_science::lean_template(),
        )?;
        fs::write(root.join("catalog.sql"),"CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW symbolic AS SELECT * FROM read_parquet('symbolic.parquet');\nCREATE OR REPLACE VIEW lineages AS SELECT * FROM read_json_auto('lineages.jsonl');\n")?;
        fs::write(
            root.join("load_dataset.py"),
            r#"from pathlib import Path
from datasets import Dataset, DatasetDict, load_dataset
import pyarrow.ipc as ipc
root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
# Arrow streams retain their schema; Hugging Face load_from_disk needs save_to_disk first.
with (root / 'trajectories.arrow').open('rb') as f:
    trajectories = Dataset(ipc.open_stream(f).read_all())
with (root / 'genomes.arrow').open('rb') as f:
    genomes = Dataset(ipc.open_stream(f).read_all())
result = DatasetDict(trajectories=trajectories, genomes=genomes,
    symbolic=load_dataset('parquet', data_files=str(root / 'symbolic.parquet'), split='train'))
result.save_to_disk(str(root / 'huggingface'))
"#,
        )?;
        write_json(
            &root.join("dataset_dict.json"),
            &json!({"splits":["trajectories","genomes","symbolic"],"format":"NOVA export manifest; run load_dataset.py before datasets.load_from_disk"}),
        )?;
        write_json(
            &root.join("manifest.json"),
            &json!({"schema":"NOVA-LAKE-v1","dataset_id":id,"config":u.config,"affinities":u.matrix,"field_extent":extent,"field_grid":grid,"status":"recording","conservative":false,"proof_status":"pending","temperature_units":"specific kinetic variance, not kelvin","stress_definition":"coarse kinetic stress from within-voxel velocity fluctuations","field_domain":"fixed [-extent,extent)^3; out-of-domain count recorded per frame"}),
        )?;
        let lineage = BufWriter::new(File::create(root.join("lineages.jsonl"))?);
        let index = BufWriter::new(File::create(root.join("lineages.index.jsonl"))?);
        let lake = Self {
            root,
            id,
            grid,
            extent,
            frames: 0,
            seen: HashSet::new(),
            trajectory,
            genomes,
            symbolic,
            ts,
            gs,
            ss,
            lineage,
            index,
            offset: 0,
            previous: HashMap::new(),
            previous_observation: None,
            previous_tick: None,
        };
        lake.zarr_metadata()?;
        Ok(lake)
    }
    pub fn record(&mut self, u: &Universe, record_all: bool) -> Result<bool> {
        u.particles.validate()?;
        ensure!(
            self.previous_tick.is_none_or(|t| u.tick > t),
            "recorded ticks must increase"
        );
        if let Some(model) = &u.fusion {
            let mut rules = OpenOptions::new()
                .create(true)
                .append(true)
                .open(self.root.join("fusion_frames.jsonl"))?;
            writeln!(
                rules,
                "{}",
                json!({"tick":u.tick,"settings":model.settings,"matrix":u.matrix,"rule_lineage":model.rule_history.last(),"upstream_manifest":"third_party/upstream.lock.json","seed":u.config.seed})
            )?;
        }
        let p = &u.particles;
        let relevance = relevance(p);
        // Observe every new genotype even when the frame relevance gate rejects its trajectory.
        self.record_lineages(u)?;
        if !record_all && !relevance.accepted {
            return Ok(false);
        }
        let n = p.len();
        let mut columns: Vec<ArrayRef> = vec![
            Arc::new(UInt64Array::from(vec![u.tick; n])),
            Arc::new(UInt64Array::from(p.ids.clone())),
        ];
        for k in 0..3 {
            columns.push(Arc::new(Float32Array::from_iter_values(
                p.positions.iter().map(|x| x.0[k]),
            )));
        }
        for k in 0..3 {
            columns.push(Arc::new(Float32Array::from_iter_values(
                p.velocities.iter().map(|x| x.0[k]),
            )));
        }
        columns.push(Arc::new(Float32Array::from_iter_values(
            p.velocities.iter().map(|x| x.0[3]),
        )));
        columns.push(Arc::new(UInt32Array::from_iter_values(
            p.phenotypes.iter().map(|x| x[0]),
        )));
        self.trajectory
            .write(&RecordBatch::try_new(self.ts.clone(), columns)?)?;
        let elapsed = self
            .previous_tick
            .map(|t| (u.tick - t) as f64 * u.config.dt as f64)
            .unwrap_or(0.);
        let mut rows = Vec::new();
        let mut targets = Vec::new();
        let mut samples = Vec::new();
        let mut order: Vec<_> = (0..n).collect();
        order.sort_unstable_by_key(|&i| p.ids[i]);
        order.truncate(256);
        for &i in &order {
            let x = p.positions[i].0;
            let v = p.velocities[i].0;
            samples.push(json!({"id":p.ids[i],"position":x,"velocity_mass":v}));
            if elapsed > 0. {
                if let Some((oldx, oldv)) = self.previous.get(&p.ids[i]) {
                    rows.push([
                        1.,
                        oldx[0] as f64,
                        oldx[1] as f64,
                        oldx[2] as f64,
                        oldv[0] as f64,
                        oldv[1] as f64,
                        oldv[2] as f64,
                    ]);
                    targets.push((v[0] - oldv[0]) as f64 / elapsed);
                }
            }
        }
        let candidate = if rows.len() >= 8 {
            serde_json::to_value(sparse_fit(&rows, &targets, 1e-4)?)?
        } else {
            json!({"status":"insufficient_samples"})
        };
        let observation = observe(p);
        let drift = if elapsed > 0. {
            self.previous_observation
                .as_ref()
                .map(|o| (observation.kinetic - o.kinetic) / elapsed)
                .unwrap_or(0.)
        } else {
            0.
        };
        let receipt = json!({"status":"pending","file":"PairMomentum.lean","claim":"pair impulse algebra only; not a proof of the discovered regression","physical_truth_proved":false});
        let strings = [
            self.id.clone(),
            serde_json::to_string(&samples)?,
            candidate.to_string(),
            receipt.to_string(),
            serde_json::to_string(&observation)?,
            serde_json::to_string(&relevance)?,
        ];
        let s = |i: usize| -> ArrayRef { Arc::new(StringArray::from(vec![strings[i].as_str()])) };
        self.symbolic.write(&RecordBatch::try_new(
            self.ss.clone(),
            vec![
                s(0),
                Arc::new(UInt64Array::from(vec![u.tick])),
                s(1),
                s(2),
                Arc::new(Float64Array::from(vec![drift])),
                s(3),
                s(4),
                s(5),
            ],
        )?)?;
        let (field, outside) = voxelize(p, self.grid, self.extent);
        ensure!(
            field.iter().all(|x| x.is_finite()),
            "nonfinite coarse field"
        );
        let raw: Vec<u8> = field.iter().flat_map(|x| x.to_le_bytes()).collect();
        let chunk = self
            .root
            .join(format!("world.zarr/fields/c/{}/0/0/0", self.frames));
        fs::create_dir_all(&chunk)?;
        let compressed = zstd::stream::encode_all(raw.as_slice(), 3)?;
        fs::write(chunk.join("0.tmp"), compressed)?;
        fs::rename(chunk.join("0.tmp"), chunk.join("0"))?;
        let mut frames = OpenOptions::new()
            .create(true)
            .append(true)
            .open(self.root.join("frames.jsonl"))?;
        writeln!(
            frames,
            "{}",
            json!({"frame":self.frames,"tick":u.tick,"time":u.tick as f64*u.config.dt as f64,"outside_field_domain":outside,"kinetic_drift":drift,"drift_available":elapsed>0.})
        )?;
        self.frames += 1;
        self.zarr_metadata()?;
        self.previous = order
            .iter()
            .map(|&i| (p.ids[i], (p.positions[i].0, p.velocities[i].0)))
            .collect();
        self.previous_tick = Some(u.tick);
        self.previous_observation = Some(observation);
        Ok(true)
    }
    fn record_lineages(&mut self, u: &Universe) -> Result<()> {
        let p = &u.particles;
        let mut hashes = Vec::new();
        let mut generation = Vec::new();
        let mut tapes = Vec::new();
        let mut metrics = Vec::new();
        let mut parents = Vec::new();
        let mut ticks = Vec::new();
        for i in 0..p.len() {
            let l = &p.lineages[i];
            if !self.seen.insert(l.hash.clone()) {
                continue;
            }
            let tape = serde_json::to_string(&p.genomes[i])?;
            let metrics_json = json!({"particle_id":p.ids[i],"species":p.phenotypes[i][0],"metabolic_energy":p.phenotypes[i][3],"replication_rate":null,"energy_efficiency":null,"behavioral_complexity":null});
            let row = json!({"lineage_hash":l.hash,"generation_depth":l.generation,"bytecode_payload":p.genomes[i],"phenotype_metrics":metrics_json,"parents":l.parents,"born_tick":l.born_tick});
            let raw = serde_json::to_vec(&row)?;
            writeln!(
                self.index,
                "{}",
                json!({"lineage_hash":l.hash,"byte_offset":self.offset,"byte_length":raw.len()})
            )?;
            self.lineage.write_all(&raw)?;
            self.lineage.write_all(b"\n")?;
            self.offset += raw.len() as u64 + 1;
            hashes.push(l.hash.clone());
            generation.push(l.generation);
            tapes.push(tape);
            metrics.push(metrics_json.to_string());
            parents.push(serde_json::to_string(&l.parents)?);
            ticks.push(l.born_tick);
        }
        if !hashes.is_empty() {
            self.genomes.write(&RecordBatch::try_new(
                self.gs.clone(),
                vec![
                    Arc::new(StringArray::from(hashes)),
                    Arc::new(UInt32Array::from(generation)),
                    Arc::new(StringArray::from(tapes)),
                    Arc::new(StringArray::from(metrics)),
                    Arc::new(StringArray::from(parents)),
                    Arc::new(UInt64Array::from(ticks)),
                ],
            )?)?;
        }
        self.lineage.flush()?;
        self.index.flush()?;
        Ok(())
    }
    fn zarr_metadata(&self) -> Result<()> {
        write_json(
            &self.root.join("world.zarr/fields/zarr.json"),
            &json!({"zarr_format":3,"node_type":"array","shape":[self.frames,12,self.grid,self.grid,self.grid],"data_type":"float32","chunk_grid":{"name":"regular","configuration":{"chunk_shape":[1,12,self.grid,self.grid,self.grid]}},"chunk_key_encoding":{"name":"default","configuration":{"separator":"/"}},"fill_value":0.,"codecs":[{"name":"bytes","configuration":{"endian":"little"}},{"name":"zstd","configuration":{"level":3,"checksum":false}}],"dimension_names":["time","channel","x","y","z"],"attributes":{"channels":["density","vx","vy","vz","divergence","kinetic_stress_xx","kinetic_stress_yy","kinetic_stress_zz","kinetic_stress_xy","kinetic_stress_xz","kinetic_stress_yz","kinetic_temperature"],"origin":[-self.extent,-self.extent,-self.extent],"spacing":2.*self.extent/self.grid as f32}}),
        )
    }
    pub fn finish(mut self) -> Result<()> {
        self.trajectory.finish()?;
        self.trajectory.into_inner()?.flush()?;
        self.genomes.finish()?;
        self.genomes.into_inner()?.flush()?;
        self.symbolic.close()?;
        self.lineage.flush()?;
        self.index.flush()?;
        let path = self.root.join("manifest.json");
        let mut manifest: Value = serde_json::from_slice(&fs::read(&path)?)?;
        manifest["status"] = json!("complete");
        manifest["frames"] = json!(self.frames);
        write_json(&path, &manifest)
    }
}
fn voxelize(p: &Particles, g: usize, extent: f32) -> (Vec<f32>, usize) {
    let n = g * g * g;
    let dx = 2. * extent / g as f32;
    let volume = dx.powi(3);
    let mut field = vec![0.; 12 * n];
    let mut outside = 0;
    let mut members = Vec::with_capacity(p.len());
    for i in 0..p.len() {
        let x = p.positions[i].0;
        let q = [
            ((x[0] + extent) / dx).floor(),
            ((x[1] + extent) / dx).floor(),
            ((x[2] + extent) / dx).floor(),
        ];
        if q.iter().any(|&x| x < 0. || x >= g as f32) {
            outside += 1;
            members.push(None);
            continue;
        }
        let idx = (q[0] as usize * g + q[1] as usize) * g + q[2] as usize;
        members.push(Some(idx));
        let v = p.velocities[i].0;
        field[idx] += v[3] / volume;
        for k in 0..3 {
            field[(k + 1) * n + idx] += v[k] * v[3] / volume;
        }
    }
    for idx in 0..n {
        if field[idx] > 0. {
            for k in 1..=3 {
                field[k * n + idx] /= field[idx];
            }
        }
    }
    for (i, idx) in members.into_iter().enumerate() {
        if let Some(idx) = idx {
            let v = p.velocities[i].0;
            let d = [
                v[0] - field[n + idx],
                v[1] - field[2 * n + idx],
                v[2] - field[3 * n + idx],
            ];
            for (k, (a, b)) in [(0, 0), (1, 1), (2, 2), (0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 2)]
                .into_iter()
                .enumerate()
            {
                field[(5 + k) * n + idx] += v[3] * d[a] * d[b] / volume;
            }
        }
    }
    for x in 0..g {
        for y in 0..g {
            for z in 0..g {
                let idx = (x * g + y) * g + z;
                let coord = [x, y, z];
                let mut div = 0.;
                for k in 0..3 {
                    let mut lo = coord;
                    let mut hi = coord;
                    lo[k] = lo[k].saturating_sub(1);
                    hi[k] = (hi[k] + 1).min(g - 1);
                    let a = (lo[0] * g + lo[1]) * g + lo[2];
                    let b = (hi[0] * g + hi[1]) * g + hi[2];
                    div += (field[(k + 1) * n + b] - field[(k + 1) * n + a])
                        / ((hi[k] - lo[k]) as f32 * dx);
                }
                field[4 * n + idx] = div;
                if field[idx] > 0. {
                    field[11 * n + idx] =
                        (field[5 * n + idx] + field[6 * n + idx] + field[7 * n + idx])
                            / (3. * field[idx]);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    (field, outside)
}

//! Bounded PVM: 16 one-byte instructions, high nibble opcode, low nibble operand.
//! No unbounded jumps or claims of Turing completeness.
use nova_core::{Lineage, Particle, SplitMix64};
#[repr(u8)]
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug)]
pub enum Opcode {
    Nop = 0,
    SenseDensity = 1,
    SetAffinity = 2,
    TransduceForce = 3,
    Metabolize = 4,
    Recombine = 5,
}
#[derive(Default, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct Registers {
    pub density: f32,
    pub affinity: f32,
    pub impulse: f32,
    pub drag_resistance: f32,
    pub recombine: bool,
}
pub fn execute(tape: &[u8; 16], density: f32, energy: &mut u32, dt: f32) -> Registers {
    let mut r = Registers {
        density,
        affinity: 1.,
        ..Default::default()
    };
    for &instruction in tape {
        let arg = (instruction & 15) as f32;
        match instruction >> 4 {
            1 => r.density = density,
            2 => r.affinity = (arg - 7.5) / 7.5,
            3 => r.impulse += (arg / 15.) / (1. + r.density),
            4 => {
                let cost = (1. + arg * dt * 100.).ceil() as u32;
                if *energy >= cost {
                    *energy -= cost;
                    r.drag_resistance = (arg / 15.).min(0.9);
                }
            }
            5 => r.recombine = *energy >= 2000,
            _ => (),
        }
    }
    r
}
pub fn crossover(
    a: &Particle,
    b: &Particle,
    la: &Lineage,
    lb: &Lineage,
    tick: u64,
    rng: &mut SplitMix64,
) -> (Particle, Lineage) {
    let cut = 1 + (rng.next_u64() % 15) as usize;
    let mut tape = a.genome_tape;
    tape[cut..].copy_from_slice(&b.genome_tape[cut..]);
    for op in &mut tape {
        if rng.unit() < 0.01 {
            *op = ((rng.next_u64() % 6) as u8) << 4 | ((rng.next_u64() % 16) as u8);
        }
    }
    let mut child = *a;
    for k in 0..3 {
        child.pos_rad[k] = (a.pos_rad[k] + b.pos_rad[k]) * 0.5;
        child.vel_mass[k] = (a.vel_mass[k] * a.vel_mass[3] + b.vel_mass[k] * b.vel_mass[3])
            / (a.vel_mass[3] + b.vel_mass[3]);
    }
    child.vel_mass[3] = (a.vel_mass[3] + b.vel_mass[3]) * 0.1;
    child.phenotype[3] = 2000;
    child.genome_tape = tape;
    let lineage = Lineage::new(
        &tape,
        vec![la.hash.clone(), lb.hash.clone()],
        la.generation.max(lb.generation) + 1,
        tick,
    );
    (child, lineage)
}

use nova_physics::{Config, Universe};
fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    let mut universe = Universe::new(4096, Config::default())?;
    universe.step(100)?;
    let positions = &universe.particles.positions; // aligned, borrowed, no serialization
    println!("{} particles at tick {}", positions.len(), universe.tick);
    Ok(())
}

#ifndef NOVA_H
#define NOVA_H

#include <stdarg.h>
#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdlib.h>

typedef struct NovaHandle NovaHandle;

/**
 * Portable 64-byte C transfer record; native SoA storage has separate alignment.
 */
typedef struct NovaParticle {
  float pos_rad[4];
  float vel_mass[4];
  uint32_t phenotype[4];
  uint8_t genome_tape[16];
} NovaParticle;

#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif // __cplusplus

/**
 * Creates an owned handle. preset: 0=canonical, 1=cosmic ink, 2=PVM, 3=liquid MPM.
 * # Safety
 * out must point to a writable handle slot. Free a successful result exactly once.
 */
int32_t nova_create(size_t count,
                    uint64_t seed,
                    uint32_t preset,
                    bool crossover,
                    struct NovaHandle **out);

/**
 * # Safety
 * handle must be live and exclusively owned for this call; no concurrent array reads.
 */
int32_t nova_step(struct NovaHandle *handle, size_t ticks);

/**
 * # Safety
 * handle must be live, exclusively owned and not previously freed. Null is allowed.
 */
void nova_destroy(struct NovaHandle *handle);

/**
 * Borrow float4 positions/radius. Pointer remains valid only until next mutation or destroy.
 * # Safety
 * handle must be live; data and count writable. Do not mutate or free returned memory.
 */
int32_t nova_positions(const struct NovaHandle *handle, const float **data, size_t *count);

/**
 * # Safety
 * Same lifetime and synchronization contract as nova_positions.
 */
int32_t nova_velocities(const struct NovaHandle *handle, const float **data, size_t *count);

/**
 * Inject a custom 64-byte particle record. The C struct need not be 64-byte aligned.
 * # Safety
 * handle exclusively live; particle points to 64 readable bytes in native C layout.
 */
int32_t nova_inject(struct NovaHandle *handle, const struct NovaParticle *particle);

/**
 * Returns required bytes including NUL. Copies at most capacity bytes of last thread-local error.
 * # Safety
 * If nonnull, destination must be writable for capacity bytes.
 */
size_t nova_last_error(char *destination, size_t capacity);

/**
 * Native upstream family: 0=hunar, 1=realethersim, 2=primordial-dish,
 * 3=par-particle-life, 4=fusion, 5=cellflow (independent implementation).
 * # Safety
 * out must be a writable handle slot; destroy a successful result exactly once.
 */
int32_t nova_create_fusion(size_t count, uint64_t seed, uint32_t family, struct NovaHandle **out);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}  // extern "C"
#endif  // __cplusplus

#endif  /* NOVA_H */
