// Separable bilateral depth + guided pigment/thickness smoothing.
struct Filter {
    axis:vec4<i32>,values:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var depth:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var pigment:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var output_depth:texture_storage_2d<r32float,write>;
@group(0) @binding(3) var output_pigment:texture_storage_2d<rgba16float,write>;
@group(0) @binding(4) var<uniform> cfg:Filter;
@compute @workgroup_size(8,8)
fn bilateral_main(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let p=vec2<i32>(id.xy);
    let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(depth));
    if(any(p>=size)){
        return;
    }
    let center=textureLoad(depth,p,0).r;
    if(center>=9.99){
        textureStore(output_depth,p,vec4(10.));
        textureStore(output_pigment,p,vec4(0.));
        return;
    }
    var sum=0.;
    var z=0.;
    var color=vec4(0.);
    for(var k=-8;k<=8;k++){
        if(abs(k)>cfg.axis.z){
            continue;
        }
        let q=clamp(p+cfg.axis.xy*k,vec2(0),size-1);
        let d=textureLoad(depth,q,0).r;
        if(d>=9.99){
            continue;
        }
        let spatial=exp(-.5*f32(k*k)/cfg.values.x);
        let range=exp(-.5*(d-center)*(d-center)/cfg.values.y);
        let w=spatial*range;
        sum+=w;
        z+=w*d;
        color+=w*textureLoad(pigment,q,0);
    }
    textureStore(output_depth,p,vec4(z/max(sum,1e-8)));
    textureStore(output_pigment,p,color/max(sum,1e-8));
}


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/shaders/fluid_lighting.wgsl
========================================================================================
// GGX surface, Beer-Lambert thickness, approximate SSS/HG and thin-film tint.
struct Camera {
    eye:vec4<f32>,right:vec4<f32>,up:vec4<f32>,forward:vec4<f32>,view:vec4<f32>,material:vec4<f32>,light:vec4<f32>,post:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var depth:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var pigment:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<uniform> cam:Camera;
struct Quad {
    @builtin(position) pos:vec4<f32>,@location(0) uv:vec2<f32>
}
@vertex fn fullscreen(@builtin(vertex_index) i:u32)->Quad{
    var p=array<vec2<f32>,3>(vec2(-1.,-1.),vec2(3.,-1.),vec2(-1.,3.));
    var out:Quad;
    out.pos=vec4(p[i],0.,1.);
    out.uv=p[i]*vec2(.5,-.5)+.5;
    return out;
}
fn ray(p:vec2<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    let uv=(p/cam.view.xy*2.-1.)*vec2(1.,-1.);
    return normalize(cam.forward.xyz+cam.right.xyz*uv.x*cam.forward.w*cam.view.x/cam.view.y+cam.up.xyz*uv.y*cam.forward.w);
}
fn position(p:vec2<i32>,fallback:f32)->vec3<f32>{
    let size=vec2<i32>(textureDimensions(depth));
    let q=clamp(p,vec2(0),size-1);
    var z=textureLoad(depth,q,0).r;
    if(z>=9.99){
        z=fallback;
    }
    let r=ray(vec2<f32>(q)+.5);
    return cam.eye.xyz+r*z/max(dot(r,cam.forward.xyz),.01);
}
fn environment(r:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    let ceiling=pow(max(r.y,0.),5.);
    let panel=pow(max(dot(r,normalize(vec3(-.5,.7,.4))),0.),45.);
    return mix(vec3(.012,.018,.035),vec3(.15,.19,.25),r.y*.5+.5)+vec3(.9,.8,.7)*ceiling+vec3(5.,5.5,6.)*panel;
}
fn phase(cosine:f32,g:f32)->f32{
    return (1.-g*g)/(12.5663706*pow(max(1.+g*g-2.*g*cosine,.001),1.5));
}
fn brdf(n:vec3<f32>,v:vec3<f32>,l:vec3<f32>,albedo:vec3<f32>,F0:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    let h=normalize(v+l);
    let nl=max(dot(n,l),0.);
    let nv=max(dot(n,v),.001);
    let nh=max(dot(n,h),0.);
    let vh=max(dot(v,h),0.);
    let a=max(.025,cam.material.x*cam.material.x);
    let a2=a*a;
    let denom=nh*nh*(a2-1.)+1.;
    let D=a2/(3.14159265*denom*denom);
    let k=(cam.material.x+1.)*(cam.material.x+1.)/8.;
    let G=nv/(nv*(1.-k)+k)*nl/max(nl*(1.-k)+k,.001);
    let F=F0+(1.-F0)*pow(1.-vh,5.);
    return ((1.-F)*albedo/3.14159265+D*G*F/max(4.*nl*nv,.001))*nl;
}
@fragment fn shade(in:Quad)->@location(0) vec4<f32>{
    let pixel=vec2<i32>(in.pos.xy);
    let z=textureLoad(depth,pixel,0).r;
    let r=ray(in.pos.xy);
    let background=environment(r)*.12+vec3(.008,.006,.018);
    if(z>=9.99){
        return vec4(background,1.);
    }
    let p=position(pixel,z);
    let xp=position(pixel+vec2(1,0),z)-p;
    let xm=p-position(pixel-vec2(1,0),z);
    let yp=position(pixel+vec2(0,1),z)-p;
    let ym=p-position(pixel-vec2(0,1),z);
    let dx=select(xm,xp,abs(dot(xp,cam.forward.xyz))<abs(dot(xm,cam.forward.xyz)));
    let dy=select(ym,yp,abs(dot(yp,cam.forward.xyz))<abs(dot(ym,cam.forward.xyz)));
    var n=normalize(cross(dx,dy)+vec3(1e-8));
    let v=normalize(cam.eye.xyz-p);
    if(dot(n,v)<0.){
        n=-n;
    }
    let raw=textureLoad(pigment,pixel,0);
    let thickness=clamp(raw.a,0.,4.);
    let albedo=clamp(raw.rgb/max(raw.a,.00001),vec3(.015),vec3(.98));
    let sigma=-log(albedo)*cam.material.y;
    let trans=exp(-sigma*thickness);
    let nv=max(dot(n,v),0.);
    let wavelength=vec3(650.,510.,475.);
    let film=.5+.5*cos(12.5663706*1.33*cam.material.w*max(.2,nv)/wavelength);
    let F0=mix(vec3(.035),vec3(.035)+.18*film,cam.light.w);
    var color=vec3(0.);
    let l1=normalize(vec3(-.6,.9,.8));
    let l2=normalize(vec3(.8,.1,-.6));
    let l3=normalize(vec3(-.4,-.5,-.8));
    color+=brdf(n,v,l1,albedo,F0)*vec3(4.,3.8,3.5);
    color+=brdf(n,v,l2,albedo,F0)*vec3(1.1,1.5,2.4);
    color+=brdf(n,v,l3,albedo,F0)*vec3(1.8,.6,1.2);
    let reflection=reflect(-v,n);
    let F=F0+(1.-F0)*pow(1.-nv,5.);
    color+=environment(reflection)*F;
    let back=pow(max(dot(-n,l2),0.),2.)+phase(dot(-v,l2),cam.light.z)*2.;
    color+=cam.material.z*(1.-trans)*albedo*(.4+back)*vec3(.9,1.1,1.5);
    let bend=r+(.18*(1.-trans.r))*n;
    let behind=environment(normalize(bend))*.2;
    color+=trans*behind*(1.-F);
    color+=.02*albedo*pow(1.-nv,3.);
    return vec4(max(color,vec3(0.)),1.);
}

ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/shaders/fluid_post.wgsl

// Bounded image-space lens/bloom; ACES fitted display curve, not full ACES OCIO.
struct Post {
    view:vec4<f32>,lens:vec4<f32>,style:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var hdr:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var depth:texture_2d<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var linear:sampler;
@group(0) @binding(3) var<uniform> cfg:Post;
struct Quad {
    @builtin(position) pos:vec4<f32>,@location(0) uv:vec2<f32>
}
@vertex fn fullscreen(@builtin(vertex_index) i:u32)->Quad{
    var p=array<vec2<f32>,3>(vec2(-1.,-1.),vec2(3.,-1.),vec2(-1.,3.));
    var out:Quad;
    out.pos=vec4(p[i],0.,1.);
    out.uv=p[i]*vec2(.5,-.5)+.5;
    return out;
}
fn aces(x:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    return clamp((x*(2.51*x+.03))/(x*(2.43*x+.59)+.14),vec3(0.),vec3(1.));
}
@fragment fn post(in:Quad)->@location(0) vec4<f32>{
    let uv=in.uv;
    let size=cfg.view.xy;
    let z=textureLoad(depth,vec2<i32>(clamp(in.pos.xy,vec2(0.),size-1.)),0).r;
    let coc=min(12.,cfg.lens.y*abs(z-cfg.lens.x)/max(z,.1));
    var color=textureSampleLevel(hdr,linear,uv,0.).rgb;
    var weight=1.;
    var bloom=vec3(0.);
    for(var i=0;i<16;i++){
        let a=f32(i)*2.399963;
        let radius=sqrt((f32(i)+.5)/16.);
        let direction=vec2(cos(a),sin(a));
        let aperture=direction*radius*coc/size;
        let sample=textureSampleLevel(hdr,linear,clamp(uv+aperture,vec2(0.),vec2(1.)),0.).rgb;
        if(coc>.5){
            color+=sample;
            weight+=1.;
        }
        let light=textureSampleLevel(hdr,linear,clamp(uv+direction*radius*24./size,vec2(0.),vec2(1.)),0.).rgb;
        bloom+=max(light-vec3(1.),vec3(0.))/16.;
    }
    color=color/weight+cfg.lens.z*bloom;
    let vignette=1.-cfg.style.x*dot(uv-.5,uv-.5);
    let noise=fract(sin(dot(in.pos.xy,vec2(12.9898,78.233))+cfg.view.z*.13)*43758.5453)-.5;
    let mapped=pow(aces(color*cfg.lens.w),vec3(1./2.2))*vignette+noise*cfg.style.y;
    return vec4(clamp(mapped,vec3(0.),vec3(1.)),1.);
}

ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/shaders/fluid_surface.wgsl

// Surface splats: sphere depth and additive pigment optical thickness.
struct Particle {
    p:vec4<f32>,v:vec4<f32>,previous:vec4<f32>
}
struct Camera {
    eye:vec4<f32>,right:vec4<f32>,up:vec4<f32>,forward:vec4<f32>,view:vec4<f32>,material:vec4<f32>,light:vec4<f32>,post:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage,read> particles:array<Particle>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<uniform> cam:Camera;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage,read> palette:array<vec4<f32>>;
struct Vertex {
    @builtin(position) position:vec4<f32>,@location(0) uv:vec2<f32>,@location(1) @interpolate(flat) depth:f32,@location(2) @interpolate(flat) pigment:vec3<f32>
}
@vertex fn splat(@builtin(vertex_index) vi:u32,@builtin(instance_index) pi:u32)->Vertex{
    var corners=array<vec2<f32>,6>(vec2(-1.,-1.),vec2(1.,-1.),vec2(-1.,1.),vec2(-1.,1.),vec2(1.,-1.),vec2(1.,1.));
    let p=particles[pi];
    let corner=corners[vi];
    let radius=cam.view.z;
    let local=p.p.xyz-cam.eye.xyz;
    let z=dot(local,cam.forward.xyz);
    let x=dot(local,cam.right.xyz)+corner.x*radius;
    let y=dot(local,cam.up.xyz)+corner.y*radius;
    let near=.05;
    let far=10.;
    var out:Vertex;
    out.position=vec4(x/(cam.forward.w*cam.view.x/cam.view.y),y/cam.forward.w,far/(far-near)*z-far*near/(far-near),z);
    out.uv=corner;
    out.depth=z;
    out.pigment=palette[u32(p.p.w)].rgb;
    return out;
}
struct DepthOut {
    @location(0) depth:f32,@builtin(frag_depth) hardware:f32
}
@fragment fn sphere_depth(in:Vertex)->DepthOut{
    let q=dot(in.uv,in.uv);
    if(q>=1.||in.depth<.06){
        discard;
    }
    let z=max(.051,in.depth-cam.view.z*sqrt(1.-q));
    var out:DepthOut;
    out.depth=z;
    out.hardware=10./9.95-.5/(9.95*z);
    return out;
}
@fragment fn optical_thickness(in:Vertex)->@location(0) vec4<f32>{
    let q=dot(in.uv,in.uv);
    if(q>=1.||in.depth<.06){
        discard;
    }
    let thickness=2.*cam.view.z*sqrt(1.-q);
    return vec4(in.pigment*thickness,thickness);
}


========================================================================================
ARQUIVO: nova/visuals/shaders/particle_life.wgsl
========================================================================================
// NOVA Particle Life + optional finite PBF constraints. All state stays on GPU.
struct Particle {
    p:vec4<f32>, v:vec4<f32>, previous:vec4<f32>
}
struct Sim {
    counts:vec4<u32>, dynamics:vec4<f32>, fluid:vec4<f32>, art:vec4<f32>, extra:vec4<f32>
}
@group(0) @binding(0) var<storage,read> src:array<Particle>;
@group(0) @binding(1) var<storage,read_write> dst:array<Particle>;
@group(0) @binding(2) var<storage,read_write> heads:array<atomic<i32>>;
@group(0) @binding(3) var<storage,read_write> links:array<i32>;
@group(0) @binding(4) var<storage,read_write> density:array<vec4<f32>>;
@group(0) @binding(5) var<storage,read> affinity:array<f32>;
@group(0) @binding(6) var<storage,read_write> stats:array<atomic<u32>>;
@group(0) @binding(7) var<uniform> cfg:Sim;
fn wrap(p:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    return p-2.*floor((p+1.)/2.);
}
fn delta(a:vec3<f32>,b:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    return wrap(a-b);
}
fn cell(p:vec3<f32>)->vec3<i32>{
    return vec3<i32>(floor((wrap(p)+1.)*.5*f32(cfg.counts.y)));
}
fn key(p:vec3<i32>)->u32{
    let n=i32(cfg.counts.y);
    let q=((p%vec3(n))+vec3(n))%vec3(n);
    return u32((q.z*n+q.y)*n+q.x);
}
fn weight(r2:f32)->f32{
    let h=cfg.dynamics.y;
    let q=max(h*h-r2,0.);
    return 315./(64.*3.14159265*pow(h,9.))*q*q*q;
}
fn gradient(d:vec3<f32>)->vec3<f32>{
    let r=length(d);
    let h=cfg.dynamics.y;
    if(r<1e-6||r>=h){
        return vec3(0.);
    }
    return -45./(3.14159265*pow(h,6.))*(h-r)*(h-r)*d/r;
}
fn neighbor_cell(c:vec3<i32>,k:i32)->u32{
    return key(c+vec3(k%3-1,(k/3)%3-1,k/9-1));
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn clear_grid(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    let n=cfg.counts.y;
    if(i<n*n*n){
        atomicStore(&heads[i],-1);
    }
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn clear_stats(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    if(id.x<4u){
        atomicStore(&stats[id.x],0u);
    }
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn build_grid(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    if(i>=cfg.counts.x){
        return;
    }
    links[i]=atomicExchange(&heads[key(cell(src[i].p.xyz))],i32(i));
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn predict(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    if(i>=cfg.counts.x){
        return;
    }
    let old=src[i];
    let c=cell(old.p.xyz);
    let h=cfg.dynamics.y;
    var force=vec3(0.);
    var viscous=vec3(0.);
    var shear=0.;
    var neighbors=0.;
    var visited=0u;
    var truncated=false;
    for(var k=0;k<27;k++){
        var j=atomicLoad(&heads[neighbor_cell(c,k)]);
        loop{
            if(j<0){
                break;
            }
            if(visited>=cfg.counts.w){
                truncated=true;
                break;
            }
            visited++;
            let other=src[u32(j)];
            if(u32(j)!=i){
                let d=delta(other.p.xyz,old.p.xyz);
                let r=length(d);
                if(r>1e-5&&r<h){
                    let q=r/h;
                    let a=affinity[u32(old.p.w)*8u+u32(other.p.w)];
                    let beta=cfg.extra.x;
                    var f=q/beta-1.;
                    if(q>=beta){
                        f=a*(1.-abs(2.*q-1.-beta)/(1.-beta));
                    }
                    force+=d/r*f;
                    let w=1.-q;
                    viscous+=(other.v.xyz-old.v.xyz)*w;
                    shear+=length(other.v.xyz-old.v.xyz)*w/max(r,.01);
                    neighbors+=1.;
                }
            }
            j=links[u32(j)];
        }
        if(truncated){
            break;
        }
    }
    if(truncated){
        atomicAdd(&stats[0],1u);
    }
    if(cfg.extra.y>.5){
        force/=max(neighbors,1.);
    }
    viscous/=max(neighbors,1.);
    shear/=max(neighbors,1.);
    let dt=cfg.dynamics.x;
    let structure=(old.v.w+dt*.5)/(1.+dt*(.5+shear));
    let eta=clamp(cfg.fluid.w+cfg.art.w*structure/(.05+shear),0.,2.);
    let p=old.p.xyz;
    let t=f32(cfg.counts.z)*dt;
    let curl=vec3(sin(2.*p.z+t*.2)+cos(2.*p.y),sin(2.*p.x)+cos(2.*p.z+t*.2),sin(2.*p.y)+cos(2.*p.x));
    force=cfg.dynamics.z*force+eta*viscous+cfg.art.x*curl+vec3(0.,cfg.art.y,0.)-cfg.art.z*p*max(dot(p,p)-.64,0.);
    var velocity=(old.v.xyz+dt*force)*exp(-cfg.dynamics.w*dt);
    let speed=length(velocity);
    if(speed>cfg.extra.z){
        velocity*=cfg.extra.z/speed;
        atomicAdd(&stats[1],1u);
    }
    var out=old;
    out.previous=old.p;
    if(cfg.extra.w>.5){
        velocity.z=0.;
        out.previous.z=0.;
    }
    out.p=vec4(wrap(p+dt*velocity),old.p.w);
    if(cfg.extra.w>.5){
        out.p.z=0.;
    }
    out.v=vec4(velocity,clamp(structure,0.,1.));
    dst[i]=out;
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn solve_density(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    if(i>=cfg.counts.x){
        return;
    }
    let p=src[i].p.xyz;
    let c=cell(p);
    var rho=0.;
    var norm=0.;
    var sum_gradient=vec3(0.);
    var visited=0u;
    var truncated=false;
    for(var k=0;k<27;k++){
        var j=atomicLoad(&heads[neighbor_cell(c,k)]);
        loop{
            if(j<0){
                break;
            }
            if(visited>=cfg.counts.w){
                truncated=true;
                break;
            }
            visited++;
            let d=delta(p,src[u32(j)].p.xyz);
            let r2=dot(d,d);
            if(r2<cfg.dynamics.y*cfg.dynamics.y){
                rho+=cfg.fluid.x*weight(r2);
                let g=cfg.fluid.x/cfg.fluid.y*gradient(d);
                if(u32(j)!=i){
                    norm+=dot(g,g);
                    sum_gradient+=g;
                }
            }
            j=links[u32(j)];
        }
        if(truncated){
            break;
        }
    }
    if(truncated){
        atomicAdd(&stats[0],1u);
    }
    let constraint=max(rho/cfg.fluid.y-1.,0.);
    density[i]=vec4(-constraint/(norm+dot(sum_gradient,sum_gradient)+cfg.fluid.z),rho,0.,0.);
    atomicMax(&stats[2],u32(min(rho,1000.)*1000.));
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn project(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    if(i>=cfg.counts.x){
        return;
    }
    let old=src[i];
    let p=old.p.xyz;
    let c=cell(p);
    var correction=vec3(0.);
    var visited=0u;
    var truncated=false;
    for(var k=0;k<27;k++){
        var j=atomicLoad(&heads[neighbor_cell(c,k)]);
        loop{
            if(j<0){
                break;
            }
            if(visited>=cfg.counts.w){
                truncated=true;
                break;
            }
            visited++;
            if(u32(j)!=i){
                let d=delta(p,src[u32(j)].p.xyz);
                if(dot(d,d)<cfg.dynamics.y*cfg.dynamics.y){
                    let relative=weight(dot(d,d))/max(weight(.09*cfg.dynamics.y*cfg.dynamics.y),1e-6);
                    let anti_clump=-.0005*pow(relative,4.);
                    correction+=(density[i].x+density[u32(j)].x+anti_clump)*gradient(d)*cfg.fluid.x/cfg.fluid.y;
                }
            }
            j=links[u32(j)];
        }
        if(truncated){
            break;
        }
    }
    if(truncated){
        atomicAdd(&stats[0],1u);
    }
    let magnitude=length(correction);
    if(magnitude>.04){
        correction*=.04/magnitude;
        atomicAdd(&stats[3],1u);
    }
    var out=old;
    out.p=vec4(wrap(p+correction),old.p.w);
    dst[i]=out;
}
@compute @workgroup_size(64)
fn finalize(@builtin(global_invocation_id) id:vec3<u32>){
    let i=id.x;
    if(i>=cfg.counts.x){
        return;
    }
    var out=src[i];
    var velocity=delta(out.p.xyz,out.previous.xyz)/cfg.dynamics.x;
    if(cfg.extra.w>.5){
        velocity.z=0.;
    }
    let speed=length(velocity);
    out.v=vec4(velocity*min(1.,cfg.extra.z/max(speed,1e-6)),out.v.w);
    dst[i]=out;
}

use anyhow::{ensure, Result};
use clap::{Parser, Subcommand, ValueEnum};
use nova_physics::{
    conway::{Conway, Rule},
    mpm::Mpm,
    Config, Universe,
};
use nova_storage::Lake;
use serde_json::json;
use std::{path::PathBuf, time::Instant};
#[derive(Parser)]
#[command(
    name = "nova",
    version,
    about = "Native programmable particle engine and bounded scientific data lake"
)]
struct Cli {
    #[command(subcommand)]
    command: Option<Command>,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, ValueEnum)]
enum Preset {
    CosmicInk,
    ParticleLife,
    ParticleVm,
    LiquidMpm,
    Fusion,
    Hunar,
    Realethersim,
    PrimordialDish,
    ParParticleLife,
    Cellflow,
}
#[derive(Subcommand)]
enum Command {
    /// Unified native ports and generator catalog from four pinned upstream repositories.
    Fuse {
        #[arg(long, default_value = "fusion")]
        source: String,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 8192)]
        particles: usize,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 600)]
        ticks: usize,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 42)]
        seed: u64,
        #[arg(long)]
        headless: bool,
        #[arg(long)]
        gpu: bool,
        #[arg(long)]
        output: Option<PathBuf>,
        #[arg(long)]
        settings: Option<PathBuf>,
        #[arg(long)]
        save_settings: Option<PathBuf>,
        #[arg(long)]
        dish_preset: Option<String>,
        #[arg(long)]
        rules: Option<usize>,
        #[arg(long)]
        positions: Option<usize>,
        #[arg(long)]
        palette: Option<usize>,
        #[arg(long)]
        breed_pick: Option<usize>,
        #[arg(long)]
        list: bool,
    },
    /// Open a native winit/wgpu liquid window. Space pauses, arrows orbit, E emits.
    Live {
        #[arg(long, value_enum, default_value = "cosmic-ink")]
        preset: Preset,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 65536)]
        particles: usize,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 42)]
        seed: u64,
        #[arg(long)]
        enable_crossover: bool,
        #[arg(long)]
        gpu: bool,
        #[arg(long)]
        output: Option<PathBuf>,
    },
    /// Stream trajectories, symbolic candidates, lineages and field tensors to disk.
    Harvest {
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 10000)]
        ticks: usize,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 4096)]
        particles: usize,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 42)]
        seed: u64,
        #[arg(long, default_value = "runs/data_lake")]
        output: PathBuf,
        #[arg(long)]
        record_all: bool,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 10)]
        sample_every: usize,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 24)]
        grid: usize,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 32.)]
        extent: f32,
        #[arg(long, value_enum, default_value = "particle-vm")]
        preset: Preset,
        #[arg(long)]
        enable_crossover: bool,
        #[arg(long)]
        gpu: bool,
    },
    /// Native 2D/3D Conway, rule-space exploration, CPU bitboards or GPU ping-pong.
    Conway {
        #[arg(long, default_value = "2048x2048")]
        grid: String,
        #[arg(long)]
        rule: Option<String>,
        #[arg(long)]
        source: Option<String>,
        #[arg(long)]
        radius: Option<u32>,
        #[arg(long)]
        gpu: bool,
        #[arg(long)]
        solid_boundaries: bool,
        #[arg(long)]
        pattern: Option<String>,
        #[arg(long)]
        classify: bool,
        #[arg(long)]
        output: Option<PathBuf>,
        #[arg(long)]
        input: Option<PathBuf>,
        #[arg(long)]
        search: Option<usize>,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 0)]
        search_start: u64,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 100)]
        ticks: usize,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 42)]
        seed: u64,
        #[arg(long)]
        benchmark: bool,
    },
    /// Open sparse-grid 3D APIC/MLS-MPM liquid with explicit CFL checks.
    Mpm {
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 100)]
        ticks: usize,
        #[arg(long, default_value_t = 512)]
        particles: usize,
        #[arg(long, default_value = "runs/mpm-lake")]
        output: PathBuf,
    },
}
fn universe(count: usize, seed: u64, preset: Preset, crossover: bool) -> Result<Universe> {
    ensure!(
        !crossover || matches!(preset, Preset::ParticleVm),
        "crossover requires --preset particle-vm"
    );
    let fusion = match preset {
        Preset::Cellflow => Some(nova_fusion::Family::CellFlow),
        Preset::Fusion => Some(nova_fusion::Family::Fusion),
        Preset::Hunar => Some(nova_fusion::Family::Hunar),
        Preset::Realethersim => Some(nova_fusion::Family::Realethersim),
        Preset::PrimordialDish => Some(nova_fusion::Family::PrimordialDish),
        Preset::ParParticleLife => Some(nova_fusion::Family::ParParticleLife),
        _ => None,
    };
    let config = Config {
        fusion,
        seed,
        mpm: matches!(preset, Preset::LiquidMpm),
        dt: if matches!(preset, Preset::LiquidMpm) {
            0.001
        } else {
            1. / 120.
        },
        pvm: matches!(preset, Preset::ParticleVm),
        crossover,
        curl: if matches!(preset, Preset::CosmicInk) {
            0.4
        } else {
            0.
        },
        max_particles: count.saturating_mul(4).max(262144).min(10_000_000),
        ..Default::default()
    };
    Universe::new(count, config)
}
fn main() -> Result<()> {
    match Cli::parse().command.unwrap_or(Command::Live {
        preset: Preset::CosmicInk,
        particles: 8192,
        seed: 42,
        enable_crossover: false,
        gpu: false,
        output: None,
    }) {
        Command::Live {
            preset,
            particles,
            seed,
            enable_crossover,
            gpu,
            output,
        } => {
            let u = universe(particles, seed, preset, enable_crossover)?;
            ensure!(
                !gpu || (!u.config.pvm && !u.config.mpm),
                "GPU backend is canonical/cosmic only; PVM/MPM use CPU"
            );
            #[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
            nova_render::run(u, gpu, output)?;
            #[cfg(not(feature = "desktop"))]
            {
                let _ = (u, gpu, output);
                anyhow::bail!("desktop disabled; build with --features desktop");
            }
        }
        Command::Harvest {
            ticks,
            particles,
            seed,
            output,
            record_all,
            sample_every,
            grid,
            extent,
            preset,
            enable_crossover,
            gpu,
        } => {
            ensure!(
                ticks > 0 && ticks <= 1_000_000_000 && sample_every > 0,
                "invalid tick/sample budget"
            );
            let mut u = universe(particles, seed, preset, enable_crossover)?;
            #[cfg(feature = "gpu")]
            let compute = if gpu {
                Some(nova_gpu::Gpu::new()?)
            } else {
                None
            };
            #[cfg(not(feature = "gpu"))]
            ensure!(!gpu, "GPU disabled in this build");
            ensure!(
                !gpu || (!u.config.pvm && !u.config.mpm),
                "GPU backend is canonical/cosmic only; PVM/MPM use CPU"
            );
            let mut lake = Lake::new(&output, &u, grid, extent)?;
            let start = Instant::now();
            let mut recorded = 0;
            for step in 0..=ticks {
                if step % sample_every == 0 || step == ticks {
                    recorded += lake.record(&u, record_all)? as usize;
                }
                if step == ticks {
                    break;
                }
                #[cfg(feature = "gpu")]
                if let Some(gpu) = &compute {
                    gpu.step(&mut u)?;
                } else {
                    u.step(1)?;
                }
                #[cfg(not(feature = "gpu"))]
                u.step(1)?;
            }
            lake.finish()?;
            println!(
                "{}",
                json!({"output":output,"ticks":u.tick,"frames":recorded,"particles":u.particles.len(),"births":u.births,"elapsed_seconds":start.elapsed().as_secs_f64(),"proof_status":"pending","backend":if gpu{"gpu-with-cpu-spatial-index"}else{"cpu"}})
            );
        }

        Command::Conway {
            grid,
            rule,
            ticks,
            seed,
            benchmark,
            source,
            radius,
            gpu,
            solid_boundaries,
            pattern,
            classify,
            output,
            input,
            search,
            search_start,
        } => {
            ensure!(
                ticks > 0 && ticks <= 1_000_000,
                "invalid automaton tick budget"
            );
            let source = source.as_deref().unwrap_or("nova");
            ensure!(
                [
                    "nova",
                    "g3dl",
                    "skeeto",
                    "webgol",
                    "randomgamingdev",
                    "bridiro",
                    "catconway"
                ]
                .contains(&source),
                "unknown Conway source profile"
            );
            let dims: Vec<usize> = grid
                .split('x')
                .map(str::parse)
                .collect::<std::result::Result<_, _>>()?;
            ensure!(
                dims.len() == 2 || dims.len() == 3,
                "expected WIDTHxHEIGHT[xDEPTH]"
            );
            let shape = [dims[0], dims[1], if dims.len() == 3 { dims[2] } else { 1 }];
            ensure!(
                source != "g3dl" || dims.len() == 3,
                "g3dl profile requires an explicit 3D --grid, e.g. 64x64x64"
            );
            let expression = rule.unwrap_or_else(|| {
                if source == "g3dl" {
                    "B5/S4-5".into()
                } else {
                    "B3/S23".into()
                }
            });
            // Preserve the existing SIMD bitboard path for ordinary periodic 2D Conway.
            if source == "nova"
                && dims.len() == 2
                && dims[0] % 64 == 0
                && radius.unwrap_or(1) == 1
                && !gpu
                && !solid_boundaries
                && pattern.is_none()
                && !classify
                && output.is_none()
                && input.is_none()
                && search.is_none()
                && Rule::parse(&expression).is_ok()
            {
                let mut world = Conway::new(dims[0], dims[1], Rule::parse(&expression)?, seed)?;
                let started = Instant::now();
                for _ in 0..ticks {
                    world.step();
                }
                let seconds = started.elapsed().as_secs_f64();
                println!(
                    "{}",
                    json!({"rule":expression,"ticks":ticks,"population":world.population(),"periodic":true,"arithmetic":"SIMD bitboard integer","seconds":seconds,"cell_updates_per_second":if benchmark{Some(dims[0] as f64*dims[1] as f64*ticks as f64/seconds)}else{None}})
                );
                return Ok(());
            }
            if let Some(count) = search {
                ensure!(
                    dims.len() == 2 && !gpu,
                    "rule-space search uses the bounded native 2D CPU evaluator"
                );
                let discoveries = nova_automata::analysis::search(
                    shape,
                    radius.unwrap_or(1),
                    search_start,
                    count,
                    ticks,
                    seed,
                )?;
                if let Some(path) = output {
                    use std::io::Write;
                    let mut file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
                        .write(true)
                        .create_new(true)
                        .open(path)?;
                    for record in discoveries {
                        writeln!(file, "{}", serde_json::to_string(&record)?)?;
                    }
                } else {
                    println!("{}", serde_json::to_string(&discoveries)?);
                }
                return Ok(());
            }
            let rules =
                nova_automata::Rules::parse(&expression, dims.len() as u32, radius.unwrap_or(1))?;
            let boundary = if solid_boundaries {
                nova_automata::Boundary::Dead
            } else {
                nova_automata::Boundary::Periodic
            };
            let mut world = if let Some(path) = input {
                nova_automata::Grid::load(path)?
            } else {
                let mut g = nova_automata::Grid::new(shape, rules, boundary)?;
                if let Some(name) = pattern {
                    g.pattern(&name)?;
                } else {
                    g.randomize(seed, 0.2)?;
                }
                g
            };
            let start = Instant::now();
            let mut observer = if classify {
                Some(nova_automata::analysis::Observer::new(world.cells.len())?)
            } else {
                None
            };
            #[cfg(feature = "gpu")]
            let mut adapter: Option<String> = None;
            #[cfg(not(feature = "gpu"))]
            let adapter: Option<String> = None;
            if gpu {
                #[cfg(feature = "gpu")]
                {
                    let mut engine = nova_gpu::automata::GpuAutomaton::new(&world)?;
                    adapter = Some(engine.adapter().to_owned());
                    if observer.is_none() {
                        engine.step(ticks)?;
                    } else {
                        for _ in 0..ticks {
                            let previous = world.cells.clone();
                            engine.step(1)?;
                            world = engine.read()?;
                            let changed = world
                                .cells
                                .iter()
                                .zip(previous)
                                .filter(|(a, b)| **a != *b)
                                .count() as u64;
                            observer.as_mut().unwrap().record(&world, changed)?;
                        }
                    }
                    world = engine.read()?;
                }
                #[cfg(not(feature = "gpu"))]
                anyhow::bail!("GPU support disabled");
            } else {
                for _ in 0..ticks {
                    let stats = world.step()?;
                    if let Some(o) = &mut observer {
                        o.record(&world, stats.changed)?;
                    }
                }
            }
            let seconds = start.elapsed().as_secs_f64();
            if let Some(path) = output {
                if path.extension().is_some_and(|x| x == "rle") {
                    use std::io::Write;
                    let mut f = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
                        .write(true)
                        .create_new(true)
                        .open(path)?;
                    f.write_all(nova_automata::rle::export(&world)?.as_bytes())?;
                } else {
                    world.save(path)?;
                }
            }
            let metrics = observer
                .as_ref()
                .map(|o| o.finish(world.rules))
                .transpose()?;
            println!(
                "{}",
                json!({"source_profile":source,"shape":world.shape,"rules":world.rules,"tick":world.tick,"population":world.population(),"backend":if gpu{"native-wgpu"}else{"native-rust"},"gpu_adapter":adapter,"seconds":seconds,"metrics":metrics,"classification_scope":"finite numerical heuristics, not a formal complexity proof","cell_updates_per_second":if benchmark{Some(world.cells.len() as f64*ticks as f64/seconds)}else{None}})
            );
        }
        Command::Mpm {
            ticks,
            particles,
            output,
        } => {
            ensure!(
                ticks > 0 && ticks <= 1_000_000 && particles > 0 && particles <= 131072,
                "MPM work budget exceeded"
            );
            let mut u = universe(particles, 42, Preset::ParticleLife, false)?;
            // Compact droplet, with total mass matching its initial volume.
            let mut rng = nova_core::SplitMix64(42);
            for i in 0..particles {
                for k in 0..3 {
                    u.particles.positions[i].0[k] = rng.signed() * 0.5;
                    u.particles.velocities[i].0[k] = 0.;
                }
                u.particles.velocities[i].0[3] = 1. / particles as f32;
            }
            let mut mpm = Mpm::new(particles);
            u.config.dt = mpm.dt;
            u.config.mpm = true;
            let mut lake = Lake::new(&output, &u, 24, 4.)?;
            // Record the actual solver, coefficients and units in addition to generic lake metadata.
            std::fs::write(
                output.join("solver.json"),
                serde_json::to_vec_pretty(
                    &json!({"solver":"open-grid APIC MLS-MPM liquid","dt":mpm.dt,"dx":mpm.dx,"bulk":mpm.bulk,"viscosity":mpm.viscosity,"density":mpm.density,"gravity":mpm.gravity,"pressure":"bulk*(J^-4 - 1)","stress_fields":"kinetic coarse-grained; constitutive grid stress is not exported"}),
                )?,
            )?;
            lake.record(&u, true)?;
            for _ in 0..ticks {
                mpm.step(&mut u.particles)?;
                u.tick += 1;
                if u.tick % 10 == 0 || u.tick == ticks as u64 {
                    lake.record(&u, true)?;
                }
            }
            lake.finish()?;
            println!(
                "{}",
                json!({"output":output,"ticks":ticks,"observables":nova_science::observe(&u.particles)})
            );
        }
        Command::Fuse {
            source,
            particles,
            ticks,
            seed,
            headless,
            gpu,
            output,
            settings,
            save_settings,
            dish_preset,
            rules,
            positions,
            palette,
            breed_pick,
            list,
        } => {
            if list {
                println!("{}", serde_json::to_string_pretty(&nova_fusion::catalog())?);
                return Ok(());
            }
            ensure!(
                ticks > 0 && ticks <= 1_000_000,
                "invalid fusion tick budget"
            );
            let family = nova_fusion::Family::parse(&source)?;
            let mut u = Universe::new(
                particles,
                Config {
                    seed,
                    fusion: Some(family),
                    max_particles: particles.max(262144),
                    ..Default::default()
                },
            )?;
            let model = u.fusion.as_mut().unwrap();
            if let Some(path) = settings {
                ensure!(
                    std::fs::metadata(&path)?.len() <= 1024 * 1024,
                    "preset too large"
                );
                let saved: nova_fusion::SavedPreset =
                    serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(path)?)?;
                saved.validate()?;
                model.settings = saved.settings;
                model.initialize(&mut u.particles, &mut u.matrix, seed)?;
                u.matrix = saved.matrix;
                model.palette = saved.palette;
                u.config.fusion = Some(model.settings.family);
            }
            if let Some(name) = dish_preset {
                model.apply_preset(&mut u.particles, &mut u.matrix, &name)?;
            }
            if rules.is_some() || positions.is_some() || palette.is_some() {
                model.select_generators(
                    &mut u.particles,
                    &mut u.matrix,
                    rules.unwrap_or(0),
                    positions.unwrap_or(0),
                    palette.unwrap_or(0),
                )?;
            }
            if let Some(pick) = breed_pick {
                ensure!(pick < 6, "breed-pick must be 0..5");
                let mate = nova_fusion::breeding::preset("symbiotes")?.matrix8()?;
                let generation = model
                    .rule_history
                    .last()
                    .map(|x| x.generation + 1)
                    .unwrap_or(1);
                let children = nova_fusion::breeding::breed(
                    &u.matrix,
                    &mate,
                    model.settings.species,
                    seed,
                    generation,
                )?;
                u.matrix = children[pick].matrix;
                model.rule_history.push(children[pick].lineage.clone());
            }
            u.config.dt = model.settings.dt;
            if let Some(path) = save_settings {
                std::fs::write(
                    path,
                    serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&nova_fusion::SavedPreset::capture(model, u.matrix))?,
                )?;
            }
            if !headless {
                #[cfg(feature = "desktop")]
                {
                    nova_render::run(u, gpu, output)?;
                    return Ok(());
                }
                #[cfg(not(feature = "desktop"))]
                anyhow::bail!("desktop disabled; use --headless");
            }
            #[cfg(feature = "gpu")]
            let compute = if gpu {
                Some(nova_gpu::Gpu::new()?)
            } else {
                None
            };
            #[cfg(not(feature = "gpu"))]
            ensure!(!gpu, "GPU disabled");
            let extent = u
                .fusion
                .as_ref()
                .unwrap()
                .settings
                .half_extent
                .into_iter()
                .fold(0., f32::max)
                * 1.01;
            let mut lake = output
                .as_ref()
                .map(|path| Lake::new(path, &u, 24, extent))
                .transpose()?;
            let start = Instant::now();
            for _ in 0..ticks {
                #[cfg(feature = "gpu")]
                if let Some(g) = &compute {
                    g.step(&mut u)?;
                } else {
                    u.step(1)?;
                }
                #[cfg(not(feature = "gpu"))]
                u.step(1)?;
                if u.tick % 10 == 0 || u.tick == ticks as u64 {
                    if let Some(lake) = &mut lake {
                        lake.record(&u, true)?;
                    }
                }
            }
            if let Some(lake) = lake {
                lake.finish()?;
            }
            #[cfg(feature = "gpu")]
            let adapter_name = compute.as_ref().map(|c| c.adapter.clone());
            #[cfg(not(feature = "gpu"))]
            let adapter_name: Option<String> = None;
            let elapsed = start.elapsed().as_secs_f64();
            println!(
                "{}",
                serde_json::json!({"source":u.config.fusion,"particles":particles,"ticks":u.tick,"backend":if gpu{"gpu"}else{"rust-rayon"},"gpu_adapter":adapter_name,"elapsed_seconds":elapsed,"particle_updates_per_second":particles as f64*ticks as f64/elapsed,"statistics":u.fusion.as_ref().unwrap().stats,"observables":nova_science::observe(&u.particles),"output":output})
            );
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
